```powershell
git -C rawreel-workspace commit -m "feat: add the Vulkan validation layer and the libraries it is built from" -m "Vulkan-Utility-Libraries 1.4.365, SPIRV-Headers vulkan-sdk-1.4.363.0, SPIRV-Tools 2026.4 and Vulkan-ValidationLayers 1.4.365: the set the layer pins in its known_good.json. The three libraries are compiled and linked into the layer, which the Vulkan loader loads at run time, so they carry the layer's linkage (runtime), not tool. The layer replaces the distribution one (Ubuntu 24.04 ships 1.3.275, which does not understand Vulkan 1.4) for Debug builds and tests and is never shipped. On Linux the post-install step rewrites the manifest library_path relative to the manifest, so VK_ADD_LAYER_PATH=<prefix>/share/vulkan/explicit_layer.d is enough without LD_LIBRARY_PATH; on Windows VK_ADD_LAYER_PATH=<prefix>/bin works as installed. Building the layer needs Python 3." -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

Note: the CI cold-cache time of every build job grows by about 8 to 14 minutes with this task, and the `lint` job of `dvv-ci.yml` runs `bootstrap.sh` without the third-party cache; adding the cache step there (same key as the build jobs) is part of the CI task (Task 23), not this one.

### Task 7: dvv-registry skeleton with component kinds and capabilities

**Files:**
- Create: repository `dvv-registry` (sibling of `dvv-base`, local only: branch `m1a`, no remote) with `.clang-format`, `.clang-tidy`, `.gitattributes`, `.gitignore`, `LICENSE`, `CMakePresets.json` (copied or identical to dvv-base's), `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `README.md`, `CMakeLists.txt`, `cmake/dvv_registryConfig.cmake.in`, `include/dvv/registry/component_info.h`, `include/dvv/registry/capabilities.h`, `src/component_info.cpp`, `src/capabilities.cpp`, `tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/main.cpp`, `tests/test_capabilities.cpp`, `tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/package/main.cpp`, `tests/package/run-package-test.cmake`
- Modify: `rawreel-workspace/repos.txt`, `rawreel-workspace/cmake/dvv-versions.cmake`, `rawreel-workspace/cmake/DvvPresets.json`, `rawreel-workspace/CMakeLists.txt`, `rawreel-workspace/README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (dvv-base): CMake package `dvv_base` 0.1 with `DVV_CMAKE_DIR`, `dvv_set_compiler_options(<target>)`, `DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT`, `DVV_SANITIZE`, option `DVV_BUILD_TESTS`; C++ `dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().setDetailed(bool)`, `dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(int)`; the workspace presets (`vs2026`, `vs2026-asan`, `ninja-clang`, `ninja-clang-asan`, `ninja-clang-tsan`) and `bootstrap.ps1`.
- Produces (CMake): target `dvv_registry` / alias `dvv::registry` (static), package `dvv_registry` 0.1.0 (`SameMinorVersion`, installed to `lib/cmake/dvv_registry`, `find_dependency(dvv_base 0.1)`); tests `dvv_registry.unit` and `dvv_registry.package` (resource lock `dvv_build_tree`); workspace: `dvv-registry` in `repos.txt`, `FetchContent_Declare(dvv_registry ...)`, `FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_REGISTRY` preset variable.
- Produces (C++, namespace `dvv`): `enum class Kind : std::uint8_t { Source, FormatDetector, Exporter, Tool, Metric, Analyser, Processor }`; `std::string_view kindName(Kind) noexcept`; `struct ComponentInfo { std::string_view id; std::string_view name; Kind kind = Kind::Source; int version = 1; }` with defaulted constexpr `operator==`; `struct Unavailable { std::string reason; }`; `class Capabilities` (`Capabilities()`, `Capabilities(std::initializer_list<std::string_view>)`, `void add(std::string_view)`, `bool has(std::string_view) const noexcept`, `const std::vector<std::string>& names() const noexcept`, sorted and unique); `std::expected<void, Unavailable> require(const Capabilities&, std::initializer_list<std::string_view>)` (reason `missing capability '<name>'` for the first missing name).

Note: the repository stays local until a later publishing task. Do not add a remote, create a GitHub repository or push. Because `repos.txt` will list `dvv-registry`, the CI of every other repository clones it, so the workspace commit of Step 14 must not be pushed before `dvv-registry` exists on GitHub (the publishing task does them in that order).

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `dvv-registry/README.md`**

````markdown
# dvv-registry

Component registry of the dvv stack used by rawreel: component descriptors (`ComponentInfo`, `Kind`),
capability probing (`Capabilities`, `require`), parameter schemas with validation, text and JSON
forms, and the self-registering `ComponentRegistry` that creates every component. C++23; depends on
[dvv-base](https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-base).

Build it through [rawreel-workspace](https://github.com/BarabashkaD/rawreel-workspace), which
provides the presets, the third-party dependencies and the CI workflow:

```sh
cmake --workflow --preset vs2026     # Linux: ninja-clang
```

Licence: MIT.
````

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `dvv-registry/CMakeLists.txt`**

The `DVV_BUILD_TESTS` override makes a stand-alone build run only this repository's tests (the normal variable hides the cache entry from dvv-base's `option()` during `FetchContent_MakeAvailable`); inside the workspace build the variable is not top-level, so every repository's tests run.

```cmake
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(dvv_registry VERSION 0.1.0 DESCRIPTION "dvv component registry" LANGUAGES CXX)

option(DVV_BUILD_TESTS "Build the dvv tests" ${PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL})

# dvv-base: inside the workspace build cmake/dvv-versions.cmake has declared it first, so this
# declaration is ignored there (spec §3.1). Stand-alone builds take it from the sibling folder
# (FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_BASE in the workspace presets), from an installed package or from Git.
include(FetchContent)
FetchContent_Declare(dvv_base
    GIT_REPOSITORY "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-base.git"
    GIT_TAG main
    FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1)
# A stand-alone build tests only this repository (the normal variable hides the cache entry from
# dvv_base's option()); the workspace build tests every repository.
if(PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL)
    set(DVV_BUILD_TESTS OFF)
endif()
FetchContent_MakeAvailable(dvv_base)
unset(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
if(NOT DVV_CMAKE_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_base did not define DVV_CMAKE_DIR")
endif()
include("${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvCompilerOptions.cmake")
include(GNUInstallDirs)
include(CMakePackageConfigHelpers)

add_library(dvv_registry STATIC
    src/capabilities.cpp
    src/component_info.cpp)
add_library(dvv::registry ALIAS dvv_registry)
set_target_properties(dvv_registry PROPERTIES EXPORT_NAME registry)
target_include_directories(dvv_registry PUBLIC
    $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>
    $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}>)
target_link_libraries(dvv_registry PUBLIC dvv::base)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_registry)

install(TARGETS dvv_registry EXPORT dvv_registryTargets)
install(DIRECTORY include/ DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR})
set(config_dir ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/dvv_registry)
install(EXPORT dvv_registryTargets NAMESPACE dvv:: DESTINATION ${config_dir})
configure_package_config_file(cmake/dvv_registryConfig.cmake.in
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_registryConfig.cmake INSTALL_DESTINATION ${config_dir})
write_basic_package_version_file(${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_registryConfigVersion.cmake
    COMPATIBILITY SameMinorVersion)
install(FILES
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_registryConfig.cmake
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_registryConfigVersion.cmake
    DESTINATION ${config_dir})

if(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
    enable_testing()
    add_subdirectory(tests)
endif()
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the failing tests**

`dvv-registry/tests/main.cpp`:

```cpp
#define DOCTEST_CONFIG_IMPLEMENT
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include "dvv/base/leak_check.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().setDetailed(true);
    doctest::Context context(argc, argv);
    const int result = context.run();
    if (context.shouldExit()) {
        return result;
    }
    return dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(result);
}
```

`dvv-registry/tests/test_capabilities.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/registry/capabilities.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_info.h"

TEST_CASE("capabilities hold names") {
    dvv::Capabilities caps;
    CHECK_FALSE(caps.has("gpu.compute"));
    caps.add("gpu.compute");
    caps.add("gpu.compute");
    caps.add("gpu.float16");
    CHECK(caps.has("gpu.compute"));
    CHECK(caps.has("gpu.float16"));
    CHECK_FALSE(caps.has("gpu"));
    CHECK(caps.names() == std::vector<std::string>{"gpu.compute", "gpu.float16"});
}

TEST_CASE("capabilities from a list") {
    const dvv::Capabilities caps{"ffmpeg", "gpu.compute"};
    CHECK(caps.has("ffmpeg"));
    CHECK(caps.has("gpu.compute"));
    CHECK(caps.names().size() == 2);
}

TEST_CASE("require names the first missing capability") {
    const dvv::Capabilities caps{"gpu.compute"};
    CHECK(dvv::require(caps, {}).has_value());
    CHECK(dvv::require(caps, {"gpu.compute"}).has_value());

    const auto missing = dvv::require(caps, {"gpu.compute", "gpu.float16", "ffmpeg"});
    REQUIRE_FALSE(missing.has_value());
    CHECK(missing.error().reason == "missing capability 'gpu.float16'");
}

TEST_CASE("component kinds have names") {
    CHECK(dvv::kindName(dvv::Kind::Source) == "Source");
    CHECK(dvv::kindName(dvv::Kind::FormatDetector) == "FormatDetector");
    CHECK(dvv::kindName(dvv::Kind::Exporter) == "Exporter");
    CHECK(dvv::kindName(dvv::Kind::Tool) == "Tool");
    CHECK(dvv::kindName(dvv::Kind::Metric) == "Metric");
    CHECK(dvv::kindName(dvv::Kind::Analyser) == "Analyser");
    CHECK(dvv::kindName(dvv::Kind::Processor) == "Processor");
}

TEST_CASE("component info is a comparable value") {
    constexpr dvv::ComponentInfo PSNR{
        .id = "psnr", .name = "PSNR", .kind = dvv::Kind::Metric, .version = 1};
    static_assert(PSNR.id == "psnr");
    dvv::ComponentInfo copy = PSNR;
    CHECK(copy == PSNR);
    copy.version = 2;
    CHECK(copy != PSNR);
}
```

```cmake
# Package test (spec §3.1): install, then build a consumer with find_package(dvv_registry) and

    add_test(NAME dvv_registry.package
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}

                     -DPREFIX=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/package-prefix
                     -DSOURCE_DIR=${dvv_registry_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DBASE_SOURCE_DIR=${dvv_base_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DCONSUMER_SOURCE=${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/package
                     -DCONSUMER_BUILD=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/package-consumer
                     -DGENERATOR=${CMAKE_GENERATOR}
                     -DPLATFORM=${CMAKE_GENERATOR_PLATFORM}
                     -DCXX_COMPILER=${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}
                     -DDEPS_PREFIX_PATH=${deps_prefix_path}
                     -P ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/package/run-package-test.cmake)
    # Package and compile-fail tests of every dvv repository drive the same build tree in the
    # workspace build, so they share one lock name.
    set_tests_properties(dvv_registry.package PROPERTIES RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree)
endif()
```

`dvv-registry/tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`:

```cmake
# Consumer of dvv_registry; built by run-package-test.cmake. With DVV_REGISTRY_SOURCE_DIR it takes
# dvv_registry in-tree through FetchContent (as the workspace build does), otherwise the installed
# package.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(dvv_registry_package_consumer LANGUAGES CXX)

if(DVV_REGISTRY_SOURCE_DIR)
    include(FetchContent)
    FetchContent_Declare(dvv_registry SOURCE_DIR "${DVV_REGISTRY_SOURCE_DIR}")
    FetchContent_MakeAvailable(dvv_registry)
else()
    find_package(dvv_registry 0.1 REQUIRED)
endif()
if(NOT DVV_CMAKE_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_base did not define DVV_CMAKE_DIR")
endif()
include("${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvCompilerOptions.cmake")

add_executable(consumer main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(consumer PRIVATE dvv::registry)
dvv_set_compiler_options(consumer)
```

```cpp
#include "dvv/registry/capabilities.h"

int main() {
    const dvv::Capabilities caps{"gpu.compute"};
    if (!dvv::require(caps, {"gpu.compute"}) || dvv::require(caps, {"ffmpeg"})) {
        return 1;
    }
    std::puts("dvv_registry package works");

}
```

`dvv-registry/tests/package/run-package-test.cmake`:

```cmake
# Installs the build tree into a scratch prefix, then configures, builds and runs the consumer twice:
# against the installed package and with dvv_registry in-tree (FetchContent of the source directory,
# dvv_base from its source directory too).
# Called by the dvv_registry.package test with -DBUILD_DIR, -DCONFIG, -DPREFIX, -DSOURCE_DIR,
# -DBASE_SOURCE_DIR, -DCONSUMER_SOURCE, -DCONSUMER_BUILD, -DGENERATOR, -DPLATFORM, -DCXX_COMPILER and
# -DDEPS_PREFIX_PATH.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

function(run)
    execute_process(COMMAND ${ARGV} RESULT_VARIABLE status)
    if(NOT status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "failed (${status}): ${ARGV}")
    endif()
endfunction()

# consumer(<build dir> <prefix path> [<extra configure arguments>...])
function(consumer build_dir prefix_path)
    # A list value cannot pass through a command line intact; an initial-cache file keeps it whole.
    set(initial_cache "${build_dir}-cache.cmake")
    file(WRITE "${initial_cache}" "set(CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH \"${prefix_path}\" CACHE STRING \"\")\n")
    set(configure "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -C "${initial_cache}" -S "${CONSUMER_SOURCE}" -B "${build_dir}"
        -G "${GENERATOR}" "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER=${CXX_COMPILER}" "-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=${CONFIG}"
        ${ARGN})
    if(PLATFORM)
        list(APPEND configure -A "${PLATFORM}")
    endif()
    run(${configure})
    run("${CMAKE_COMMAND}" --build "${build_dir}" --config "${CONFIG}")

    file(GLOB_RECURSE executable "${build_dir}/consumer" "${build_dir}/consumer.exe")
    list(FILTER executable EXCLUDE REGEX "CMakeFiles")
    if(NOT executable)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "consumer executable not found in ${build_dir}")
    endif()
    list(GET executable 0 executable)
    run("${executable}")
endfunction()

string(REPLACE "," ";" deps_prefix_path "${DEPS_PREFIX_PATH}")
file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${PREFIX}" "${CONSUMER_BUILD}" "${CONSUMER_BUILD}-in-tree")
run("${CMAKE_COMMAND}" --install "${BUILD_DIR}" --config "${CONFIG}" --prefix "${PREFIX}")
consumer("${CONSUMER_BUILD}" "${PREFIX};${deps_prefix_path}")
consumer("${CONSUMER_BUILD}-in-tree" "${deps_prefix_path}"
         "-DDVV_REGISTRY_SOURCE_DIR=${SOURCE_DIR}"
         "-DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_BASE=${BASE_SOURCE_DIR}"
         -DFETCHCONTENT_TRY_FIND_PACKAGE_MODE=NEVER)
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement**

`dvv-registry/include/dvv/registry/component_info.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Component descriptors (spec §9.2, §9.3).

#include <cstdint>
#include <string_view>

namespace dvv {

enum class Kind : std::uint8_t {
    Source,
    FormatDetector,
    Exporter,
    Tool,
    Metric,
    Analyser,
    Processor
};

[[nodiscard]] std::string_view kindName(Kind kind) noexcept;

// What a component reports about itself. id and name refer to static storage (string literals).
// The id is unique among all components and is what session files and the CLI store.
struct ComponentInfo {
    std::string_view id;
    std::string_view name;
    Kind kind = Kind::Source;
    int version = 1;

    friend constexpr bool operator==(const ComponentInfo&, const ComponentInfo&) = default;
};

}  // namespace dvv
```

`dvv-registry/src/component_info.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/registry/component_info.h"

namespace dvv {

std::string_view kindName(Kind kind) noexcept {
    switch (kind) {
        case Kind::Source: return "Source";
        case Kind::FormatDetector: return "FormatDetector";
        case Kind::Exporter: return "Exporter";
        case Kind::Tool: return "Tool";
        case Kind::Metric: return "Metric";
        case Kind::Analyser: return "Analyser";
        case Kind::Processor: return "Processor";
    }
    return "Unknown";
}

}  // namespace dvv
```

`dvv-registry/include/dvv/registry/capabilities.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Capability probing (spec §9.2): the environment reports named capabilities ("gpu.compute",
// "ffmpeg", ...) and each component's probe() decides from them whether it can run.

#include <expected>
#include <initializer_list>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace dvv {

// Why a component cannot run here; shown next to the greyed-out component.
struct Unavailable {
    std::string reason;
};

// A set of capability names.
class Capabilities {
public:
    Capabilities() = default;
    Capabilities(std::initializer_list<std::string_view> names);

    void add(std::string_view name);
    [[nodiscard]] bool has(std::string_view name) const noexcept;
    // Sorted, without duplicates.
    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<std::string>& names() const noexcept { return names_; }

private:
    std::vector<std::string> names_;
};

// Succeeds when caps has every name; otherwise names the first missing one.
[[nodiscard]] std::expected<void, Unavailable> require(
    const Capabilities& caps, std::initializer_list<std::string_view> names);

}  // namespace dvv
```

`dvv-registry/src/capabilities.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/registry/capabilities.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <format>
#include <functional>

namespace dvv {

Capabilities::Capabilities(std::initializer_list<std::string_view> names) {
    for (const std::string_view name : names) {
        add(name);
    }
}

void Capabilities::add(std::string_view name) {
    const auto position = std::ranges::lower_bound(names_, name, std::less<>{});
    if (position == names_.end() || *position != name) {
        names_.emplace(position, name);
    }
}

bool Capabilities::has(std::string_view name) const noexcept {
    return std::ranges::binary_search(names_, name, std::less<>{});
}

std::expected<void, Unavailable> require(const Capabilities& caps,
                                         std::initializer_list<std::string_view> names) {
    for (const std::string_view name : names) {
        if (!caps.has(name)) {
            return std::unexpected(Unavailable{std::format("missing capability '{}'", name)});
        }
    }
    return {};
}

}  // namespace dvv
```

### Task 8: ParameterSchema with defaults and validation

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-registry/include/dvv/registry/parameters.h`, `dvv-registry/src/parameters.cpp`, `dvv-registry/tests/test_parameters.cpp`
- Modify: `dvv-registry/CMakeLists.txt` (add `src/parameters.cpp`), `dvv-registry/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (add `test_parameters.cpp`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `dvv::Result<T>`, `dvv::makeError(Errc, std::string)`, `dvv::Errc` (dvv-base `error.h`).
- Produces (namespace `dvv`; Task 9 adds `parse`, `format`, `toJson`, `fromJson` to `ParameterSchema`): `struct Range { double low = 0; double high = 0; }` and `struct Colour { float r = 0, g = 0, b = 0, a = 1; }` (both with defaulted `==`); `using ParameterValue = std::variant<std::int64_t, double, bool, std::string, Range, Colour>` (an enum value is the option's name); `using ParameterValues = std::map<std::string, ParameterValue, std::less<>>`; `enum class ParameterType : std::uint8_t { Int, Float, Bool, Enum, Range, Colour }` (same order as the variant); `std::string_view parameterTypeName(ParameterType) noexcept`; `ParameterType parameterTypeOf(const ParameterValue&) noexcept`; specs `IntParameter{min, max, defaultValue}`, `FloatParameter{min, max, defaultValue}`, `BoolParameter{defaultValue}`, `EnumParameter{options, defaultValue}`, `RangeParameter{min = 0, max = 1, defaultValue}`, `ColourParameter{defaultValue}`; `struct Parameter { std::string name; std::string label; std::variant<...specs> spec; ParameterType type() const noexcept; ParameterValue defaultValue() const; }`; `class ParameterSchema` (`parameters()`, `find(name)`, `defaults()`, `Result<void> validate(const ParameterValues&) const`, `Result<ParameterValues> resolve(const ParameterValues&) const`); `ParameterSchema::Builder` (`addInt/addFloat/addBool/addEnum/addRange/addColour(name, label, spec)`, `Result<ParameterSchema> build() const`).
- Decision (design left it open): `build()` returns `Errc::Internal` naming the first invalid declaration (bad or duplicate name, `min` above `max`, non-finite float limits, empty, duplicate or missing enum options, a default that does not fit). A component writes `Builder().addInt(...).build().value()`; a broken schema then throws `std::bad_expected_access<Error>` from its `schema()`, which Task 10's registry turns into an unavailable component. Value errors from `validate()` are `Errc::Format`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`dvv-registry/tests/test_parameters.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <limits>
#include <string>
#include <variant>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/registry/parameters.h"

namespace {

dvv::ParameterSchema sampleSchema() {
    return dvv::ParameterSchema::Builder()
        .addInt("radius", "Radius", {.min = 1, .max = 10, .defaultValue = 3})
        .addFloat("gain", "Gain", {.min = 0.0, .max = 4.0, .defaultValue = 1.0})
        .addBool("invert", "Invert", {.defaultValue = false})
        .addEnum("mode", "Mode", {.options = {"linear", "log"}, .defaultValue = "linear"})
        .addRange("levels", "Levels",
                  {.min = 0.0, .max = 1.0, .defaultValue = {.low = 0.1, .high = 0.9}})
        .addColour("tint", "Tint", {.defaultValue = {.r = 1.0F, .g = 0.5F, .b = 0.0F, .a = 1.0F}})
        .build()
        .value();
}

// Builds a one-parameter schema and returns the build error message, or "" when it builds.
template <class Add>
std::string buildError(Add add) {
    dvv::ParameterSchema::Builder builder;
    add(builder);
    const auto schema = builder.build();
    if (schema.has_value()) {
        return "";
    }
    CHECK(schema.error().code == dvv::Errc::Internal);
    return schema.error().message;
}

std::string validateError(const dvv::ParameterSchema& schema, const dvv::ParameterValues& values) {
    const auto result = schema.validate(values);
    if (result.has_value()) {
        return "";
    }
    CHECK(result.error().code == dvv::Errc::Format);
    return result.error().message;
}

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("a schema keeps its parameters in order") {
    const auto schema = sampleSchema();
    REQUIRE(schema.parameters().size() == 6);
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[0].name == "radius");
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[0].label == "Radius");
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[0].type() == dvv::ParameterType::Int);
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[1].type() == dvv::ParameterType::Float);
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[2].type() == dvv::ParameterType::Bool);
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[3].type() == dvv::ParameterType::Enum);
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[4].type() == dvv::ParameterType::Range);
    CHECK(schema.parameters()[5].type() == dvv::ParameterType::Colour);

    const dvv::Parameter* mode = schema.find("mode");
    REQUIRE(mode != nullptr);
    const auto& spec = std::get<dvv::EnumParameter>(mode->spec);
    CHECK(spec.options == std::vector<std::string>{"linear", "log"});
    CHECK(schema.find("missing") == nullptr);
}

TEST_CASE("defaults hold every parameter") {
    const auto defaults = sampleSchema().defaults();
    CHECK(defaults.size() == 6);
    CHECK(defaults.at("radius") == dvv::ParameterValue{std::int64_t{3}});
    CHECK(defaults.at("gain") == dvv::ParameterValue{1.0});
    CHECK(defaults.at("invert") == dvv::ParameterValue{false});
    CHECK(defaults.at("mode") == dvv::ParameterValue{std::string("linear")});
    CHECK(defaults.at("levels") == dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Range{.low = 0.1, .high = 0.9}});
    CHECK(defaults.at("tint") ==
          dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Colour{.r = 1.0F, .g = 0.5F, .b = 0.0F, .a = 1.0F}});
    CHECK(dvv::ParameterSchema().defaults().empty());
}

TEST_CASE("parameter types have names") {
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeName(dvv::ParameterType::Int) == "int");
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeName(dvv::ParameterType::Float) == "float");
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeName(dvv::ParameterType::Bool) == "bool");
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeName(dvv::ParameterType::Enum) == "enum");
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeName(dvv::ParameterType::Range) == "range");
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeName(dvv::ParameterType::Colour) == "colour");
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeOf(dvv::ParameterValue{std::int64_t{1}}) == dvv::ParameterType::Int);
    CHECK(dvv::parameterTypeOf(dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Colour{}}) == dvv::ParameterType::Colour);
}

TEST_CASE("duplicate names are rejected when the schema is built") {
    const auto error = buildError([](auto& builder) {
        builder.addBool("invert", "Invert", {}).addInt("invert", "Again", {});
    });
    CHECK(error == "parameter schema: duplicate parameter 'invert'");
}

TEST_CASE("invalid parameter declarations are rejected when the schema is built") {
    using Builder = dvv::ParameterSchema::Builder;
    constexpr double NAN_VALUE = std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN();
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) { b.addBool("", "Empty", {}); }) ==
          "parameter schema: invalid parameter name ''");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) { b.addBool("a=b", "Equals", {}); }) ==
          "parameter schema: invalid parameter name 'a=b'");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addInt("n", "N", {.min = 5, .max = 1, .defaultValue = 5});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'n': min 5 is above max 1");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addInt("n", "N", {.min = 1, .max = 5, .defaultValue = 0});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'n': 0 is outside [1, 5]");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addFloat("f", "F", {.min = NAN_VALUE, .max = 1.0, .defaultValue = 0.0});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'f': min and max must be finite");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addFloat("f", "F", {.min = 0.0, .max = 1.0, .defaultValue = 2.0});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'f': 2 is outside [0, 1]");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addEnum("e", "E", {.options = {}, .defaultValue = ""});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'e': an enum needs at least one option");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addEnum("e", "E", {.options = {"a", ""}, .defaultValue = "a"});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'e': empty enum option");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addEnum("e", "E", {.options = {"a", "a"}, .defaultValue = "a"});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'e': duplicate enum option 'a'");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addEnum("e", "E", {.options = {"a", "b"}, .defaultValue = "c"});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'e': 'c' is not one of: a, b");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) {
              b.addRange("r", "R",
                         {.min = 0.0, .max = 1.0, .defaultValue = {.low = 0.8, .high = 0.2}});
          }) == "parameter schema: parameter 'r': low 0.8 is above high 0.2");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) { b.addColour("c", "C", {.defaultValue = {.r = 2.0F}}); }) ==
          "parameter schema: parameter 'c': colour components must be in [0, 1]");
    CHECK(buildError([](Builder& b) { b.addInt("n", "N", {}).addFloat("f", "F", {}); }).empty());
}

TEST_CASE("validate accepts partial values that fit the schema") {
    const auto schema = sampleSchema();
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {}).empty());
    CHECK(validateError(schema, schema.defaults()).empty());
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"radius", std::int64_t{10}}, {"mode", std::string("log")}})
              .empty());
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"levels", dvv::Range{.low = 0.0, .high = 0.0}}}).empty());
}

TEST_CASE("validate names the first problem") {
    const auto schema = sampleSchema();
    constexpr double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"size", std::int64_t{1}}}) == "unknown parameter 'size'");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"gain", std::int64_t{1}}}) ==
          "parameter 'gain': expected float, got int");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"mode", true}}) == "parameter 'mode': expected enum, got bool");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"radius", std::int64_t{11}}}) ==
          "parameter 'radius': 11 is outside [1, 10]");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"gain", -0.5}}) == "parameter 'gain': -0.5 is outside [0, 4]");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"gain", INF}}) == "parameter 'gain': not a finite number");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"mode", std::string("cubic")}}) ==
          "parameter 'mode': 'cubic' is not one of: linear, log");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"levels", dvv::Range{.low = 0.5, .high = 0.4}}}) ==
          "parameter 'levels': low 0.5 is above high 0.4");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"levels", dvv::Range{.low = -1.0, .high = 0.4}}}) ==
          "parameter 'levels': -1 is outside [0, 1]");
    CHECK(validateError(schema, {{"tint", dvv::Colour{.r = std::nanf("")}}}) ==
          "parameter 'tint': colour components must be in [0, 1]");
}

TEST_CASE("resolve fills in the defaults") {
    const auto schema = sampleSchema();
    const auto values = schema.resolve({{"radius", std::int64_t{7}}});
    REQUIRE(values.has_value());
    CHECK(values->size() == 6);
    CHECK(values->at("radius") == dvv::ParameterValue{std::int64_t{7}});
    CHECK(values->at("gain") == dvv::ParameterValue{1.0});

    const auto bad = schema.resolve({{"radius", std::int64_t{70}}});
    REQUIRE_FALSE(bad.has_value());
    CHECK(bad.error().code == dvv::Errc::Format);
}
```

`dvv-registry/include/dvv/registry/parameters.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Component parameters (spec §9.2): a ParameterSchema describes them for the GUI (property grid),
// the CLI (--param name=value) and session files; ParameterValues carries the chosen values.

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <limits>
#include <map>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <variant>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"

namespace dvv {

// A low/high pair, e.g. black and white levels.
struct Range {
    double low = 0.0;
    double high = 0.0;

    friend bool operator==(const Range&, const Range&) = default;
};

// RGBA, each component in [0, 1].
struct Colour {
    float r = 0.0F;
    float g = 0.0F;
    float b = 0.0F;
    float a = 1.0F;

    friend bool operator==(const Colour&, const Colour&) = default;
};

// One value; an enum value is the option's name.
using ParameterValue = std::variant<std::int64_t, double, bool, std::string, Range, Colour>;

// Parameter name -> value, sorted by name.
using ParameterValues = std::map<std::string, ParameterValue, std::less<>>;

// Same order as the alternatives of ParameterValue and Parameter::spec.
enum class ParameterType : std::uint8_t { Int, Float, Bool, Enum, Range, Colour };

[[nodiscard]] std::string_view parameterTypeName(ParameterType type) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] ParameterType parameterTypeOf(const ParameterValue& value) noexcept;

struct IntParameter {
    std::int64_t min = std::numeric_limits<std::int64_t>::min();
    std::int64_t max = std::numeric_limits<std::int64_t>::max();
    std::int64_t defaultValue = 0;
};

// min, max and every value are finite.
struct FloatParameter {
    double min = std::numeric_limits<double>::lowest();
    double max = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    double defaultValue = 0.0;
};

struct BoolParameter {
    bool defaultValue = false;
};

// Options are non-empty and unique; the default is one of them.
struct EnumParameter {
    std::vector<std::string> options;
    std::string defaultValue;
};

// Both ends lie in [min, max] and low <= high.
struct RangeParameter {
    double min = 0.0;
    double max = 1.0;
    Range defaultValue{.low = 0.0, .high = 1.0};
};

struct ColourParameter {
    Colour defaultValue;
};

struct Parameter {
    std::string name;   // key in ParameterValues, the CLI and session files: [A-Za-z0-9_.-]+
    std::string label;  // for people
    std::variant<IntParameter, FloatParameter, BoolParameter, EnumParameter, RangeParameter,
                 ColourParameter>
        spec;

    [[nodiscard]] ParameterType type() const noexcept {
        return static_cast<ParameterType>(spec.index());
    }
    [[nodiscard]] ParameterValue defaultValue() const;
};

// Immutable description of a component's parameters, built with ParameterSchema::Builder.
class ParameterSchema {
public:
    class Builder;

    // No parameters.
    ParameterSchema() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const Parameter> parameters() const noexcept { return parameters_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const Parameter* find(std::string_view name) const noexcept;

    [[nodiscard]] ParameterValues defaults() const;

    // Errc::Format naming the first unknown name, wrong type, value out of range or enum value
    // that is not an option. Missing names are allowed.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<void> validate(const ParameterValues& values) const;

    // validate(), then the defaults for every missing name.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<ParameterValues> resolve(const ParameterValues& values) const;

private:
    explicit ParameterSchema(std::vector<Parameter> parameters) noexcept
        : parameters_(std::move(parameters)) {}

    std::vector<Parameter> parameters_;
};

// Collects parameter declarations; build() checks them. Invalid declarations (bad or duplicate
// names, min above max, a default that does not fit) are programming errors: build() returns
// Errc::Internal naming the first one.
class ParameterSchema::Builder {
public:
    Builder& addInt(std::string name, std::string label, IntParameter spec);
    Builder& addFloat(std::string name, std::string label, FloatParameter spec);
    Builder& addBool(std::string name, std::string label, BoolParameter spec);
    Builder& addEnum(std::string name, std::string label, EnumParameter spec);
    Builder& addRange(std::string name, std::string label, RangeParameter spec);
    Builder& addColour(std::string name, std::string label, ColourParameter spec);

    [[nodiscard]] Result<ParameterSchema> build() const;

private:
    std::vector<Parameter> parameters_;
};

}  // namespace dvv
```

`dvv-registry/src/parameters.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/registry/parameters.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <format>
#include <iterator>
#include <type_traits>

namespace dvv {

namespace {

std::unexpected<Error> invalid(const Parameter& parameter, std::string_view problem) {
    return makeError(Errc::Format, std::format("parameter '{}': {}", parameter.name, problem));
}

bool isValidName(std::string_view name) noexcept {
    return !name.empty() && std::ranges::all_of(name, [](char c) {
        return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') ||
               c == '_' || c == '.' || c == '-';
    });
}

std::string joinOptions(const std::vector<std::string>& options) {
    std::string text;
    for (const auto& option : options) {
        if (!text.empty()) {
            text += ", ";
        }
        text += option;
    }
    return text;
}

template <class T>
Result<void> checkBounds(const Parameter& parameter, T number, T min, T max) {
    if (number < min || number > max) {
        return invalid(parameter, std::format("{} is outside [{}, {}]", number, min, max));
    }
    return {};
}

template <class T>
Result<void> checkLimits(const Parameter& parameter, T min, T max) {
    if constexpr (std::is_floating_point_v<T>) {
        if (!std::isfinite(min) || !std::isfinite(max)) {
            return invalid(parameter, "min and max must be finite");
        }
    }
    if (min > max) {
        return invalid(parameter, std::format("min {} is above max {}", min, max));
    }
    return {};
}

bool isUnitInterval(float component) noexcept {
    return component >= 0.0F && component <= 1.0F;  // false for NaN
}

// Checks a value of any type against one parameter.
Result<void> checkValue(const Parameter& parameter, const ParameterValue& value) {
    const ParameterType type = parameterTypeOf(value);
    if (type != parameter.type()) {
        return invalid(parameter,
                       std::format("expected {}, got {}", parameterTypeName(parameter.type()),
                                   parameterTypeName(type)));
    }
    switch (type) {
        case ParameterType::Int: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<IntParameter>(parameter.spec);
            return checkBounds(parameter, std::get<std::int64_t>(value), spec.min, spec.max);
        }
        case ParameterType::Float: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<FloatParameter>(parameter.spec);
            const double number = std::get<double>(value);
            if (!std::isfinite(number)) {
                return invalid(parameter, "not a finite number");
            }
            return checkBounds(parameter, number, spec.min, spec.max);
        }
        case ParameterType::Bool: return {};
        case ParameterType::Enum: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<EnumParameter>(parameter.spec);
            const auto& text = std::get<std::string>(value);
            if (std::ranges::find(spec.options, text) == spec.options.end()) {
                return invalid(parameter, std::format("'{}' is not one of: {}", text,
                                                      joinOptions(spec.options)));
            }
            return {};
        }
        case ParameterType::Range: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<RangeParameter>(parameter.spec);
            const auto& range = std::get<Range>(value);
            if (!std::isfinite(range.low) || !std::isfinite(range.high)) {
                return invalid(parameter, "not a finite number");
            }
            if (range.low > range.high) {
                return invalid(parameter,
                               std::format("low {} is above high {}", range.low, range.high));
            }
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkBounds(parameter, range.low, spec.min, spec.max));
            return checkBounds(parameter, range.high, spec.min, spec.max);
        }
        case ParameterType::Colour: {
            const auto& colour = std::get<Colour>(value);
            if (!isUnitInterval(colour.r) || !isUnitInterval(colour.g) ||
                !isUnitInterval(colour.b) || !isUnitInterval(colour.a)) {
                return invalid(parameter, "colour components must be in [0, 1]");
            }
            return {};
        }
    }
    return invalid(parameter, "unknown parameter type");
}

// Checks a declaration: its limits or options, then its default.
Result<void> checkDeclaration(const Parameter& parameter) {
    switch (parameter.type()) {
        case ParameterType::Int: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<IntParameter>(parameter.spec);
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkLimits(parameter, spec.min, spec.max));
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Float: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<FloatParameter>(parameter.spec);
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkLimits(parameter, spec.min, spec.max));
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Range: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<RangeParameter>(parameter.spec);
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkLimits(parameter, spec.min, spec.max));
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Enum: {
            const auto& options = std::get<EnumParameter>(parameter.spec).options;
            if (options.empty()) {
                return invalid(parameter, "an enum needs at least one option");
            }
            for (auto option = options.begin(); option != options.end(); ++option) {
                if (option->empty()) {
                    return invalid(parameter, "empty enum option");
                }
                if (std::find(std::next(option), options.end(), *option) != options.end()) {
                    return invalid(parameter, std::format("duplicate enum option '{}'", *option));
                }
            }
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Bool:
        case ParameterType::Colour: break;
    }
    return checkValue(parameter, parameter.defaultValue());
}

}  // namespace

std::string_view parameterTypeName(ParameterType type) noexcept {
    switch (type) {
        case ParameterType::Int: return "int";
        case ParameterType::Float: return "float";
        case ParameterType::Bool: return "bool";
        case ParameterType::Enum: return "enum";
        case ParameterType::Range: return "range";
        case ParameterType::Colour: return "colour";
    }
    return "unknown";
}

ParameterType parameterTypeOf(const ParameterValue& value) noexcept {
    return static_cast<ParameterType>(value.index());
}

ParameterValue Parameter::defaultValue() const {
    return std::visit([](const auto& typed) { return ParameterValue{typed.defaultValue}; }, spec);
}

const Parameter* ParameterSchema::find(std::string_view name) const noexcept {
    const auto found = std::ranges::find(parameters_, name, &Parameter::name);
    return found != parameters_.end() ? &*found : nullptr;
}

ParameterValues ParameterSchema::defaults() const {
    ParameterValues values;
    for (const auto& parameter : parameters_) {
        values.emplace(parameter.name, parameter.defaultValue());
    }
    return values;
}

Result<void> ParameterSchema::validate(const ParameterValues& values) const {
    for (const auto& [name, value] : values) {
        const Parameter* parameter = find(name);
        if (parameter == nullptr) {
            return makeError(Errc::Format, std::format("unknown parameter '{}'", name));
        }
        DVV_TRY_VOID(checkValue(*parameter, value));
    }
    return {};
}

Result<ParameterValues> ParameterSchema::resolve(const ParameterValues& values) const {
    DVV_TRY_VOID(validate(values));
    ParameterValues resolved = defaults();
    for (const auto& [name, value] : values) {
        resolved.insert_or_assign(name, value);
    }
    return resolved;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addInt(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                           IntParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addFloat(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                             FloatParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addBool(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                            BoolParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addEnum(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                            EnumParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), std::move(spec)});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addRange(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                             RangeParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addColour(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                              ColourParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

Result<ParameterSchema> ParameterSchema::Builder::build() const {
    const auto fail = [](std::string problem) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal, "parameter schema: " + std::move(problem));
    };
    for (auto parameter = parameters_.begin(); parameter != parameters_.end(); ++parameter) {
        if (!isValidName(parameter->name)) {
            return fail(std::format("invalid parameter name '{}'", parameter->name));
        }
        if (std::find_if(parameters_.begin(), parameter, [&](const Parameter& earlier) {
                return earlier.name == parameter->name;
            }) != parameter) {
            return fail(std::format("duplicate parameter '{}'", parameter->name));
        }
        if (auto checked = checkDeclaration(*parameter); !checked) {
            return fail(std::move(checked.error().message));
        }
    }
    return ParameterSchema(parameters_);
}

}  // namespace dvv
```

```powershell

@'
feat: add ParameterSchema with defaults and validation

Parameters of type int, float, bool, enum, range and colour (spec §9.2), each with a name, a label, limits or options and a default. ParameterValue is a std::variant in the same order as ParameterType; ParameterValues maps names to values. ParameterSchema::Builder checks every declaration when the schema is built and returns Errc::Internal for the first invalid one (bad or duplicate name, min above max, a default that does not fit). defaults(), validate() (Errc::Format naming the first unknown name, wrong type, value out of range or unlisted enum value) and resolve() (validate, then fill in the defaults).
```

### Task 9: Parameter text and JSON forms

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-registry/tests/test_parameter_io.cpp`
- Modify: `dvv-registry/include/dvv/registry/parameters.h`, `dvv-registry/src/parameters.cpp`, `dvv-registry/tests/CMakeLists.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 8's `ParameterSchema`, `ParameterValue(s)`; `dvv::json::Value` (dvv-base `json.h`).
- Produces (members of `dvv::ParameterSchema`): `Result<ParameterValue> parse(std::string_view name, std::string_view text) const` (CLI `--param name=value`: int decimal, float decimal or exponent, bool `true|false|1|0`, enum option name, range `low,high`, colour `r,g,b` or `r,g,b,a`; spaces around fields allowed; the result is validated; `Errc::Format` otherwise); `static std::string format(const ParameterValue&)` (shortest round-trip text, `1.0` is written `1`, range `0.25,0.5`, colour `0.1,0.2,0.3,1`); `static json::Value toJson(const ParameterValues&)` (range `[low, high]`, colour `[r, g, b, a]`, float components written as the double of their shortest decimal form so JSON shows `0.1`); `Result<ParameterValues> fromJson(const json::Value&) const` (integers accepted for floats, RGB colours accepted, `Errc::Format` for a non-object, unknown name, wrong JSON type, value that fails `validate()`).
- `format` and `toJson` are static members (they do not need the schema); a free `dvv::format` is avoided because ADL on `std::variant` would also find `std::format`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`dvv-registry/tests/test_parameter_io.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/json.h"
#include "dvv/registry/parameters.h"

namespace {

dvv::ParameterSchema sampleSchema() {
    return dvv::ParameterSchema::Builder()
        .addInt("radius", "Radius", {.min = -5, .max = 10, .defaultValue = 3})
        .addFloat("gain", "Gain", {.min = 0.0, .max = 4.0, .defaultValue = 1.0})
        .addBool("invert", "Invert", {.defaultValue = false})
        .addEnum("mode", "Mode", {.options = {"linear", "log"}, .defaultValue = "linear"})
        .addRange("levels", "Levels",
                  {.min = 0.0, .max = 1.0, .defaultValue = {.low = 0.1, .high = 0.9}})
        .addColour("tint", "Tint", {.defaultValue = {.r = 1.0F, .g = 0.5F, .b = 0.0F, .a = 1.0F}})
        .build()
        .value();
}

std::string parseError(std::string_view name, std::string_view text) {
    const auto value = sampleSchema().parse(name, text);
    if (value.has_value()) {
        return "";
    }
    CHECK(value.error().code == dvv::Errc::Format);
    return value.error().message;
}

std::string fromJsonError(std::string_view text) {
    const auto values = sampleSchema().fromJson(dvv::json::parse(text).value());
    if (values.has_value()) {
        return "";
    }
    CHECK(values.error().code == dvv::Errc::Format);
    return values.error().message;
}

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("parse reads every parameter type") {
    const auto schema = sampleSchema();
    CHECK(schema.parse("radius", "7").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{std::int64_t{7}});
    CHECK(schema.parse("radius", "-5").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{std::int64_t{-5}});
    CHECK(schema.parse("gain", "2.5").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{2.5});
    CHECK(schema.parse("gain", "1e-1").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{0.1});
    CHECK(schema.parse("gain", "3").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{3.0});
    CHECK(schema.parse("invert", "true").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{true});
    CHECK(schema.parse("invert", "0").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{false});
    CHECK(schema.parse("mode", "log").value() == dvv::ParameterValue{std::string("log")});
    CHECK(schema.parse("levels", "0.2,0.8").value() ==
          dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Range{.low = 0.2, .high = 0.8}});
    CHECK(schema.parse("levels", " 0.2 , 0.8 ").value() ==
          dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Range{.low = 0.2, .high = 0.8}});
    CHECK(schema.parse("tint", "1,0,0").value() ==
          dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Colour{.r = 1.0F, .g = 0.0F, .b = 0.0F, .a = 1.0F}});
    CHECK(schema.parse("tint", "0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4").value() ==
          dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Colour{.r = 0.1F, .g = 0.2F, .b = 0.3F, .a = 0.4F}});
}

TEST_CASE("parse names the problem") {
    CHECK(parseError("size", "1") == "unknown parameter 'size'");
    CHECK(parseError("radius", "7.5") == "parameter 'radius': '7.5' is not an integer");
    CHECK(parseError("radius", "") == "parameter 'radius': '' is not an integer");
    CHECK(parseError("radius", "99999999999999999999") ==
          "parameter 'radius': '99999999999999999999' is not an integer");
    CHECK(parseError("radius", "11") == "parameter 'radius': 11 is outside [-5, 10]");
    CHECK(parseError("gain", "abc") == "parameter 'gain': 'abc' is not a number");
    CHECK(parseError("gain", "2x") == "parameter 'gain': '2x' is not a number");
    CHECK(parseError("gain", "nan") == "parameter 'gain': not a finite number");
    CHECK(parseError("invert", "maybe") ==
          "parameter 'invert': 'maybe' is not a boolean (true, false, 1 or 0)");
    CHECK(parseError("mode", "cubic") == "parameter 'mode': 'cubic' is not one of: linear, log");
    CHECK(parseError("levels", "0.5") == "parameter 'levels': '0.5' is not a range (low,high)");
    CHECK(parseError("levels", "0.9,0.1") == "parameter 'levels': low 0.9 is above high 0.1");
    CHECK(parseError("tint", "1,1") ==
          "parameter 'tint': '1,1' is not a colour (r,g,b or r,g,b,a)");
    CHECK(parseError("tint", "2,0,0") == "parameter 'tint': colour components must be in [0, 1]");
}

TEST_CASE("format writes the text parse reads") {
    using dvv::ParameterSchema;
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(std::int64_t{-3}) == "-3");
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(0.1) == "0.1");
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(1.0) == "1");
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(true) == "true");
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(false) == "false");
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(std::string("log")) == "log");
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(dvv::Range{.low = 0.25, .high = 0.5}) == "0.25,0.5");
    CHECK(ParameterSchema::format(dvv::Colour{.r = 0.1F, .g = 0.2F, .b = 0.3F, .a = 1.0F}) ==
          "0.1,0.2,0.3,1");

    const auto schema = sampleSchema();
    const dvv::ParameterValues samples{
        {"radius", std::int64_t{-5}},
        {"gain", 1.0 / 3.0},
        {"invert", true},
        {"mode", std::string("log")},
        {"levels", dvv::Range{.low = 0.1, .high = 0.7}},
        {"tint", dvv::Colour{.r = 0.1F, .g = 0.7F, .b = 1.0F / 3.0F, .a = 0.0F}},
    };
    for (const auto& [name, value] : samples) {
        CAPTURE(name);
        CHECK(schema.parse(name, ParameterSchema::format(value)).value() == value);
    }
}

TEST_CASE("values round-trip through JSON") {
    const auto schema = sampleSchema();
    const dvv::ParameterValues values{
        {"radius", std::int64_t{7}},
        {"gain", 2.5},
        {"invert", true},
        {"mode", std::string("log")},
        {"levels", dvv::Range{.low = 0.1, .high = 0.9}},
        {"tint", dvv::Colour{.r = 0.1F, .g = 0.2F, .b = 0.3F, .a = 1.0F}},
    };
    const dvv::json::Value object = dvv::ParameterSchema::toJson(values);
    CHECK(dvv::json::dump(object) ==
          R"({"gain":2.5,"invert":true,"levels":[0.1,0.9],"mode":"log","radius":7,)"
          R"("tint":[0.1,0.2,0.3,1.0]})");
    CHECK(schema.fromJson(object).value() == values);
    CHECK(schema.fromJson(dvv::json::Value::object()).value().empty());
}

TEST_CASE("fromJson accepts an integer for a float and an RGB colour") {
    const auto values =
        sampleSchema().fromJson(dvv::json::parse(R"({"gain": 2, "tint": [0, 1, 0]})").value());
    REQUIRE(values.has_value());
    CHECK(values->at("gain") == dvv::ParameterValue{2.0});
    CHECK(values->at("tint") ==
          dvv::ParameterValue{dvv::Colour{.r = 0.0F, .g = 1.0F, .b = 0.0F, .a = 1.0F}});
}

TEST_CASE("fromJson names the problem") {
    CHECK(fromJsonError("[]") == "parameters: expected a JSON object, got []");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"size": 1})") == "unknown parameter 'size'");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"radius": 1.5})") ==
          "parameter 'radius': expected an integer, got 1.5");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"radius": 18446744073709551615})") ==
          "parameter 'radius': expected an integer, got 18446744073709551615");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"radius": 11})") == "parameter 'radius': 11 is outside [-5, 10]");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"gain": true})") == "parameter 'gain': expected a number, got true");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"invert": 1})") == "parameter 'invert': expected a boolean, got 1");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"mode": 2})") == "parameter 'mode': expected a string, got 2");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"mode": "cubic"})") ==
          "parameter 'mode': 'cubic' is not one of: linear, log");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"levels": [0.1]})") ==
          "parameter 'levels': expected [low, high], got [0.1]");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"levels": [0.1, "x"]})") ==
          R"(parameter 'levels': expected [low, high], got [0.1,"x"])");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"tint": {"r": 1}})") ==
          R"(parameter 'tint': expected [r, g, b] or [r, g, b, a], got {"r":1})");
    CHECK(fromJsonError(R"({"tint": [1e300, 0, 0]})") ==
          "parameter 'tint': colour components must be in [0, 1]");
}
```

The complete file after this task replaces the Task 8 file:

```cpp
#include "dvv/registry/parameters.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <charconv>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstddef>
#include <format>
#include <iterator>
#include <limits>
#include <optional>
#include <ranges>
#include <system_error>
#include <type_traits>

namespace dvv {

namespace {

std::unexpected<Error> invalid(const Parameter& parameter, std::string_view problem) {
    return makeError(Errc::Format, std::format("parameter '{}': {}", parameter.name, problem));
}

bool isValidName(std::string_view name) noexcept {
    return !name.empty() && std::ranges::all_of(name, [](char c) {
        return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') ||
               c == '_' || c == '.' || c == '-';
    });
}

std::string joinOptions(const std::vector<std::string>& options) {
    std::string text;
    for (const auto& option : options) {
        if (!text.empty()) {
            text += ", ";
        }
        text += option;
    }
    return text;
}

template <class T>
Result<void> checkBounds(const Parameter& parameter, T number, T min, T max) {
    if (number < min || number > max) {
        return invalid(parameter, std::format("{} is outside [{}, {}]", number, min, max));
    }
    return {};
}

template <class T>
Result<void> checkLimits(const Parameter& parameter, T min, T max) {
    if constexpr (std::is_floating_point_v<T>) {
        if (!std::isfinite(min) || !std::isfinite(max)) {
            return invalid(parameter, "min and max must be finite");
        }
    }
    if (min > max) {
        return invalid(parameter, std::format("min {} is above max {}", min, max));
    }
    return {};
}

bool isUnitInterval(float component) noexcept {
    return component >= 0.0F && component <= 1.0F;  // false for NaN
}

// Checks a value of any type against one parameter.
Result<void> checkValue(const Parameter& parameter, const ParameterValue& value) {
    const ParameterType type = parameterTypeOf(value);
    if (type != parameter.type()) {
        return invalid(parameter,
                       std::format("expected {}, got {}", parameterTypeName(parameter.type()),
                                   parameterTypeName(type)));
    }
    switch (type) {
        case ParameterType::Int: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<IntParameter>(parameter.spec);
            return checkBounds(parameter, std::get<std::int64_t>(value), spec.min, spec.max);
        }
        case ParameterType::Float: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<FloatParameter>(parameter.spec);
            const double number = std::get<double>(value);
            if (!std::isfinite(number)) {
                return invalid(parameter, "not a finite number");
            }
            return checkBounds(parameter, number, spec.min, spec.max);
        }
        case ParameterType::Bool: return {};
        case ParameterType::Enum: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<EnumParameter>(parameter.spec);
            const auto& text = std::get<std::string>(value);
            if (std::ranges::find(spec.options, text) == spec.options.end()) {
                return invalid(parameter, std::format("'{}' is not one of: {}", text,
                                                      joinOptions(spec.options)));
            }
            return {};
        }
        case ParameterType::Range: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<RangeParameter>(parameter.spec);
            const auto& range = std::get<Range>(value);
            if (!std::isfinite(range.low) || !std::isfinite(range.high)) {
                return invalid(parameter, "not a finite number");
            }
            if (range.low > range.high) {
                return invalid(parameter,
                               std::format("low {} is above high {}", range.low, range.high));
            }
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkBounds(parameter, range.low, spec.min, spec.max));
            return checkBounds(parameter, range.high, spec.min, spec.max);
        }
        case ParameterType::Colour: {
            const auto& colour = std::get<Colour>(value);
            if (!isUnitInterval(colour.r) || !isUnitInterval(colour.g) ||
                !isUnitInterval(colour.b) || !isUnitInterval(colour.a)) {
                return invalid(parameter, "colour components must be in [0, 1]");
            }
            return {};
        }
    }
    return invalid(parameter, "unknown parameter type");
}

// Checks a declaration: its limits or options, then its default.
Result<void> checkDeclaration(const Parameter& parameter) {
    switch (parameter.type()) {
        case ParameterType::Int: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<IntParameter>(parameter.spec);
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkLimits(parameter, spec.min, spec.max));
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Float: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<FloatParameter>(parameter.spec);
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkLimits(parameter, spec.min, spec.max));
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Range: {
            const auto& spec = std::get<RangeParameter>(parameter.spec);
            DVV_TRY_VOID(checkLimits(parameter, spec.min, spec.max));
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Enum: {
            const auto& options = std::get<EnumParameter>(parameter.spec).options;
            if (options.empty()) {
                return invalid(parameter, "an enum needs at least one option");
            }
            for (auto option = options.begin(); option != options.end(); ++option) {
                if (option->empty()) {
                    return invalid(parameter, "empty enum option");
                }
                if (std::find(std::next(option), options.end(), *option) != options.end()) {
                    return invalid(parameter, std::format("duplicate enum option '{}'", *option));
                }
            }
            break;
        }
        case ParameterType::Bool:
        case ParameterType::Colour: break;
    }
    return checkValue(parameter, parameter.defaultValue());
}

std::string_view trim(std::string_view text) noexcept {
    const auto first = text.find_first_not_of(" \t");
    if (first == std::string_view::npos) {
        return {};
    }
    return text.substr(first, text.find_last_not_of(" \t") - first + 1);
}

// The whole text (spaces around it ignored) as one number, or nothing.
template <class T>
std::optional<T> readNumber(std::string_view text) noexcept {
    text = trim(text);
    if (text.empty()) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    T number{};
    const char* const begin = text.data();
    const char* const end = begin + text.size();
    const auto [stop, status] = std::from_chars(begin, end, number);
    if (status != std::errc{} || stop != end) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    return number;
}

// Comma-separated numbers; nothing when a field is not a number.
template <class T>
std::optional<std::vector<T>> readNumbers(std::string_view text) {
    std::vector<T> numbers;
    for (const auto field : std::views::split(text, ',')) {
        const auto number = readNumber<T>(std::string_view(field.begin(), field.end()));
        if (!number) {
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        numbers.push_back(*number);
    }
    return numbers;
}

// A JSON number as a colour component; NaN (rejected by checkValue) when it does not fit a float.
float toComponent(const json::Value& number) {
    const auto wide = number.get<double>();
    return std::fabs(wide) <= std::numeric_limits<float>::max()
               ? static_cast<float>(wide)
               : std::numeric_limits<float>::quiet_NaN();
}

// The double with the float's shortest decimal form, so JSON shows 0.1 for 0.1F rather than
// 0.10000000149011612; reading it back as a float gives the same float.
double toJsonComponent(float component) {
    const std::string text = std::format("{}", component);
    double number = 0.0;
    std::from_chars(text.data(), text.data() + text.size(), number);
    return number;
}

// The CLI text of a value for one parameter, before checkValue.
Result<ParameterValue> readText(const Parameter& parameter, std::string_view text) {
    switch (parameter.type()) {
        case ParameterType::Int:
            if (const auto number = readNumber<std::int64_t>(text)) {
                return *number;
            }
            return invalid(parameter, std::format("'{}' is not an integer", text));
        case ParameterType::Float:
            if (const auto number = readNumber<double>(text)) {
                return *number;
            }
            return invalid(parameter, std::format("'{}' is not a number", text));
        case ParameterType::Bool: {
            const std::string_view word = trim(text);
            if (word == "true" || word == "1") {
                return true;
            }
            if (word == "false" || word == "0") {
                return false;
            }
            return invalid(parameter,
                           std::format("'{}' is not a boolean (true, false, 1 or 0)", text));
        }
        case ParameterType::Enum: return std::string(trim(text));
        case ParameterType::Range:
            if (const auto numbers = readNumbers<double>(text); numbers && numbers->size() == 2) {
                return Range{.low = (*numbers)[0], .high = (*numbers)[1]};
            }
            return invalid(parameter, std::format("'{}' is not a range (low,high)", text));
        case ParameterType::Colour:
            if (const auto numbers = readNumbers<float>(text);
                numbers && (numbers->size() == 3 || numbers->size() == 4)) {
                return Colour{.r = (*numbers)[0],
                              .g = (*numbers)[1],
                              .b = (*numbers)[2],
                              .a = numbers->size() == 4 ? (*numbers)[3] : 1.0F};
            }
            return invalid(parameter, std::format("'{}' is not a colour (r,g,b or r,g,b,a)", text));
    }
    return invalid(parameter, "unknown parameter type");
}

// The JSON form of a value for one parameter, before checkValue.
Result<ParameterValue> readJson(const Parameter& parameter, const json::Value& value) {
    const auto mismatch = [&](std::string_view expected) {
        return invalid(parameter, std::format("expected {}, got {}", expected, json::dump(value)));
    };
    const auto is_number_array = [&](std::size_t size) {
        return value.is_array() && value.size() == size &&
               std::all_of(value.begin(), value.end(),
                           [](const json::Value& item) { return item.is_number(); });
    };
    switch (parameter.type()) {
        case ParameterType::Int:
            // nlohmann/json keeps non-negative integers as unsigned; they must fit int64.
            if (!value.is_number_integer() ||
                (value.is_number_unsigned() &&
                 !std::in_range<std::int64_t>(value.get<std::uint64_t>()))) {
                return mismatch("an integer");
            }
            return value.get<std::int64_t>();
        case ParameterType::Float:
            if (!value.is_number()) {
                return mismatch("a number");
            }
            return value.get<double>();
        case ParameterType::Bool:
            if (!value.is_boolean()) {
                return mismatch("a boolean");
            }
            return value.get<bool>();
        case ParameterType::Enum:
            if (!value.is_string()) {
                return mismatch("a string");
            }
            return value.get<std::string>();
        case ParameterType::Range:
            if (!is_number_array(2)) {
                return mismatch("[low, high]");
            }
            return Range{.low = value[0].get<double>(), .high = value[1].get<double>()};
        case ParameterType::Colour:
            if (!is_number_array(3) && !is_number_array(4)) {
                return mismatch("[r, g, b] or [r, g, b, a]");
            }
            return Colour{.r = toComponent(value[0]),
                          .g = toComponent(value[1]),
                          .b = toComponent(value[2]),
                          .a = value.size() == 4 ? toComponent(value[3]) : 1.0F};
    }
    return invalid(parameter, "unknown parameter type");
}

}  // namespace

std::string_view parameterTypeName(ParameterType type) noexcept {
    switch (type) {
        case ParameterType::Int: return "int";
        case ParameterType::Float: return "float";
        case ParameterType::Bool: return "bool";
        case ParameterType::Enum: return "enum";
        case ParameterType::Range: return "range";
        case ParameterType::Colour: return "colour";
    }
    return "unknown";
}

ParameterType parameterTypeOf(const ParameterValue& value) noexcept {
    return static_cast<ParameterType>(value.index());
}

ParameterValue Parameter::defaultValue() const {
    return std::visit([](const auto& typed) { return ParameterValue{typed.defaultValue}; }, spec);
}

const Parameter* ParameterSchema::find(std::string_view name) const noexcept {
    const auto found = std::ranges::find(parameters_, name, &Parameter::name);
    return found != parameters_.end() ? &*found : nullptr;
}

ParameterValues ParameterSchema::defaults() const {
    ParameterValues values;
    for (const auto& parameter : parameters_) {
        values.emplace(parameter.name, parameter.defaultValue());
    }
    return values;
}

Result<void> ParameterSchema::validate(const ParameterValues& values) const {
    for (const auto& [name, value] : values) {
        const Parameter* parameter = find(name);
        if (parameter == nullptr) {
            return makeError(Errc::Format, std::format("unknown parameter '{}'", name));
        }
        DVV_TRY_VOID(checkValue(*parameter, value));
    }
    return {};
}

Result<ParameterValues> ParameterSchema::resolve(const ParameterValues& values) const {
    DVV_TRY_VOID(validate(values));
    ParameterValues resolved = defaults();
    for (const auto& [name, value] : values) {
        resolved.insert_or_assign(name, value);
    }
    return resolved;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addInt(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                           IntParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addFloat(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                             FloatParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addBool(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                            BoolParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addEnum(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                            EnumParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), std::move(spec)});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addRange(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                             RangeParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

ParameterSchema::Builder& ParameterSchema::Builder::addColour(std::string name, std::string label,
                                                              ColourParameter spec) {
    parameters_.push_back(Parameter{std::move(name), std::move(label), spec});
    return *this;
}

Result<ParameterSchema> ParameterSchema::Builder::build() const {
    const auto fail = [](std::string problem) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal, "parameter schema: " + std::move(problem));
    };
    for (auto parameter = parameters_.begin(); parameter != parameters_.end(); ++parameter) {
        if (!isValidName(parameter->name)) {
            return fail(std::format("invalid parameter name '{}'", parameter->name));
        }
        if (std::find_if(parameters_.begin(), parameter, [&](const Parameter& earlier) {
                return earlier.name == parameter->name;
            }) != parameter) {
            return fail(std::format("duplicate parameter '{}'", parameter->name));
        }
        if (auto checked = checkDeclaration(*parameter); !checked) {
            return fail(std::move(checked.error().message));
        }
    }
    return ParameterSchema(parameters_);
}

Result<ParameterValue> ParameterSchema::parse(std::string_view name, std::string_view text) const {
    const Parameter* parameter = find(name);
    if (parameter == nullptr) {
        return makeError(Errc::Format, std::format("unknown parameter '{}'", name));
    }
    DVV_TRY(ParameterValue value, readText(*parameter, text));
    DVV_TRY_VOID(checkValue(*parameter, value));
    return value;
}

std::string ParameterSchema::format(const ParameterValue& value) {
    return std::visit(
        [](const auto& typed) -> std::string {
            using T = std::decay_t<decltype(typed)>;
            if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, bool>) {
                return typed ? "true" : "false";
            } else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, std::string>) {
                return typed;
            } else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, Range>) {
                return std::format("{},{}", typed.low, typed.high);
            } else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, Colour>) {
                return std::format("{},{},{},{}", typed.r, typed.g, typed.b, typed.a);
            } else {
                return std::format("{}", typed);
            }
        },
        value);
}

json::Value ParameterSchema::toJson(const ParameterValues& values) {
    json::Value object = json::Value::object();
    for (const auto& [name, value] : values) {
        object[name] = std::visit(
            [](const auto& typed) -> json::Value {
                using T = std::decay_t<decltype(typed)>;
                if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, Range>) {
                    return json::Value::array({typed.low, typed.high});
                } else if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, Colour>) {
                    return json::Value::array({toJsonComponent(typed.r), toJsonComponent(typed.g),
                                               toJsonComponent(typed.b), toJsonComponent(typed.a)});
                } else {
                    return json::Value(typed);
                }
            },
            value);
    }
    return object;
}

Result<ParameterValues> ParameterSchema::fromJson(const json::Value& object) const {
    if (!object.is_object()) {
        return makeError(Errc::Format, std::format("parameters: expected a JSON object, got {}",
                                                   json::dump(object)));
    }
    ParameterValues values;
    for (auto item = object.begin(); item != object.end(); ++item) {
        const Parameter* parameter = find(item.key());
        if (parameter == nullptr) {
            return makeError(Errc::Format, std::format("unknown parameter '{}'", item.key()));
        }
        DVV_TRY(ParameterValue value, readJson(*parameter, item.value()));
        DVV_TRY_VOID(checkValue(*parameter, value));
        values.emplace(item.key(), std::move(value));
    }
    return values;
}

}  // namespace dvv
```

### Task 10: Component concept, ComponentRegistry and `DVV_REGISTER`

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-registry/include/dvv/registry/component_registry.h`, `dvv-registry/src/component_registry.cpp`, `dvv-registry/tests/test_component_registry.cpp`, `dvv-registry/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`, `dvv-registry/tests/compile_fail/run-compile-fail.cmake`
- Modify: `dvv-registry/CMakeLists.txt` (add `src/component_registry.cpp`), `dvv-registry/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (add `test_component_registry.cpp` and the compile-fail block)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `dvv::Managed<T>`, `dvv::CreationKey<F>`, `dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<T>(ObjectId owner)`, `dvv::ObjectId`, `dvv::test::TestAuthority` (`dvv/base/testing/test_authority.h`), the dvv logger, `DVV_TRY`; Tasks 7-9 (`Capabilities`, `Unavailable`, `ComponentInfo`, `Kind`, `ParameterSchema`, `ParameterValues`).
- Produces (namespace `dvv`): `template <class T, class I> concept Component` (`std::derived_from<T, I>`; static `ComponentInfo T::info() noexcept`; static `const ParameterSchema& T::schema()`; static `std::expected<void, Unavailable> T::probe(const Capabilities&)`); `struct ComponentStatus { ComponentInfo info; const ParameterSchema* schema = nullptr; std::optional<Unavailable> unavailable = std::nullopt; bool available() const noexcept; }`; `class ComponentRegistry final : public Managed<ComponentRegistry>` with `static std::unique_ptr<ComponentRegistry> create(const Capabilities&)`, `std::vector<ComponentStatus> list() const`, `std::vector<ComponentStatus> list(Kind) const`, `template <class I> Result<std::unique_ptr<I>> create(std::string_view id, const ParameterValues& values = {})`; macro `DVV_REGISTER(Interface, Type)` (namespace scope, the user adds `;`); `detail::RegistrationNode` / `detail::ComponentRegistration<I>`.
- A component is a `final` class implementing interface `I`, deriving `Managed<Self>`, with static `info()`, `schema()`, `probe()` and a constructor `(CreationKey<ComponentRegistry>, const ParameterValues&)`. Registration needs no allocation during static initialisation: the descriptor links itself into a `static constinit` intrusive list.
- Rules for component authors (record them in the README of the later components task): `DVV_REGISTER` only at namespace scope in a `.cpp` of a component static library that is linked with `dvv_link_components` (Task 11); create a `ComponentRegistry` only after static initialisation (from `main`); no dynamically loaded component libraries (registration happens before `main` and the list is not synchronised).
- Registry behaviour: `create(caps)` calls `info()`, then `schema()` and, if that worked, `probe(caps)`, each exactly once; a throwing `schema()` or `probe()` gives the reason `Internal: schema() threw: <Error::describe()>` / `Internal: probe() threw: <what>`; entries are sorted by id; every entry of a duplicated id becomes unavailable (`Internal: duplicate component id: <id>`) because cross-translation-unit registration order is unspecified. `create<I>(id, values)` returns `Errc::Unsupported` for an unknown id, another interface or an unavailable component, `Errc::Format` (`component '<id>': ...`) for invalid values, records the registry as owner of the new component, turns `std::bad_alloc` from the constructor into `Errc::OutOfMemory` (`component '<id>': out of memory`) without disabling the component, and turns any other constructor exception into `Errc::Internal` (`component '<id>' failed: <what>`) that marks the component unavailable for this registry only.
- Compile-fail tests `dvv_registry.compile_fail.1..5` (control target `dvv_registry_compile_ok` builds normally).

Note: the compile-fail runner is written here as its own copy (dvv-base's runner plus white-space folding, because MSBuild wraps long `static_assert` diagnostics between words). A later task (12a) moves the runner into dvv-base and removes this copy; do not anticipate that here.

Note: beyond the prototype, `ComponentRegistry::create<I>` maps `std::bad_alloc` from a constructor to `Errc::OutOfMemory` (`component '<id>': out of memory`) and does not disable the component (review finding: a transient out-of-memory is not a component bug); the test component `BadAlloc` and one doctest case cover it.
