```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <expected>
#include <memory>
#include <new>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"

namespace {

class IGreeter {
public:
    IGreeter() = default;
    IGreeter(const IGreeter&) = delete;
    IGreeter& operator=(const IGreeter&) = delete;
    IGreeter(IGreeter&&) = delete;
    IGreeter& operator=(IGreeter&&) = delete;
    virtual ~IGreeter() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string greet() const = 0;
};

class ICounter {
public:
    ICounter() = default;
    ICounter(const ICounter&) = delete;
    ICounter& operator=(const ICounter&) = delete;
    ICounter(ICounter&&) = delete;
    ICounter& operator=(ICounter&&) = delete;
    virtual ~ICounter() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual int count() const = 0;
};

using Key = dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry>;
using Probed = std::expected<void, dvv::Unavailable>;

// A greeter with parameters; always available.
class Greeter final : public IGreeter, public dvv::Managed<Greeter> {
public:
    Greeter(Key key, const dvv::ParameterValues& values)
        : Managed(std::move(key)),
          greeting_(std::get<std::string>(values.at("greeting"))),
          repeat_(std::get<std::int64_t>(values.at("repeat"))) {}

    static dvv::ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "test.greeter", .name = "Greeter", .kind = dvv::Kind::Tool, .version = 2};
    }
    static const dvv::ParameterSchema& schema() {
        static const dvv::ParameterSchema SCHEMA =
            dvv::ParameterSchema::Builder()
                .addEnum("greeting", "Greeting",
                         {.options = {"hello", "hi"}, .defaultValue = "hello"})
                .addInt("repeat", "Repeat", {.min = 1, .max = 3, .defaultValue = 1})
                .build()
                .value();
        return SCHEMA;
    }
    static Probed probe(const dvv::Capabilities& /*caps*/) { return {}; }

    [[nodiscard]] std::string greet() const override {
        std::string text;
        for (std::int64_t i = 0; i < repeat_; ++i) {
            text += text.empty() ? greeting_ : " " + greeting_;
        }
        return text;
    }

private:
    std::string greeting_;
    std::int64_t repeat_;
};
DVV_REGISTER(IGreeter, Greeter);

// Needs a capability; counts its probe calls.
class GpuGreeter final : public IGreeter, public dvv::Managed<GpuGreeter> {
public:
    GpuGreeter(Key key, const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}

    static dvv::ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "test.gpu_greeter", .name = "GPU greeter", .kind = dvv::Kind::Tool};
    }
    static const dvv::ParameterSchema& schema() {
        static const dvv::ParameterSchema SCHEMA;
        return SCHEMA;
    }
    static Probed probe(const dvv::Capabilities& caps) {
        probe_calls.fetch_add(1);
        return dvv::require(caps, {"gpu.compute"});
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::string greet() const override { return "hello from the GPU"; }

    static inline std::atomic<int> probe_calls{0};
};
DVV_REGISTER(IGreeter, GpuGreeter);

class ThrowingProbe final : public IGreeter, public dvv::Managed<ThrowingProbe> {
public:
    ThrowingProbe(Key key, const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}

    static dvv::ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "test.throwing_probe", .name = "Throwing probe", .kind = dvv::Kind::Tool};
    }
    static const dvv::ParameterSchema& schema() {
        static const dvv::ParameterSchema SCHEMA;
        return SCHEMA;
    }
    static Probed probe(const dvv::Capabilities& /*caps*/) {
        throw std::runtime_error("probe boom");
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::string greet() const override { return ""; }
};
DVV_REGISTER(IGreeter, ThrowingProbe);

// Its schema has a duplicate parameter, so schema() throws from build().value().
class BrokenSchema final : public IGreeter, public dvv::Managed<BrokenSchema> {

        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}

        return {.id = "test.broken_schema", .name = "Broken schema", .kind = dvv::Kind::Tool};

        static const dvv::ParameterSchema SCHEMA = dvv::ParameterSchema::Builder()
                                                       .addBool("flag", "Flag", {})
                                                       .addBool("flag", "Flag again", {})
                                                       .build()
                                                       .value();
        return SCHEMA;
    }
    static Probed probe(const dvv::Capabilities& /*caps*/) { return {}; }

    [[nodiscard]] std::string greet() const override { return ""; }
};
DVV_REGISTER(IGreeter, BrokenSchema);

class ThrowingConstructor final : public IGreeter, public dvv::Managed<ThrowingConstructor> {
public:
    ThrowingConstructor(Key key, const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) : Managed(std::move(key)) {

        return {.id = "test.throwing_constructor",
                .name = "Throwing constructor",

DVV_REGISTER(IGreeter, ThrowingConstructor);

// Its constructor runs out of memory; counts the constructor calls.
class BadAlloc final : public IGreeter, public dvv::Managed<BadAlloc> {
public:
    BadAlloc(Key key, const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) : Managed(std::move(key)) {

        return SCHEMA;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::string greet() const override { return ""; }

    static inline std::atomic<int> constructor_calls{0};
};
DVV_REGISTER(IGreeter, BadAlloc);

class Counter final : public ICounter, public dvv::Managed<Counter> {
public:
    Counter(Key key, const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept : Managed(std::move(key)) {}

    static dvv::ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "test.counter", .name = "Counter", .kind = dvv::Kind::Metric};
    }

        static const dvv::ParameterSchema SCHEMA;
        return SCHEMA;

};
DVV_REGISTER(ICounter, Counter);

// Two classes claiming the same id.
template <int N>
class Duplicate final : public ICounter, public dvv::Managed<Duplicate<N>> {
public:
    Duplicate(Key key, const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : dvv::Managed<Duplicate<N>>(std::move(key)) {}

    static dvv::ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "test.duplicate", .name = "Duplicate", .kind = dvv::Kind::Metric};
    }
    static const dvv::ParameterSchema& schema() {
        static const dvv::ParameterSchema SCHEMA;
        return SCHEMA;
    }
    static Probed probe(const dvv::Capabilities& /*caps*/) { return {}; }

    [[nodiscard]] int count() const override { return N; }
};
using FirstDuplicate = Duplicate<1>;
using SecondDuplicate = Duplicate<2>;
DVV_REGISTER(ICounter, FirstDuplicate);
DVV_REGISTER(ICounter, SecondDuplicate);

const dvv::ComponentStatus* findStatus(const std::vector<dvv::ComponentStatus>& statuses,
                                       std::string_view id) {
    const auto found = std::ranges::find(
        statuses, id, [](const dvv::ComponentStatus& status) { return status.info.id; });
    return found != statuses.end() ? &*found : nullptr;
}

std::string reasonOf(const dvv::ComponentRegistry& registry, std::string_view id) {
    const auto statuses = registry.list();
    const dvv::ComponentStatus* status = findStatus(statuses, id);
    REQUIRE(status != nullptr);
    return status->unavailable ? status->unavailable->reason : "";
}

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("the registry lists registered components by kind, sorted by id") {
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});

    const auto tools = registry->list(dvv::Kind::Tool);
    std::vector<std::string_view> ids;
    for (const auto& status : tools) {
        CHECK(status.info.kind == dvv::Kind::Tool);
        ids.push_back(status.info.id);
    }
    CHECK(ids == std::vector<std::string_view>{"test.bad_alloc", "test.broken_schema",
                                               "test.gpu_greeter", "test.greeter",
                                               "test.throwing_constructor",
                                               "test.throwing_probe"});

    const dvv::ComponentStatus* greeter = findStatus(tools, "test.greeter");
    REQUIRE(greeter != nullptr);
    CHECK(greeter->info == Greeter::info());
    CHECK(greeter->available());
    CHECK(greeter->schema == &Greeter::schema());

    const auto metrics = registry->list(dvv::Kind::Metric);
    CHECK(metrics.size() == 3);  // test.counter and the two test.duplicate
    CHECK(findStatus(metrics, "test.counter") != nullptr);
    CHECK(registry->list(dvv::Kind::Source).empty());
    CHECK(registry->list().size() == tools.size() + metrics.size());
}

TEST_CASE("probes decide availability and run once per registry") {
    const int before = GpuGreeter::probe_calls.load();
    const auto without = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    CHECK(GpuGreeter::probe_calls.load() == before + 1);
    CHECK(reasonOf(*without, "test.gpu_greeter") == "missing capability 'gpu.compute'");

    const auto with = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{"gpu.compute"});
    CHECK(GpuGreeter::probe_calls.load() == before + 2);
    CHECK(reasonOf(*with, "test.gpu_greeter").empty());
    auto greeter = with->create<IGreeter>("test.gpu_greeter");
    REQUIRE(greeter.has_value());
    CHECK((*greeter)->greet() == "hello from the GPU");
    static_cast<void>(with->list());
    CHECK(GpuGreeter::probe_calls.load() == before + 2);
}

TEST_CASE("throwing probes and schemas make a component unavailable") {
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    CHECK(reasonOf(*registry, "test.throwing_probe") == "Internal: probe() threw: probe boom");
    // The reason carries the Error of the failed build().value(), with its source location.
    const std::string schema_reason = reasonOf(*registry, "test.broken_schema");
    const std::string_view expected =
        "Internal: schema() threw: Internal: parameter schema: duplicate parameter 'flag'";
    CHECK(schema_reason.starts_with(expected));
    const auto statuses = registry->list();
    const dvv::ComponentStatus* broken = findStatus(statuses, "test.broken_schema");
    REQUIRE(broken != nullptr);
    CHECK(broken->schema == nullptr);

    const auto created = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.throwing_probe");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(created.has_value());
    CHECK(created.error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(created.error().message ==
          "component 'test.throwing_probe' is unavailable: Internal: probe() threw: probe boom");
}

TEST_CASE("a duplicated id disables every component that claims it") {
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    const auto metrics = registry->list(dvv::Kind::Metric);
    const auto duplicates = std::ranges::count_if(metrics, [](const dvv::ComponentStatus& status) {
        return status.info.id == "test.duplicate" && status.unavailable &&
               status.unavailable->reason == "Internal: duplicate component id: test.duplicate";
    });
    CHECK(duplicates == 2);
    CHECK(registry->create<ICounter>("test.duplicate").error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);
}

TEST_CASE("create builds a component with validated and completed parameters") {
    auto& lifetimes = dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance();
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    const std::size_t live_before = lifetimes.liveCount();

    auto plain = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.greeter");
    REQUIRE(plain.has_value());
    CHECK((*plain)->greet() == "hello");

    auto custom = registry->create<IGreeter>(
        "test.greeter", {{"greeting", std::string("hi")}, {"repeat", std::int64_t{3}}});
    REQUIRE(custom.has_value());
    CHECK((*custom)->greet() == "hi hi hi");
    CHECK(lifetimes.liveCount() == live_before + 2);

    // The registry is the recorded owner of what it creates.
    const auto* managed = dynamic_cast<const dvv::ManagedObject*>(custom->get());
    REQUIRE(managed != nullptr);
    CHECK(managed->ownerId() == registry->objectId());

    auto counter = registry->create<ICounter>("test.counter");
    REQUIRE(counter.has_value());
    CHECK((*counter)->count() == 42);

    plain->reset();
    custom->reset();
    counter->reset();
    CHECK(lifetimes.liveCount() == live_before);
}

TEST_CASE("create reports unknown ids, other interfaces and invalid values") {
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});

    const auto unknown = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.missing");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(unknown.has_value());
    CHECK(unknown.error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(unknown.error().message == "unknown component 'test.missing'");

    const auto wrong_interface = registry->create<ICounter>("test.greeter");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(wrong_interface.has_value());
    CHECK(wrong_interface.error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(wrong_interface.error().message.starts_with(
        "component 'test.greeter' does not implement "));
    CHECK(wrong_interface.error().message.find("ICounter") != std::string::npos);

    const auto invalid = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.greeter", {{"repeat", std::int64_t{9}}});
    REQUIRE_FALSE(invalid.has_value());
    CHECK(invalid.error().code == dvv::Errc::Format);
    CHECK(invalid.error().message ==
          "component 'test.greeter': parameter 'repeat': 9 is outside [1, 3]");
}

TEST_CASE("a throwing constructor disables the component for the registry's lifetime") {
    const std::size_t live_before = dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount();
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    CHECK(reasonOf(*registry, "test.throwing_constructor").empty());

    const auto first = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.throwing_constructor");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(first.has_value());
    CHECK(first.error().code == dvv::Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(first.error().message ==
          "component 'test.throwing_constructor' failed: constructor boom");
    CHECK(reasonOf(*registry, "test.throwing_constructor") ==
          "Internal: constructor threw: constructor boom");

    const auto second = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.throwing_constructor");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(second.has_value());
    CHECK(second.error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);

    // Other registries are not affected.
    const auto fresh = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    CHECK(reasonOf(*fresh, "test.throwing_constructor").empty());
    // The registries themselves are the only live objects this test added.
    CHECK(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == live_before + 2);
}

TEST_CASE("std::bad_alloc from a constructor is out of memory and does not disable the component") {
    const std::size_t live_before = dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount();
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    const int calls_before = BadAlloc::constructor_calls.load();

    const auto first = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.bad_alloc");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(first.has_value());
    CHECK(first.error().code == dvv::Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(first.error().message == "component 'test.bad_alloc': out of memory");
    CHECK(reasonOf(*registry, "test.bad_alloc").empty());
    CHECK(BadAlloc::constructor_calls.load() == calls_before + 1);

    // The component was not disabled: a second create still reaches the constructor.
    const auto second = registry->create<IGreeter>("test.bad_alloc");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(second.has_value());
    CHECK(second.error().code == dvv::Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(BadAlloc::constructor_calls.load() == calls_before + 2);
    CHECK(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == live_before + 1);
}

TEST_CASE("the registry is a managed object") {
    static_assert(dvv::ManagedType<dvv::ComponentRegistry>);
    static_assert(dvv::Component<Greeter, IGreeter>);
    static_assert(!dvv::Component<Greeter, ICounter>);
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    CHECK(registry->typeName().find("ComponentRegistry") != std::string_view::npos);
}
```

`dvv-registry/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`:

```cpp
// Compile-fail cases for components (spec §9.4). DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE selects one:
//   0  control: a registered component created through the registry compiles
//   1  constructing a CreationKey<ComponentRegistry> outside the registry
//   2  constructing a registered component without a key
//   3  registering a class that is not final
//   4  registering a class whose constructor takes another authority's key
//   5  registering a class without probe()
#include <expected>
#include <memory>
#include <utility>

#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/base/testing/test_authority.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"

namespace {

class IWidget {
public:
    IWidget() = default;
    IWidget(const IWidget&) = delete;
    IWidget& operator=(const IWidget&) = delete;
    IWidget(IWidget&&) = delete;
    IWidget& operator=(IWidget&&) = delete;
    virtual ~IWidget() = default;
};

using Probed = std::expected<void, dvv::Unavailable>;

// The static parts every widget shares.
struct WidgetStatics {
    static dvv::ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "compile_fail.widget", .name = "Widget", .kind = dvv::Kind::Tool};
    }
    static const dvv::ParameterSchema& schema() {
        static const dvv::ParameterSchema SCHEMA;
        return SCHEMA;
    }
    static Probed probe(const dvv::Capabilities& /*caps*/) { return {}; }
};

class Widget final : public IWidget, public WidgetStatics, public dvv::Managed<Widget> {
public:
    Widget(dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry> key,
           const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}
};
DVV_REGISTER(IWidget, Widget);

#if DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 3
class NotFinal : public IWidget, public WidgetStatics, public dvv::Managed<NotFinal> {
public:
    NotFinal(dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry> key,
             const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}
};
DVV_REGISTER(IWidget, NotFinal);
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 4
class WrongKey final : public IWidget, public WidgetStatics, public dvv::Managed<WrongKey> {
public:
    WrongKey(dvv::CreationKey<dvv::test::TestAuthority> key,
             const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}
};
DVV_REGISTER(IWidget, WrongKey);
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 5
class NoProbe final : public IWidget, public dvv::Managed<NoProbe> {
public:
    NoProbe(dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry> key,
            const dvv::ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}
    static dvv::ComponentInfo info() noexcept { return WidgetStatics::info(); }
    static const dvv::ParameterSchema& schema() { return WidgetStatics::schema(); }
};
DVV_REGISTER(IWidget, NoProbe);
#endif

}  // namespace

int main() {
    const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
    auto widget = registry->create<IWidget>("compile_fail.widget");
#if DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 1
    dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry> forged(
        dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<Widget>());
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 2
    Widget direct(dvv::ParameterValues{});
#endif
    return widget.has_value() ? 0 : 1;
}
```

`dvv-registry/tests/compile_fail/run-compile-fail.cmake`:

```cmake
# Builds one compile-fail target and requires it to fail for the expected reason.
# Called by the dvv_registry.compile_fail.* tests with -DBUILD_DIR, -DCONFIG, -DTARGET and
# -DPATTERN (a regular expression the compiler diagnostics must match). dvv-base's runner, plus
# white-space folding: MSBuild wraps long diagnostics (static_assert messages) between words.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

execute_process(
    COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" --build "${BUILD_DIR}" --target "${TARGET}" --config "${CONFIG}"
    RESULT_VARIABLE status
    OUTPUT_VARIABLE output
    ERROR_VARIABLE output)

if(status EQUAL 0)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "${TARGET} built successfully, but it must fail to compile")
endif()
string(REGEX REPLACE "[ \t\r\n]+" " " folded "${output}")
if(NOT folded MATCHES "${PATTERN}")
    message(FATAL_ERROR
            "${TARGET} failed (${status}) but not for the expected reason: the output does not "
            "match '${PATTERN}'.\n${output}")
endif()
```

```cmake
# compile with the expected diagnostic: a forged registry key, construction without a key, and
# DVV_REGISTER of a class that is not final, takes another authority's key or lacks probe().

else()
    set(compile_fail_patterns "private" "no matching constructor")
endif()

     "must be final" "must be constructible from" "must implement IWidget")
foreach(case IN ITEMS 1 2 3 4 5)
    set(target dvv_registry_compile_fail_${case})
    add_executable(${target} EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL ${compile_fail_source})
    target_compile_definitions(${target} PRIVATE DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE=${case})
    target_link_libraries(${target} PRIVATE dvv::registry)
    dvv_set_compiler_options(${target})
    math(EXPR pattern_index "${case} - 1")
    list(GET compile_fail_patterns ${pattern_index} pattern)
    # cmake --build needs the top-level tree; in the workspace build dvv-registry is a subproject.
    add_test(NAME dvv_registry.compile_fail.${case}
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                     -DBUILD_DIR=${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}

                     -DTARGET=${target}
                     "-DPATTERN=${pattern}"
                     -P ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/compile_fail/run-compile-fail.cmake)
    set_tests_properties(dvv_registry.compile_fail.${case} PROPERTIES RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree)
endforeach()
```

`dvv-registry/include/dvv/registry/component_registry.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Components and the registry that creates them (spec §9.1, §9.2, §9.4).
//
// A component is a final class implementing one interface I (IMetric, ISource, ...), deriving
// Managed<Self>, with static info(), schema() and probe() and a constructor taking
// (CreationKey<ComponentRegistry>, const ParameterValues&). One line at namespace scope in its .cpp
// registers it:
//
//     DVV_REGISTER(IMetric, PsnrMetric);
//
// The line defines a static descriptor that links itself into a process-wide list during static
// initialisation (no allocation). ComponentRegistry reads that list; it is the only creator of
// components. Component libraries are linked with dvv_link_components() (WHOLE_ARCHIVE), so the
// descriptors survive linking.

#include <concepts>
#include <expected>
#include <format>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <new>
#include <optional>
#include <string_view>
#include <type_traits>
#include <typeinfo>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/registry/capabilities.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_info.h"
#include "dvv/registry/parameters.h"

namespace dvv {

// T implements interface I as a component (spec §9.2). info() must not throw: the registry needs
// the id to report anything else about the component.
template <class T, class I>
concept Component = std::derived_from<T, I> && requires(const Capabilities& caps) {
    { T::info() } noexcept -> std::same_as<ComponentInfo>;
    { T::schema() } -> std::same_as<const ParameterSchema&>;
    { T::probe(caps) } -> std::same_as<std::expected<void, Unavailable>>;
};

class ComponentRegistry;

namespace detail {

// One registered component; a static object created by DVV_REGISTER. The constructor links it
// into the process-wide list, so registration happens during static initialisation, before main.
class RegistrationNode {
public:
    RegistrationNode(const RegistrationNode&) = delete;
    RegistrationNode& operator=(const RegistrationNode&) = delete;
    RegistrationNode(RegistrationNode&&) = delete;
    RegistrationNode& operator=(RegistrationNode&&) = delete;

protected:
    using InfoFunction = ComponentInfo (*)() noexcept;
    using SchemaFunction = const ParameterSchema& (*)();
    using ProbeFunction = std::expected<void, Unavailable> (*)(const Capabilities&);

    RegistrationNode(const std::type_info& interface, InfoFunction info, SchemaFunction schema,
                     ProbeFunction probe) noexcept;
    ~RegistrationNode() = default;

private:
    friend class dvv::ComponentRegistry;

    // Constant-initialised, so it is valid before any registration runs.
    static constinit RegistrationNode* list_head;

    const std::type_info* interface_;
    InfoFunction info_;
    SchemaFunction schema_;
    ProbeFunction probe_;
    RegistrationNode* next_;
};

template <class I>
class ComponentRegistration final : public RegistrationNode {
public:
    template <class T>
    explicit ComponentRegistration(std::type_identity<T> type) noexcept;

private:
    friend class dvv::ComponentRegistry;
    using CreateFunction = std::unique_ptr<I> (*)(ObjectId owner, const ParameterValues& values);

    CreateFunction create_;
};

}  // namespace detail

// What the registry knows about one component.
struct ComponentStatus {
    ComponentInfo info;
    const ParameterSchema* schema = nullptr;  // null when schema() threw
    // Empty when the component can be created.
    std::optional<Unavailable> unavailable = std::nullopt;

    [[nodiscard]] bool available() const noexcept { return !unavailable.has_value(); }
};

// The creation authority of every component (spec §9.4). Thread-safe.
class ComponentRegistry final : public Managed<ComponentRegistry> {
    struct Entry {
        const detail::RegistrationNode* registration;
        ComponentStatus status;
    };

public:
    // Runs every registered component's schema() and probe(caps) once. A component whose schema()
    // or probe() throws is unavailable with an "Internal: ..." reason, and so is every component
    // whose id is registered more than once.
    [[nodiscard]] static std::unique_ptr<ComponentRegistry> create(const Capabilities& caps);

    // For create(caps) only (Entry is private).
    ComponentRegistry(CreationKey<ComponentRegistry> key, std::vector<Entry> entries) noexcept;

    // Components sorted by id: all of them, or those of one kind.
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<ComponentStatus> list() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<ComponentStatus> list(Kind kind) const;

    // Creates component `id` with `values` validated against its schema and completed with the
    // defaults. Errc::Unsupported for an unknown or unavailable id or one with another interface,
    // Errc::Format for invalid values. std::bad_alloc from the constructor becomes
    // Errc::OutOfMemory and leaves the component available; any other constructor exception becomes
    // Errc::Internal and makes the component unavailable for this registry's lifetime.
    template <class I>
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::unique_ptr<I>> create(std::string_view id,
                                                    const ParameterValues& values = {});

private:
    template <class I>
    friend class detail::ComponentRegistration;

    struct Prepared {
        const detail::RegistrationNode* registration;
        ParameterValues values;
    };

    template <class I, class T>
    static std::unique_ptr<I> construct(ObjectId owner, const ParameterValues& values) {
        return std::make_unique<T>(
            CreationKey<ComponentRegistry>(LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<T>(owner)), values);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] Result<Prepared> prepare(std::string_view id, const std::type_info& interface,
                                           const ParameterValues& values) const;
    // Called from a catch block: disables `id` and describes the current exception.
    [[nodiscard]] Error constructionFailed(std::string_view id);

    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    std::vector<Entry> entries_;  // sorted by id
};

template <class I>
template <class T>
detail::ComponentRegistration<I>::ComponentRegistration(std::type_identity<T> /*type*/) noexcept
    : RegistrationNode(
          typeid(I), []() noexcept { return T::info(); },
          []() -> const ParameterSchema& { return T::schema(); },
          [](const Capabilities& caps) { return T::probe(caps); }),
      create_(&ComponentRegistry::construct<I, T>) {}

template <class I>
Result<std::unique_ptr<I>> ComponentRegistry::create(std::string_view id,
                                                     const ParameterValues& values) {
    DVV_TRY(Prepared prepared, prepare(id, typeid(I), values));
    // prepare() checked the interface, so the node is a ComponentRegistration<I>.
    const auto& registration =
        static_cast<const detail::ComponentRegistration<I>&>(*prepared.registration);
    try {
        return registration.create_(objectId(), prepared.values);
    } catch (const std::bad_alloc&) {
        // A shortage of memory is not the component's fault: it stays available.
        return makeError(Errc::OutOfMemory, std::format("component '{}': out of memory", id));
    } catch (...) {
        return std::unexpected(constructionFailed(id));
    }
}

}  // namespace dvv

// Registers component Type for interface Interface; use once at namespace scope in the component's
// .cpp, followed by a semicolon. Checks the rules of spec §9.2 and §9.4 at compile time.
// Interface and Type are type names (template arguments), which cannot be parenthesized.
// NOLINTBEGIN(bugprone-macro-parentheses)
#define DVV_REGISTER(Interface, Type)                                                         \
    static_assert(std::is_final_v<Type>, "DVV_REGISTER: " #Type " must be final");            \
    static_assert(std::derived_from<Type, ::dvv::Managed<Type>>,                              \
                  "DVV_REGISTER: " #Type " must derive dvv::Managed<" #Type ">");             \
    static_assert(!std::is_copy_constructible_v<Type> && !std::is_move_constructible_v<Type>, \
                  "DVV_REGISTER: " #Type " must not be copy or move constructible");          \
    static_assert(std::has_virtual_destructor_v<Interface>,                                   \
                  "DVV_REGISTER: " #Interface " needs a virtual destructor");                 \
    static_assert(::dvv::Component<Type, Interface>,                                          \
                  "DVV_REGISTER: " #Type " must implement " #Interface                        \
                  " and provide static info() noexcept, schema() and probe()");               \
    static_assert(std::is_constructible_v<Type, ::dvv::CreationKey<::dvv::ComponentRegistry>, \
                                          const ::dvv::ParameterValues&>,                     \
                  "DVV_REGISTER: " #Type                                                      \
                  " must be constructible from "                                              \
                  "(dvv::CreationKey<dvv::ComponentRegistry>, const dvv::ParameterValues&)"); \
    static const ::dvv::detail::ComponentRegistration<Interface> DVV_DETAIL_CONCAT(           \
        dvv_registration_, __LINE__)(std::type_identity<Type>{})
// NOLINTEND(bugprone-macro-parentheses)
```

`dvv-registry/src/component_registry.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <exception>
#include <format>
#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/log.h"

namespace dvv {

namespace detail {

constinit RegistrationNode* RegistrationNode::list_head = nullptr;

RegistrationNode::RegistrationNode(const std::type_info& interface, InfoFunction info,
                                   SchemaFunction schema, ProbeFunction probe) noexcept
    : interface_(&interface), info_(info), schema_(schema), probe_(probe), next_(list_head) {
    list_head = this;
}

}  // namespace detail

namespace {

// Describes the exception being handled; call only from a catch block.
std::string describeCurrentException() {
    try {
        throw;
    } catch (const std::bad_expected_access<Error>& error) {
        // A failed Result<T>::value(), e.g. ParameterSchema::Builder::build().value().
        return error.error().describe();
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        return error.what();
    } catch (...) {
        return "unknown exception";
    }
}

std::string internalReason(std::string_view what, std::string_view detail) {
    return std::format("{}: {}: {}", errcName(Errc::Internal), what, detail);
}

}  // namespace

std::unique_ptr<ComponentRegistry> ComponentRegistry::create(const Capabilities& caps) {
    std::vector<Entry> entries;
    for (const detail::RegistrationNode* node = detail::RegistrationNode::list_head;
         node != nullptr; node = node->next_) {
        Entry entry{node, ComponentStatus{.info = node->info_()}};
        ComponentStatus& status = entry.status;
        try {
            status.schema = &node->schema_();
        } catch (...) {
            status.unavailable =
                Unavailable{internalReason("schema() threw", describeCurrentException())};
        }
        if (status.schema != nullptr) {
            try {
                if (auto probed = node->probe_(caps); !probed) {
                    status.unavailable = std::move(probed.error());
                }
            } catch (...) {
                status.unavailable =
                    Unavailable{internalReason("probe() threw", describeCurrentException())};
            }
        }
        if (const auto& unavailable = status.unavailable) {
            DVV_LOG_INFO("component '{}' is unavailable: {}", status.info.id, unavailable->reason);
        }
        entries.push_back(std::move(entry));
    }

    std::ranges::stable_sort(entries, {}, [](const Entry& entry) { return entry.status.info.id; });
    // Registration order across translation units is unspecified, so no copy of a duplicated id
    // may win: all of them are unavailable.
    for (auto first = entries.begin(); first != entries.end();) {
        const auto last = std::ranges::find_if(first, entries.end(), [&](const Entry& entry) {
            return entry.status.info.id != first->status.info.id;
        });
        if (std::distance(first, last) > 1) {
            DVV_LOG_ERROR("component id '{}' is registered more than once", first->status.info.id);
            for (auto entry = first; entry != last; ++entry) {
                entry->status.unavailable = Unavailable{
                    internalReason("duplicate component id", std::string(first->status.info.id))};
            }
        }
        first = last;
    }

    return std::make_unique<ComponentRegistry>(
        CreationKey<ComponentRegistry>(LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<ComponentRegistry>()),
        std::move(entries));
}

ComponentRegistry::ComponentRegistry(CreationKey<ComponentRegistry> key,
                                     std::vector<Entry> entries) noexcept
    : Managed(std::move(key)), entries_(std::move(entries)) {}

std::vector<ComponentStatus> ComponentRegistry::list() const {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    std::vector<ComponentStatus> statuses;
    statuses.reserve(entries_.size());
    for (const Entry& entry : entries_) {
        statuses.push_back(entry.status);
    }
    return statuses;
}

std::vector<ComponentStatus> ComponentRegistry::list(Kind kind) const {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    std::vector<ComponentStatus> statuses;
    for (const Entry& entry : entries_) {
        if (entry.status.info.kind == kind) {
            statuses.push_back(entry.status);
        }
    }
    return statuses;
}

Result<ComponentRegistry::Prepared> ComponentRegistry::prepare(
    std::string_view id, const std::type_info& interface, const ParameterValues& values) const {
    const detail::RegistrationNode* registration = nullptr;
    const ParameterSchema* schema = nullptr;
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        const auto found = std::ranges::find(
            entries_, id, [](const Entry& entry) { return entry.status.info.id; });
        if (found == entries_.end()) {
            return makeError(Errc::Unsupported, std::format("unknown component '{}'", id));
        }
        if (*found->registration->interface_ != interface) {
            return makeError(Errc::Unsupported,
                             std::format("component '{}' does not implement {}", id,
                                         detail::demangle(interface.name())));
        }
        if (const auto& unavailable = found->status.unavailable) {
            return makeError(Errc::Unsupported, std::format("component '{}' is unavailable: {}", id,
                                                            unavailable->reason));
        }
        registration = found->registration;
        schema = found->status.schema;
    }
    auto resolved = schema->resolve(values);
    if (!resolved) {
        return makeError(Errc::Format,
                         std::format("component '{}': {}", id, resolved.error().message));
    }
    return Prepared{registration, std::move(*resolved)};
}

Error ComponentRegistry::constructionFailed(std::string_view id) {
    const std::string what = describeCurrentException();
    DVV_LOG_ERROR("component '{}' threw from its constructor and is disabled: {}", id, what);
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        for (Entry& entry : entries_) {
            if (entry.status.info.id == id) {
                entry.status.unavailable = Unavailable{internalReason("constructor threw", what)};
            }
        }
    }
    return makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("component '{}' failed: {}", id, what)).error();
}

}  // namespace dvv
```

```powershell
git -C dvv-registry add -A
@'
feat: add the component registry and DVV_REGISTER

Component<T, I> (spec §9.2) requires T to implement I with static info() (noexcept), schema() and probe(). DVV_REGISTER(Interface, Type) static_asserts the rules of spec §9.4 (final, derives Managed<Type>, not copy or move constructible, a virtual destructor on the interface, Component, constructible from the registry's key and the parameter values) and defines a static descriptor that links itself into a process-wide intrusive list during static initialisation.

ComponentRegistry is managed and the only creator of components: create(caps) runs every schema() and probe() once (a throwing one, or a duplicated id, makes the component unavailable with an Internal reason), list() and list(kind) report availability sorted by id, and create<I>(id, values) checks the interface and availability, validates and completes the values, records the registry as owner, turns std::bad_alloc from a constructor into Errc::OutOfMemory (the component stays available) and any other constructor exception into Errc::Internal that disables the component for the registry's lifetime.

Compile-fail tests reuse dvv-base's runner (with white space folded, because MSBuild wraps long static_assert diagnostics): a forged key, construction without a key, and registering a class that is not final, takes another authority's key or lacks probe().

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C dvv-registry commit -F -
```

### Task 11: `dvv_link_components`

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-registry/cmake/DvvComponents.cmake`, `dvv-registry/tests/linked_component.h`, `dvv-registry/tests/linked_component.cpp`, `dvv-registry/tests/link_check.cpp`, `dvv-registry/tests/package/component.h`, `dvv-registry/tests/package/component.cpp`
- Modify: `dvv-registry/CMakeLists.txt`, `dvv-registry/cmake/dvv_registryConfig.cmake.in`, `dvv-registry/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `dvv-registry/tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`, `dvv-registry/tests/package/main.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 10's `DVV_REGISTER`, `ComponentRegistry`, `Component`.
- Produces (CMake, installed with the package and included by `dvv_registryConfig.cmake`; included in-tree by the project): `dvv_link_components(<target> [<component library>...])` links each static component library into `<target>` with `$<LINK_LIBRARY:WHOLE_ARCHIVE,<lib>>` (MSVC `/WHOLEARCHIVE`, GNU `--whole-archive`), so the `DVV_REGISTER` descriptors survive linking although nothing references them. It fails with `FATAL_ERROR` when `<target>` or a library is not a target or a library is not a `STATIC_LIBRARY`; an empty list is allowed (every component switched off). Use it for executables and shared libraries; a library must not also be linked plainly into the same target.
- Tests: `dvv_registry.link.whole_archive` (component present and creatable) and `dvv_registry.link.plain` (the control: a plain link must drop the component).

Note (deferred items for the documentation task, like Task 2's note): (1) `dvv_add_component()` (KIND/SHADERS/REQUIRES) is deferred; M1a components are registered with `DVV_REGISTER` and linked with `dvv_link_components` only. (2) Spec §9.2 must be updated: the concept is `Component<T, I>` (not `Component<I>`), additionally requires `std::derived_from<T, I>` and a `noexcept` static `info()`; the §9.2 code block differs from Task 10.

```cpp
// A component in its own static library; see linked_component.h.
#include "linked_component.h"

#include <expected>
#include <utility>

#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"

namespace dvv::test {

namespace {

class Linked final : public ILinked, public Managed<Linked> {
public:
    Linked(CreationKey<ComponentRegistry> key, const ParameterValues& /*values*/) noexcept
        : Managed(std::move(key)) {}

    static ComponentInfo info() noexcept {
        return {.id = "test.linked", .name = "Linked", .kind = Kind::Analyser};
    }
    static const ParameterSchema& schema() {
        static const ParameterSchema SCHEMA;
        return SCHEMA;
    }
    static std::expected<void, Unavailable> probe(const Capabilities& /*caps*/) { return {}; }

    [[nodiscard]] int answer() const override { return 42; }
};
DVV_REGISTER(ILinked, Linked);

}  // namespace

}  // namespace dvv::test
```

`dvv-registry/tests/link_check.cpp`:

```cpp
// Link test: reports whether the component of dvv_registry_linked_component is registered in this
// executable. Usage: <exe> present|absent; exit code 0 when that is what it finds.
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdio>
#include <span>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/leak_check.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"
#include "linked_component.h"

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    const std::span<char*> arguments(argv, static_cast<std::size_t>(argc));
    if (arguments.size() != 2) {
        std::fputs("usage: link_check present|absent\n", stderr);
        return 2;
    }
    const bool expected = std::string_view(arguments[1]) == "present";

    int status = 1;
    {
        const auto registry = dvv::ComponentRegistry::create(dvv::Capabilities{});
        auto linked = registry->create<dvv::test::ILinked>("test.linked");
        const bool present = linked.has_value() && (*linked)->answer() == 42;
        std::printf("test.linked is %s\n", present ? "present" : "absent");
        status = present == expected ? 0 : 1;
    }
    return dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(status);
}
```

```cmake
# symbol, is registered when linked with dvv_link_components() and dropped by a plain link. The

endforeach()

add_test(NAME dvv_registry.link.plain COMMAND dvv_registry_link_plain absent)
set_tests_properties(dvv_registry.link.whole_archive dvv_registry.link.plain PROPERTIES
    TIMEOUT 60
    ENVIRONMENT DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail
    ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "${DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT}")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`dvv-registry/cmake/DvvComponents.cmake`:

```cmake
# Component linking (spec §9.1). Installed with dvv_registry and included by its package
# configuration; in-tree the dvv_registry project includes it.
include_guard(GLOBAL)

# dvv_link_components(<target> [<component library>...])
# Links each component library into <target> as a whole archive ($<LINK_LIBRARY:WHOLE_ARCHIVE,...>:
# MSVC /WHOLEARCHIVE, GNU --whole-archive, Apple -force_load), so the DVV_REGISTER descriptors in it
# survive linking although nothing references them. Use it for executables and shared libraries.
# Each library must be a static library target that is linked into <target> only this way (CMake
# rejects a library linked both with and without WHOLE_ARCHIVE). An empty list is allowed (every
# component switched off).
function(dvv_link_components target)
    if(NOT TARGET ${target})
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_link_components: '${target}' is not a target")
    endif()
    foreach(library IN LISTS ARGN)
        if(NOT TARGET ${library})
            message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_link_components: '${library}' is not a target")
        endif()
        get_target_property(type ${library} TYPE)
        if(NOT type STREQUAL "STATIC_LIBRARY")
            message(FATAL_ERROR
                    "dvv_link_components: '${library}' is a ${type}; component libraries are static")
        endif()
        target_link_libraries(${target} PRIVATE "$<LINK_LIBRARY:WHOLE_ARCHIVE,${library}>")
    endforeach()
endfunction()
```

### Task 12a: Shared compile-fail runner

**Files:**
- dvv-base: Create `dvv-base/cmake/run-compile-fail.cmake`; Delete `dvv-base/tests/compile_fail/run-compile-fail.cmake`; Modify `dvv-base/CMakeLists.txt`, `dvv-base/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `dvv-base/tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`
- dvv-registry: Delete `dvv-registry/tests/compile_fail/run-compile-fail.cmake`; Modify `dvv-registry/tests/CMakeLists.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DVV_CMAKE_DIR` (Task 1), the `dvv_build_tree` lock name, dvv-registry's compile-fail tests `dvv_registry.compile_fail.1` to `.5` (Task 10).
- Produces (CMake, installed with the `dvv_base` package next to `DvvCompilerOptions.cmake`): the script `${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/run-compile-fail.cmake`, called as `${CMAKE_COMMAND} -DBUILD_DIR=<top-level build tree> -DCONFIG=<config> -DTARGET=<target> "-DPATTERN=<regex>" -P ${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/run-compile-fail.cmake`. It builds `<target>`, requires the build to fail, folds white space in the compiler output to single spaces (MSBuild wraps long `static_assert` diagnostics between words) and requires the folded output to match `PATTERN`. It stops with `run-compile-fail.cmake needs -D<name>=<non-empty value>` when one of the four arguments is missing or empty (an empty `PATTERN` would match any output). Every dvv repository (dvv-gpu from Task 13 on) runs it from there; no repository keeps its own copy.

Note: Task 10 gave dvv-registry its own copy of the runner (dvv-base's runner plus white-space folding). This task moves the folding into dvv-base, installs the runner, and removes dvv-registry's copy. It also rejects empty argument values (review fix). Two commits, one per repository; the dvv-base commit comes first because dvv-registry finds the runner through the sibling dvv-base.

Windows workflows can take several minutes: run each `cmake --workflow` with a 10-minute timeout or in the background.

- [ ] **Step 3: Move the runner into dvv-base and install it**

Create `dvv-base/cmake/run-compile-fail.cmake`:

```cmake
# Builds one compile-fail target and requires it to fail for the expected reason (spec §9.4).
# Installed with dvv_base, so every dvv repository runs it as
#   ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -DBUILD_DIR=<top-level build tree> -DCONFIG=<config> -DTARGET=<target>
#                    -DPATTERN=<regex> -P ${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/run-compile-fail.cmake
# PATTERN is a regular expression the compiler diagnostics must match. White space in the output is
# folded to single spaces before the match, because MSBuild wraps long diagnostics (static_assert
# messages) between words.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

foreach(variable IN ITEMS BUILD_DIR CONFIG TARGET PATTERN)
    # An empty PATTERN would match any output, so every argument must have a value.
    if(NOT DEFINED ${variable} OR "${${variable}}" STREQUAL "")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "run-compile-fail.cmake needs -D${variable}=<non-empty value>")
    endif()
endforeach()

execute_process(
    COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" --build "${BUILD_DIR}" --target "${TARGET}" --config "${CONFIG}"
    RESULT_VARIABLE status
    OUTPUT_VARIABLE output
    ERROR_VARIABLE output)

if(status EQUAL 0)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "${TARGET} built successfully, but it must fail to compile")
endif()
string(REGEX REPLACE "[ \t\r\n]+" " " folded "${output}")
if(NOT folded MATCHES "${PATTERN}")
    message(FATAL_ERROR
            "${TARGET} failed (${status}) but not for the expected reason: the output does not "
            "match '${PATTERN}'.\n${output}")
endif()
```

### Task 12: dvv-gpu skeleton, Vulkan configuration and result conversion

**Files:**
- Create: repository `dvv-gpu` (sibling of `dvv-base`, local only: branch `m1a`, no remote) with `.clang-format`, `.clang-tidy`, `.gitattributes`, `.gitignore`, `LICENSE`, `CMakePresets.json` (copied from dvv-base; `.clang-tidy` with one changed line), `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `README.md`, `CMakeLists.txt`, `cmake/dvv_gpuConfig.cmake.in`, `include/dvv/gpu/device_id.h`, `src/vk.h`, `src/vk_result.h`, `src/vk_result.cpp`, `src/vma.cpp`, `tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/main.cpp`, `tests/test_device_id.cpp`, `tests/test_vk_result.cpp`, `tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`, `tests/package/main.cpp`, `tests/package/run-package-test.cmake`
- Modify: `rawreel-workspace/repos.txt`, `rawreel-workspace/cmake/dvv-versions.cmake`, `rawreel-workspace/cmake/DvvPresets.json`, `rawreel-workspace/CMakeLists.txt`, `rawreel-workspace/README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (dvv-base): CMake package `dvv_base` 0.1 with `DVV_CMAKE_DIR`, `dvv_set_compiler_options(<target>)`, `DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT`, `DVV_SANITIZE`, option `DVV_BUILD_TESTS`; C++ `dvv::Error`, `dvv::Errc` (`DeviceLost`, `OutOfMemory`, `Unsupported`, `Internal`), `dvv::Result<T>`, `dvv::makeError(Errc, std::string, std::source_location)`, `dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().setDetailed(bool)`, `dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(int)`; the workspace presets and `bootstrap.ps1`. Third-party prefix (Task 4): packages `VulkanHeaders` 1.4.365 (target `Vulkan::Headers`, includes Vulkan-Hpp), `VulkanMemoryAllocator` 3.4.0 (target `GPUOpen::VulkanMemoryAllocator`), `<prefix>/include/VkBootstrap.h` and the source `<prefix>/src/vk-bootstrap/VkBootstrap.cpp`.
- Produces (CMake): target `dvv_gpu` / alias `dvv::gpu` (static, `VK_NO_PROTOTYPES` as a public compile definition, links `dvv::base` and `Vulkan::Headers` publicly; the compiled-in `VkBootstrap.cpp` keeps the target's warning flags but not warnings-as-errors), package `dvv_gpu` 0.1.0 (`SameMinorVersion`, installed to `lib/cmake/dvv_gpu`, `find_dependency(dvv_base 0.1)` and `find_dependency(VulkanHeaders 1.4.365 EXACT CONFIG)`); tests `dvv_gpu.unit` and `dvv_gpu.package` (resource lock `dvv_build_tree`); workspace: `dvv-gpu` in `repos.txt`, `FetchContent_Declare(dvv_gpu ...)`, `FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_GPU` preset variable.
- Produces (C++, public, namespace `dvv::gpu`): `class DeviceId` (`constexpr explicit DeviceId(std::uint32_t) noexcept`, `constexpr std::uint32_t index() const noexcept`, defaulted `==` and `<=>`, `std::hash` and `std::formatter` specialisations that print the index); `using DeviceGeneration = std::uint32_t;` `inline constexpr DeviceGeneration FIRST_GENERATION = 1;`.
- Produces (C++, private, `src/`, namespace `dvv::gpu::detail`): `src/vk.h` (the only way to include Vulkan-Hpp; see its header comment for the configuration), `[[nodiscard]] Errc errcOf(vk::Result) noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] std::unexpected<Error> vkError(vk::Result|VkResult, std::string_view what, std::source_location = current())`, `[[nodiscard]] Result<void> vkCheck(vk::Result|VkResult, std::string_view what, std::source_location = current())`; message form `<what> failed: <result name> (VkResult <number>)`.

Note: the repository stays local until a later publishing task. Do not add a remote, create a GitHub repository or push. Because `repos.txt` will list `dvv-gpu`, the CI of every other repository clones it, so the workspace commit of Step 14 must not be pushed before `dvv-gpu` exists on GitHub (the publishing task does them in that order). dvv-gpu does not depend on dvv-registry (spec §5; GPU capabilities become registry `Capabilities` in a higher layer).

Note (review fixes): `errcOf` maps `eErrorMemoryMapFailed` and `eErrorTooManyObjects` to `OutOfMemory` and `eErrorNotPermitted` to `Unsupported`; the package config restores the consumer's `CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_Vulkan` after finding Vulkan-Headers.

Note (user decision): `VkBootstrap.cpp` is third-party code compiled into `dvv_gpu`, so it is compiled without warnings-as-errors: a per-source `COMPILE_OPTIONS` of `/WX-` (MSVC) or `-Wno-error` (Clang) in `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt`. Its warnings stay visible in the build output but never fail the build; our own sources keep `/W4 /WX` and `-Werror`. During execution these changes were applied as separate fix commits after Task 13 (see Changes during execution); the code blocks below show the final code.

Windows workflows can take several minutes (from this task on they also compile `VkBootstrap.cpp` and VMA): run each `cmake --workflow` with a 10-minute timeout or in the background.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create the repository and its identity**

```powershell
git init -b m1a dvv-gpu
```

In `rawreel-workspace/repos.txt` replace the two comment lines and append the repository, so the whole file reads:

```text
# Repositories that live next to rawreel-workspace, in dependency order. Later milestones add
# dvv-platform (before dvv-gpu, which will use it), dvv-media, dvv-components and rawreel.
dvv-base
dvv-registry
dvv-gpu
```

Run `./rawreel-workspace/bootstrap.ps1 -NoClone`. It copies `user.name`/`user.email` from rawreel-workspace into every listed repository and sets its hooks path (it does not clone; `dvv-gpu` exists already).
Check: `git -C dvv-gpu config user.email` ends with `@users.noreply.github.com`; `git -C dvv-gpu config core.hooksPath` is `../rawreel-workspace/tools/githooks`. `repos.txt` is committed in Step 14, not now.

- [ ] **Step 2: Copy the shared files from dvv-base**

```powershell
Copy-Item dvv-base/.clang-format, dvv-base/.clang-tidy, dvv-base/.gitattributes, dvv-base/.gitignore, dvv-base/LICENSE, dvv-base/CMakePresets.json dvv-gpu/
```

`CMakePresets.json` includes `../rawreel-workspace/cmake/DvvPresets.json`, so every workflow preset comes from the workspace. Check: `Get-Content dvv-gpu/CMakePresets.json` shows exactly

```json
{
  "version": 6,
  "include": [ "../rawreel-workspace/cmake/DvvPresets.json" ]
}
```

dvv-gpu keeps private headers in `src/` next to the sources, so clang-tidy must also check them. In `dvv-gpu/.clang-tidy` replace the line

```yaml
HeaderFilterRegex: '.*/include/dvv/.*'
```

with

```yaml
# Private headers live in src/ next to the sources.
HeaderFilterRegex: '.*/(include/dvv|src)/.*'
```

```yaml
on:

  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `dvv-gpu/README.md`**

````markdown
# dvv-gpu

Headless Vulkan 1.4 GPU core of the dvv stack used by rawreel. C++23; depends on
[dvv-base](https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-base) and on the pinned Vulkan-Headers,
VulkanMemoryAllocator and vk-bootstrap from the rawreel-workspace third-party prefix. The Vulkan
loader is loaded at run time; nothing links it.

Build it through [rawreel-workspace](https://github.com/BarabashkaD/rawreel-workspace), which
provides the presets, the third-party dependencies and the CI workflow:

```sh
cmake --workflow --preset vs2026     # Linux: ninja-clang
```

Licence: MIT.
````

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt`**

Vulkan comes only from the pinned packages in the third-party prefix: `CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_Vulkan` keeps CMake's `FindVulkan` (and an installed Vulkan SDK reachable through `VULKAN_SDK`) out. `VkBootstrap.cpp` is compiled into `dvv_gpu` because the prebuilt vk-bootstrap library cannot be linked into an MSVC Debug build (different runtime library, LNK2038); being third-party code, it is compiled without warnings-as-errors (`set_source_files_properties(... COMPILE_OPTIONS ...)`). `VK_NO_PROTOTYPES` is public: no consumer links the Vulkan loader.

```cmake
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(dvv_gpu VERSION 0.1.0 DESCRIPTION "dvv GPU core" LANGUAGES CXX)

option(DVV_BUILD_TESTS "Build the dvv tests" ${PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL})

# dvv-base: inside the workspace build cmake/dvv-versions.cmake has declared it first, so this
# declaration is ignored there (spec §3.1). Stand-alone builds take it from the sibling folder
# (FETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_BASE in the workspace presets), from an installed package or from Git.
include(FetchContent)
FetchContent_Declare(dvv_base
    GIT_REPOSITORY "https://github.com/BarabashkaD/dvv-base.git"
    GIT_TAG main
    FIND_PACKAGE_ARGS 0.1)
# A stand-alone build tests only this repository (the normal variable hides the cache entry from
# dvv_base's option()); the workspace build tests every repository.
if(PROJECT_IS_TOP_LEVEL)
    set(DVV_BUILD_TESTS OFF)
endif()
FetchContent_MakeAvailable(dvv_base)
unset(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
if(NOT DVV_CMAKE_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_base did not define DVV_CMAKE_DIR")
endif()
include("${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvCompilerOptions.cmake")
include(GNUInstallDirs)
include(CMakePackageConfigHelpers)

# Vulkan comes only from the pinned packages in the third-party prefix, never from CMake's FindVulkan
# module, which would pick up an installed Vulkan SDK through VULKAN_SDK.
set(CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_Vulkan ON)
find_package(VulkanHeaders 1.4.365 EXACT CONFIG REQUIRED)
find_package(VulkanMemoryAllocator 3.4.0 CONFIG REQUIRED)
# vk-bootstrap is compiled into dvv_gpu from the source the superbuild installs: its prebuilt
# Release library cannot be linked into MSVC Debug builds (different runtime library).
find_file(DVV_VK_BOOTSTRAP_SOURCE VkBootstrap.cpp
    PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} PATH_SUFFIXES src/vk-bootstrap NO_DEFAULT_PATH REQUIRED)
find_path(DVV_VK_BOOTSTRAP_INCLUDE_DIR VkBootstrap.h
    PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} PATH_SUFFIXES include NO_DEFAULT_PATH REQUIRED)

add_library(dvv_gpu STATIC
    src/vk_result.cpp
    src/vma.cpp
    "${DVV_VK_BOOTSTRAP_SOURCE}")
add_library(dvv::gpu ALIAS dvv_gpu)
# VkBootstrap.cpp is third-party code: its warnings stay visible but never fail our build; our own
# warning rules apply to our code only. Source options follow the target's /WX or -Werror.
set_source_files_properties("${DVV_VK_BOOTSTRAP_SOURCE}" PROPERTIES
    COMPILE_OPTIONS "$<IF:$<CXX_COMPILER_ID:MSVC>,/WX-,-Wno-error>")
set_target_properties(dvv_gpu PROPERTIES EXPORT_NAME gpu)
target_include_directories(dvv_gpu PUBLIC
    $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>
    $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}>)
target_include_directories(dvv_gpu SYSTEM PRIVATE "${DVV_VK_BOOTSTRAP_INCLUDE_DIR}")
# Vulkan's C headers are part of the interface, always without prototypes: nothing links the
# Vulkan loader, it is loaded at run time. VMA is header-only and compiled into dvv_gpu (src/vma.cpp),
# so installed consumers do not need it.
target_compile_definitions(dvv_gpu PUBLIC VK_NO_PROTOTYPES)
target_link_libraries(dvv_gpu
    PUBLIC dvv::base Vulkan::Headers
    PRIVATE $<BUILD_INTERFACE:GPUOpen::VulkanMemoryAllocator> ${CMAKE_DL_LIBS})
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_gpu)

install(TARGETS dvv_gpu EXPORT dvv_gpuTargets)
install(DIRECTORY include/ DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR})
set(config_dir ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/dvv_gpu)
install(EXPORT dvv_gpuTargets NAMESPACE dvv:: DESTINATION ${config_dir})
configure_package_config_file(cmake/dvv_gpuConfig.cmake.in
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_gpuConfig.cmake INSTALL_DESTINATION ${config_dir})
write_basic_package_version_file(${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_gpuConfigVersion.cmake
    COMPATIBILITY SameMinorVersion)
install(FILES
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_gpuConfig.cmake
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_gpuConfigVersion.cmake
    DESTINATION ${config_dir})

if(DVV_BUILD_TESTS)
    enable_testing()
    add_subdirectory(tests)
endif()
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `dvv-gpu/cmake/dvv_gpuConfig.cmake.in`**

The config file runs in the consumer's scope, so it saves the consumer's `CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_Vulkan`, disables `FindVulkan` only while it finds Vulkan-Headers, and then restores the value.

```cmake
@PACKAGE_INIT@

include(CMakeFindDependencyMacro)
find_dependency(dvv_base 0.1)
# The pinned Vulkan-Headers package only, never CMake's FindVulkan module; the consumer's own
# setting is restored afterwards (this file runs in the consumer's scope).
set(_dvv_gpu_disable_vulkan "${CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_Vulkan}")
set(CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_Vulkan ON)
find_dependency(VulkanHeaders 1.4.365 EXACT CONFIG)
set(CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_Vulkan "${_dvv_gpu_disable_vulkan}")
unset(_dvv_gpu_disable_vulkan)

include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/dvv_gpuTargets.cmake")

check_required_components(dvv_gpu)
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the failing tests**

`dvv-gpu/tests/main.cpp`:

```cpp
#define DOCTEST_CONFIG_IMPLEMENT
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include "dvv/base/leak_check.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().setDetailed(true);
    doctest::Context context(argc, argv);
    const int result = context.run();
    if (context.shouldExit()) {
        return result;
    }
    return dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(result);
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/test_device_id.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <format>
#include <functional>
#include <type_traits>
#include <unordered_set>

#include "dvv/gpu/device_id.h"

using dvv::gpu::DeviceId;

static_assert(!std::is_convertible_v<std::uint32_t, DeviceId>, "construction must be explicit");
static_assert(!std::is_convertible_v<DeviceId, std::uint32_t>);
static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<DeviceId>);
static_assert(DeviceId(2).index() == 2);
static_assert(DeviceId(1) == DeviceId(1));
static_assert(DeviceId(1) < DeviceId(2));
static_assert(dvv::gpu::FIRST_GENERATION == 1);

TEST_CASE("a DeviceId compares, hashes and formats as its index") {
    const DeviceId first(0);
    const DeviceId second(1);
    CHECK(first != second);
    CHECK(first < second);
    CHECK(std::format("{}", second) == "1");
    CHECK(std::format("[{:>3}]", second) == "[  1]");
    CHECK(std::hash<DeviceId>{}(second) == std::hash<std::uint32_t>{}(1));

    const std::unordered_set<DeviceId> ids{first, second, DeviceId(1)};
    CHECK(ids.size() == 2);
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/test_vk_result.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "vk_result.h"

using dvv::Errc;
using dvv::gpu::detail::errcOf;
using dvv::gpu::detail::vkCheck;
using dvv::gpu::detail::vkError;

TEST_CASE("Vulkan results map to dvv error codes") {
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorDeviceLost) == Errc::DeviceLost);

    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorOutOfHostMemory) == Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorOutOfDeviceMemory) == Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorOutOfPoolMemory) == Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorFragmentedPool) == Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorFragmentation) == Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorMemoryMapFailed) == Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorTooManyObjects) == Errc::OutOfMemory);

    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorInitializationFailed) == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorIncompatibleDriver) == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorLayerNotPresent) == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorExtensionNotPresent) == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorFeatureNotPresent) == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorFormatNotSupported) == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorImageUsageNotSupportedKHR) == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorNotPermitted) == Errc::Unsupported);

    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorUnknown) == Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eErrorInvalidExternalHandle) == Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eTimeout) == Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(errcOf(vk::Result::eNotReady) == Errc::Internal);
}

TEST_CASE("a Vulkan error names the call and the result") {
    const auto error = vkError(vk::Result::eErrorDeviceLost, "vkQueueSubmit2").error();
    CHECK(error.code == Errc::DeviceLost);
    CHECK(error.message == "vkQueueSubmit2 failed: ErrorDeviceLost (VkResult -4)");
    CHECK(std::string(error.where.file_name()).find("test_vk_result.cpp") != std::string::npos);

    const auto c_error = vkError(VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_DEVICE_MEMORY, "vmaCreateBuffer").error();
    CHECK(c_error.code == Errc::OutOfMemory);
    CHECK(c_error.message == "vmaCreateBuffer failed: ErrorOutOfDeviceMemory (VkResult -2)");
}

TEST_CASE("vkCheck passes success and converts everything else") {
    CHECK(vkCheck(vk::Result::eSuccess, "vkDeviceWaitIdle").has_value());
    CHECK(vkCheck(VK_SUCCESS, "vmaFlushAllocation").has_value());

    const auto timeout = vkCheck(vk::Result::eTimeout, "vkWaitSemaphores");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(timeout.has_value());
    CHECK(timeout.error().code == Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(timeout.error().message == "vkWaitSemaphores failed: Timeout (VkResult 2)");

    const auto layer = vkCheck(VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT, "vkCreateInstance");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(layer.has_value());
    CHECK(layer.error().code == Errc::Unsupported);
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (the unit tests include private headers from `src/`):

```cmake
find_package(doctest 2.5 REQUIRED CONFIG)

add_executable(dvv_gpu_tests
    main.cpp
    test_device_id.cpp
    test_vk_result.cpp)
target_link_libraries(dvv_gpu_tests PRIVATE dvv::gpu doctest::doctest)
# The unit tests also check private parts (src/), e.g. the Vulkan result conversion.
target_include_directories(dvv_gpu_tests PRIVATE ${dvv_gpu_SOURCE_DIR}/src)
# doctest otherwise only forward-declares std::ostream, which breaks printing std types.
target_compile_definitions(dvv_gpu_tests PRIVATE DOCTEST_CONFIG_USE_STD_HEADERS)
dvv_set_compiler_options(dvv_gpu_tests)
add_test(NAME dvv_gpu.unit COMMAND dvv_gpu_tests)
# The timeout turns a hang into a failure long before the CI job limit.
set_tests_properties(dvv_gpu.unit PROPERTIES
    TIMEOUT 300
    ENVIRONMENT DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail
    ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "${DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT}")

# Package test (spec §3.1): install, then build a consumer with find_package(dvv_gpu) and another
# with dvv_gpu in-tree. The whole build tree is installed, so the prefix also holds dvv_base when it
# is a subproject here. Skipped for sanitizer builds, whose consumers would need the same runtime
# flags.
if(NOT DVV_SANITIZE)
    string(REPLACE ";" "," deps_prefix_path "${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}")
    add_test(NAME dvv_gpu.package
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                     -DBUILD_DIR=${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}
                     -DCONFIG=$<CONFIG>
                     -DPREFIX=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/package-prefix
                     -DSOURCE_DIR=${dvv_gpu_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DBASE_SOURCE_DIR=${dvv_base_SOURCE_DIR}
                     -DCONSUMER_SOURCE=${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/package
                     -DCONSUMER_BUILD=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/package-consumer
                     -DGENERATOR=${CMAKE_GENERATOR}
                     -DPLATFORM=${CMAKE_GENERATOR_PLATFORM}
                     -DCXX_COMPILER=${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}
                     -DDEPS_PREFIX_PATH=${deps_prefix_path}
                     -P ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/package/run-package-test.cmake)
    # Package and compile-fail tests of every dvv repository drive the same build tree in the
    # workspace build, so they share one lock name.
    set_tests_properties(dvv_gpu.package PROPERTIES RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree)
endif()
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create the package test**

It installs the whole top-level build tree (`CMAKE_BINARY_DIR`, so the prefix also holds `dvv_base` when it is a subproject, as in the workspace build), then builds a consumer from the installed package and in-tree. The consumer only includes a public header; it proves that the installed package finds its Vulkan-Headers dependency without CMake's `FindVulkan`.

`dvv-gpu/tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`:

```cmake
# Consumer of dvv_gpu; built by run-package-test.cmake. With DVV_GPU_SOURCE_DIR it takes dvv_gpu
# in-tree through FetchContent (as the workspace build does), otherwise the installed package.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(dvv_gpu_package_consumer LANGUAGES CXX)

if(DVV_GPU_SOURCE_DIR)
    include(FetchContent)
    FetchContent_Declare(dvv_gpu SOURCE_DIR "${DVV_GPU_SOURCE_DIR}")
    FetchContent_MakeAvailable(dvv_gpu)
else()
    find_package(dvv_gpu 0.1 REQUIRED)
endif()
if(NOT DVV_CMAKE_DIR)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_base did not define DVV_CMAKE_DIR")
endif()
include("${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/DvvCompilerOptions.cmake")

add_executable(consumer main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(consumer PRIVATE dvv::gpu)
dvv_set_compiler_options(consumer)
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/package/main.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <cstdio>
#include <format>

#include "dvv/gpu/device_id.h"

int main() {
    constexpr dvv::gpu::DeviceId ID(3);
    if (std::format("{}", ID) != "3") {
        return 1;
    }
    std::puts("dvv_gpu package works");
    return 0;
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/package/run-package-test.cmake`:

```cmake
# Installs the build tree into a scratch prefix, then configures, builds and runs the consumer twice:
# against the installed package and with dvv_gpu in-tree (FetchContent of the source directory,
# dvv_base from its source directory too).
# Called by the dvv_gpu.package test with -DBUILD_DIR, -DCONFIG, -DPREFIX, -DSOURCE_DIR,
# -DBASE_SOURCE_DIR, -DCONSUMER_SOURCE, -DCONSUMER_BUILD, -DGENERATOR, -DPLATFORM, -DCXX_COMPILER and
# -DDEPS_PREFIX_PATH.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

function(run)
    execute_process(COMMAND ${ARGV} RESULT_VARIABLE status)
    if(NOT status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "failed (${status}): ${ARGV}")
    endif()
endfunction()

# consumer(<build dir> <prefix path> [<extra configure arguments>...])
function(consumer build_dir prefix_path)
    # A list value cannot pass through a command line intact; an initial-cache file keeps it whole.
    set(initial_cache "${build_dir}-cache.cmake")
    file(WRITE "${initial_cache}" "set(CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH \"${prefix_path}\" CACHE STRING \"\")\n")
    set(configure "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -C "${initial_cache}" -S "${CONSUMER_SOURCE}" -B "${build_dir}"
        -G "${GENERATOR}" "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER=${CXX_COMPILER}" "-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=${CONFIG}"
        ${ARGN})
    if(PLATFORM)
        list(APPEND configure -A "${PLATFORM}")
    endif()
    run(${configure})
    run("${CMAKE_COMMAND}" --build "${build_dir}" --config "${CONFIG}")

    file(GLOB_RECURSE executable "${build_dir}/consumer" "${build_dir}/consumer.exe")
    list(FILTER executable EXCLUDE REGEX "CMakeFiles")
    if(NOT executable)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "consumer executable not found in ${build_dir}")
    endif()
    list(GET executable 0 executable)
    run("${executable}")
endfunction()

string(REPLACE "," ";" deps_prefix_path "${DEPS_PREFIX_PATH}")
file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${PREFIX}" "${CONSUMER_BUILD}" "${CONSUMER_BUILD}-in-tree")
run("${CMAKE_COMMAND}" --install "${BUILD_DIR}" --config "${CONFIG}" --prefix "${PREFIX}")
consumer("${CONSUMER_BUILD}" "${PREFIX};${deps_prefix_path}")
consumer("${CONSUMER_BUILD}-in-tree" "${deps_prefix_path}"
         "-DDVV_GPU_SOURCE_DIR=${SOURCE_DIR}"
         "-DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DVV_BASE=${BASE_SOURCE_DIR}"
         -DFETCHCONTENT_TRY_FIND_PACKAGE_MODE=NEVER)
```

Run: `Push-Location dvv-gpu; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026; Pop-Location`
Expected: FAIL at configure with `CMake Error ... Cannot find source file: src/vk_result.cpp` (the library sources do not exist yet).

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement**

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/device_id.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <compare>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <format>
#include <functional>

namespace dvv::gpu {

// A physical device: its index in the GpuRegistry's enumeration (spec §5.2). A strong type, so it
// cannot be mixed up with other integers.
class DeviceId {
public:
    constexpr explicit DeviceId(std::uint32_t index) noexcept : index_(index) {}

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint32_t index() const noexcept { return index_; }

    friend constexpr bool operator==(DeviceId, DeviceId) noexcept = default;
    friend constexpr std::strong_ordering operator<=>(DeviceId, DeviceId) noexcept = default;

private:
    std::uint32_t index_;
};

// Device generation (spec §5.2): 1 for the first device of a context; a device-lost recovery
// recreates the device with the next generation. GPU resources record their generation.
using DeviceGeneration = std::uint32_t;
inline constexpr DeviceGeneration FIRST_GENERATION = 1;

}  // namespace dvv::gpu

template <>
struct std::hash<dvv::gpu::DeviceId> {
    std::size_t operator()(dvv::gpu::DeviceId id) const noexcept {
        return std::hash<std::uint32_t>{}(id.index());
    }
};

// Formats as the index, e.g. "0".
template <>
struct std::formatter<dvv::gpu::DeviceId> : std::formatter<std::uint32_t> {
    auto format(dvv::gpu::DeviceId id, std::format_context& context) const {
        return std::formatter<std::uint32_t>::format(id.index(), context);
    }
};
```

`dvv-gpu/src/vk.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The one place that configures Vulkan-Hpp and VMA. Every dvv-gpu source includes Vulkan through
// this header, so the configuration is identical in every translation unit:
// - VK_NO_PROTOTYPES (set by the build for every user of dvv_gpu): nothing links the Vulkan
//   loader; it is loaded at run time.
// - Dynamic dispatch with explicit dispatchers: every instance and device has its own
//   vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic, passed to every call. No default dispatcher storage is
//   defined, so a call that forgets its dispatcher fails to link.
// - No exceptions: calls return results, which are converted to dvv::Error (vk_result.h).
//   VULKAN_HPP_ASSERT_ON_RESULT is a no-op; by default Debug builds would abort on recoverable
//   errors such as VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_DEVICE_MEMORY.
// - No struct constructors: structures are filled with designated initialisers.
// - VMA fetches every Vulkan function through vkGetInstanceProcAddr / vkGetDeviceProcAddr.

#if defined(VULKAN_HPP)
#error "vulkan.hpp was included before vk.h; include Vulkan only through vk.h"
#endif
#if !defined(VK_NO_PROTOTYPES)
#error "dvv_gpu is built with VK_NO_PROTOTYPES"
#endif

#define VULKAN_HPP_DISPATCH_LOADER_DYNAMIC 1
#define VULKAN_HPP_NO_EXCEPTIONS
#define VULKAN_HPP_NO_STRUCT_CONSTRUCTORS
#define VULKAN_HPP_ASSERT_ON_RESULT(condition) static_cast<void>(0)
#define VMA_STATIC_VULKAN_FUNCTIONS 0
#define VMA_DYNAMIC_VULKAN_FUNCTIONS 1

#include <vulkan/vulkan.hpp>
```

`dvv-gpu/src/vk_result.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Vulkan results become dvv::Error here and nowhere else (spec §11).

#include <expected>
#include <source_location>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "vk.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

// VK_ERROR_DEVICE_LOST -> DeviceLost; out of host/device/pool memory, fragmentation, a failed map
// and too many objects -> OutOfMemory; a missing driver, layer, extension, feature or format and a
// refused permission -> Unsupported; everything else (including success codes that a caller
// treats as a failure) -> Internal.
[[nodiscard]] Errc errcOf(vk::Result result) noexcept;

// Errc from errcOf(), message "<what> failed: <result name> (VkResult <number>)".
[[nodiscard]] std::unexpected<Error> vkError(
    vk::Result result, std::string_view what,
    std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());
// For the C results of VMA and vk-bootstrap.
[[nodiscard]] std::unexpected<Error> vkError(
    VkResult result, std::string_view what,
    std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());

// Success -> {}, anything else -> vkError().
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> vkCheck(vk::Result result, std::string_view what,
                                   std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> vkCheck(VkResult result, std::string_view what,
                                   std::source_location where = std::source_location::current());

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/vk_result.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "vk_result.h"

#include <format>
#include <string>

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

Errc errcOf(vk::Result result) noexcept {
    switch (result) {
        case vk::Result::eErrorDeviceLost: return Errc::DeviceLost;
        case vk::Result::eErrorOutOfHostMemory:
        case vk::Result::eErrorOutOfDeviceMemory:
        case vk::Result::eErrorOutOfPoolMemory:
        case vk::Result::eErrorFragmentedPool:
        case vk::Result::eErrorMemoryMapFailed:
        case vk::Result::eErrorTooManyObjects:
        case vk::Result::eErrorFragmentation: return Errc::OutOfMemory;
        case vk::Result::eErrorInitializationFailed:
        case vk::Result::eErrorIncompatibleDriver:
        case vk::Result::eErrorLayerNotPresent:
        case vk::Result::eErrorExtensionNotPresent:
        case vk::Result::eErrorFeatureNotPresent:
        case vk::Result::eErrorFormatNotSupported:
        case vk::Result::eErrorNotPermitted:
        case vk::Result::eErrorImageUsageNotSupportedKHR: return Errc::Unsupported;
        default: return Errc::Internal;
    }
}

std::unexpected<Error> vkError(vk::Result result, std::string_view what,
                               std::source_location where) {
    return makeError(errcOf(result),
                     std::format("{} failed: {} (VkResult {})", what, vk::to_string(result),
                                 static_cast<int>(result)),
                     where);
}

std::unexpected<Error> vkError(VkResult result, std::string_view what, std::source_location where) {
    return vkError(static_cast<vk::Result>(result), what, where);
}

Result<void> vkCheck(vk::Result result, std::string_view what, std::source_location where) {
    if (result == vk::Result::eSuccess) {
        return {};
    }
    return vkError(result, what, where);
}

Result<void> vkCheck(VkResult result, std::string_view what, std::source_location where) {
    return vkCheck(static_cast<vk::Result>(result), what, where);
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```
