### Task 13: GpuRegistry with the Vulkan instance and validation settings

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/gpu_registry.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/src/instance.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/instance.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/src/loader.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/loader.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/test_loader.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/test_validation.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`
- Modify: `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt` (add the three sources), `dvv-gpu/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (add the tests and the compile-fail cases), `dvv-gpu/tests/package/main.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 12 (`vk.h` and `vk_result.h`: `errcOf`, `vkCheck`), dvv-base `dvv::getEnvironment(std::string_view) -> std::optional<std::string>` (`dvv/base/environment.h`), `DVV_TRY`, `DVV_TRY_VOID`, `DVV_LOG`, `DVV_LOG_INFO`, `DVV_LOG_WARN`, `dvv::Logger::instance()`, `Logger::enabled(LogLevel) const noexcept`, `Logger::write(LogLevel, std::string_view, std::source_location = std::source_location::current())` (`dvv/base/log.h`; the default argument attributes every Vulkan message to `onMessage` in instance.cpp), `dvv::Managed<T>`, `dvv::CreationKey<T>`, `dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<T>()`, `dvv::ManagedType`, the shared compile-fail runner (Task 12a), vk-bootstrap's `VkBootstrap.h` (Task 12 compiles its source).
- Produces (C++, public, `dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu`; Task 14 extends the class with devices): `enum class ValidationMode : std::uint8_t { Off, On, Sync }`; `std::string_view validationModeName(ValidationMode) noexcept` (`"off"`, `"on"`, `"sync"`); `std::string formatVulkanVersion(std::uint32_t)` (`"major.minor.patch"`); `struct GpuRegistryOptions { std::string applicationName = "dvv"; std::optional<ValidationMode> validation; std::optional<bool> validationRequired; }`; `final class GpuRegistry : Managed<GpuRegistry>, std::enable_shared_from_this<GpuRegistry>` with `static Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry>> create(const GpuRegistryOptions& = {})`, constructor `GpuRegistry(CreationKey<GpuRegistry>, std::unique_ptr<detail::InstanceCore>) noexcept`, `ValidationMode validation() const noexcept`, `std::uint64_t validationMessageCount() const noexcept`, `std::uint32_t loaderVersion() const noexcept`.
- Produces (C++, private, namespace `dvv::gpu::detail`): `loadVulkanLoader(const std::string& library_name) -> Result<PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr>` and `vulkanLoader()` (`src/loader.h`); `ValidationRequest`, `parseValidationMode`, `resolveValidation`, `effectiveValidation`, `countsAsFailure`, `debugMessageLevel`, `formatDebugMessage`, `[[nodiscard]] std::mutex& vkBootstrapMutex()` (every vk-bootstrap call, including the physical-device and device builders of later tasks, must hold it), `[[nodiscard]] bool retryWithoutValidation(VkResult, ValidationMode, bool required) noexcept` and `class InstanceCore` (public default constructor, used by `create()` and white-box tests) with `instance()`, `dispatch()`, `validation()`, `debugUtils()`, `loaderVersion()`, `validationMessageCount()`, `onMessage(...)` (`src/instance.h`).
- Behaviour: validation comes from `GpuRegistryOptions::validation`, else the environment variable `DVV_VK_VALIDATION` (`off`, `on`, `sync`), else `On` in Debug builds and `Off` in Release builds; `DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED` (`0` or `1`; or `validationRequired`) turns a missing layer into `Errc::Unsupported`; otherwise a missing layer, or one older than 1.4, turns validation off with a log line. A layer that is listed but whose library fails to load (`vkCreateInstance` returns `VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT` from the Linux loader 1.3.275 or `VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY` from the Windows loader 1.4.341) turns validation off with a warning, resets the validation-message count to zero (the loader reports the failed library load at ERROR through the instance-creation messenger; that is not a validation finding) and the instance is built once more without it, unless validation is required (a real out-of-memory failure then fails again in the retry). `Sync` adds synchronization validation (`validate_sync`) through `VK_EXT_layer_settings`. The debug messenger counts every message of severity error and every warning of type validation or performance (general warnings come from the loader and depend on the machine); every message goes to the dvv `Logger`. A missing loader is `Errc::Unsupported` with a message that starts `Vulkan loader not found`. The instance asks for API 1.4 and accepts a loader of at least 1.3 (Ubuntu 24.04 ships 1.3.275).

Note: the loader library is kept loaded for the whole process (never unloaded), because vk-bootstrap keeps the first `vkGetInstanceProcAddr` it is given in process-wide tables. vk-bootstrap also keeps the instance-level function pointers of the first instance for the whole process; this is safe only because they are loader trampolines. Its tables are not synchronised, so instances are built one at a time under `vkBootstrapMutex()` (declared in `src/instance.h`, so later vk-bootstrap users take the same lock). The registry's destructor destroys the messenger and the instance through the registry's own dispatcher, not through vk-bootstrap. No test in this task creates a real instance (that needs a Vulkan loader); the package consumer does when one is installed, and Task 15 adds the GPU test harness.

Note (review fixes): a loader-free unit test drives `InstanceCore::onMessage` and checks the counter; a listed validation layer that fails to load is dropped and the instance rebuilt once (`retryWithoutValidation`); `instance.cpp` documents two vk-bootstrap 1.4.365 defects: it drops the application and engine names (inverted `empty()` check, fixed upstream later; deferred to the next vk-bootstrap update), and it leaks the instance when creating the messenger fails (practically unreachable here because `debug_utils_` is checked first). Task 15 must add a positive control: one GPU test that, with validation on, deliberately triggers a validation message (for example a `vkCmdFillBuffer` write-after-write hazard under `sync`) and requires `validationMessageCount()` above zero.

Note (fixes after the Task 13 review): when the instance is rebuilt without a validation layer that failed to load, `InstanceCore::create` resets the validation-message count, so the loader's ERROR messages about the failed library load are not counted as validation findings (Task 15 adds a GPU test with a layer manifest whose library does not exist); `retryWithoutValidation` also retries on `VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY`, which the Windows loader 1.4.341 returns for such a layer (unit test `a validation layer that fails to load is dropped unless validation is required` checks both results; the case count stays 16); the comments in `instance.h` and `instance.cpp` say so. During execution these changes were applied as separate fix commits after Task 13 (see Changes during execution); the code blocks below show the final code.

Note: loading the default `vulkan-1.dll` through the default DLL search order (DLL planting) is deferred to a later docs task.

Windows workflows can take several minutes: run each `cmake --workflow` with a 10-minute timeout or in the background.

`dvv-gpu/tests/test_loader.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "loader.h"

TEST_CASE("a missing Vulkan loader is reported as unsupported") {
    const auto loader = dvv::gpu::detail::loadVulkanLoader("dvv-test-no-such-vulkan-loader");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(loader.has_value());
    CHECK(loader.error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(loader.error().message == "Vulkan loader not found ('dvv-test-no-such-vulkan-loader')");
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/test_validation.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <optional>
#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/log.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "instance.h"

using dvv::Errc;
using dvv::LogLevel;
using dvv::gpu::GpuRegistryOptions;
using dvv::gpu::ValidationMode;
using dvv::gpu::detail::countsAsFailure;
using dvv::gpu::detail::debugMessageLevel;
using dvv::gpu::detail::effectiveValidation;
using dvv::gpu::detail::formatDebugMessage;
using dvv::gpu::detail::parseValidationMode;
using dvv::gpu::detail::resolveValidation;
using dvv::gpu::detail::retryWithoutValidation;
using dvv::gpu::detail::ValidationRequest;
using Severity = vk::DebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT;
using Type = vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagBitsEXT;
using Types = vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT;

namespace {

const std::optional<std::string> UNSET;

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("validation modes parse from off, on and sync") {
    CHECK(parseValidationMode("off").value() == ValidationMode::Off);
    CHECK(parseValidationMode("on").value() == ValidationMode::On);
    CHECK(parseValidationMode("sync").value() == ValidationMode::Sync);
    for (const char* text : {"", "ON", "1", "sync "}) {
        const auto mode = parseValidationMode(text);
        REQUIRE_FALSE(mode.has_value());
        CHECK(mode.error().code == Errc::Format);
    }
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::validationModeName(ValidationMode::Sync) == "sync");
}

TEST_CASE("validation defaults to on in Debug builds and off in Release builds") {
    const auto debug = resolveValidation({}, UNSET, UNSET, true).value();
    CHECK(debug.mode == ValidationMode::On);
    CHECK_FALSE(debug.required);
    CHECK_FALSE(debug.explicitlyRequested);
    CHECK(resolveValidation({}, UNSET, UNSET, false).value().mode == ValidationMode::Off);
    // An empty variable counts as unset.
    CHECK(resolveValidation({}, std::string(), std::string(), false).value().mode ==
          ValidationMode::Off);
}

TEST_CASE("the environment sets validation unless the options do") {
    const auto from_environment = resolveValidation({}, "sync", "1", false).value();
    CHECK(from_environment.mode == ValidationMode::Sync);
    CHECK(from_environment.required);
    CHECK(from_environment.explicitlyRequested);
    CHECK_FALSE(resolveValidation({}, "off", "0", true).value().required);

    const GpuRegistryOptions options{.validation = ValidationMode::Off,
                                     .validationRequired = false};
    const auto from_options = resolveValidation(options, "sync", "1", true).value();
    CHECK(from_options.mode == ValidationMode::Off);
    CHECK_FALSE(from_options.required);
    CHECK(from_options.explicitlyRequested);
}

TEST_CASE("bad validation variables are format errors") {
    const auto mode = resolveValidation({}, "full", UNSET, true);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(mode.has_value());
    CHECK(mode.error().code == Errc::Format);
    CHECK(mode.error().message == "DVV_VK_VALIDATION must be off, on or sync, got 'full'");

    const auto required = resolveValidation({}, UNSET, "yes", true);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(required.has_value());
    CHECK(required.error().code == Errc::Format);
    CHECK(required.error().message == "DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED must be 0 or 1, got 'yes'");

    // Variables that the options override are not read at all.
    CHECK(resolveValidation({.validation = ValidationMode::On, .validationRequired = true}, "full",
                            "yes", true)
              .has_value());
}

TEST_CASE("a missing validation layer turns validation off unless it is required") {
    const ValidationRequest sync{.mode = ValidationMode::Sync};
    CHECK(effectiveValidation(sync, true).value() == ValidationMode::Sync);
    CHECK(effectiveValidation(sync, false).value() == ValidationMode::Off);

    const ValidationRequest required{.mode = ValidationMode::On, .required = true};
    CHECK(effectiveValidation(required, true).value() == ValidationMode::On);
    const auto missing = effectiveValidation(required, false);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(missing.has_value());
    CHECK(missing.error().code == Errc::Unsupported);
    CHECK(missing.error().message ==
          "Vulkan validation 'on' is required, but VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation 1.4 or newer is not "
          "installed");

    // Requiring validation while switching it off asks for nothing.
    const ValidationRequest off{.mode = ValidationMode::Off, .required = true};
    CHECK(effectiveValidation(off, false).value() == ValidationMode::Off);
}

TEST_CASE("errors and validation or performance warnings count as failures") {
    const Types general = Type::eGeneral;
    const Types validation = Type::eValidation;
    const Types performance = Type::ePerformance;

    CHECK(countsAsFailure(Severity::eError, general));
    CHECK(countsAsFailure(Severity::eError, validation));
    CHECK(countsAsFailure(Severity::eWarning, validation));
    CHECK(countsAsFailure(Severity::eWarning, performance));
    CHECK(countsAsFailure(Severity::eWarning, general | validation));

    CHECK_FALSE(countsAsFailure(Severity::eWarning, general));
    CHECK_FALSE(countsAsFailure(Severity::eInfo, validation));
    CHECK_FALSE(countsAsFailure(Severity::eVerbose, performance));
}

TEST_CASE("debug messages are logged at their severity, uncounted ones one level lower") {
    CHECK(debugMessageLevel(Severity::eError, true) == LogLevel::Error);
    CHECK(debugMessageLevel(Severity::eWarning, true) == LogLevel::Warn);
    CHECK(debugMessageLevel(Severity::eWarning, false) == LogLevel::Info);
    CHECK(debugMessageLevel(Severity::eInfo, false) == LogLevel::Debug);
    CHECK(debugMessageLevel(Severity::eVerbose, false) == LogLevel::Trace);
}

TEST_CASE("debug messages name their types, severity and id") {
    CHECK(formatDebugMessage(Severity::eError, Type::eValidation, "VUID-x", "bad call") ==
          "Vulkan validation error [VUID-x]: bad call");
    CHECK(formatDebugMessage(Severity::eWarning, Type::eGeneral | Type::ePerformance, "", "slow") ==
          "Vulkan general|performance warning: slow");
    CHECK(formatDebugMessage(Severity::eInfo, Types{}, "", "loader") ==
          "Vulkan other info: loader");
    CHECK(formatDebugMessage(Severity::eVerbose, Type::eGeneral, "", "") ==
          "Vulkan general verbose: ");
}

TEST_CASE("Vulkan versions format as major.minor.patch") {
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::formatVulkanVersion(VK_MAKE_API_VERSION(0, 1, 4, 318)) == "1.4.318");
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::formatVulkanVersion(VK_API_VERSION_1_3) == "1.3.0");
}

TEST_CASE("the messenger callback counts by the policy and survives missing strings") {
    dvv::gpu::detail::InstanceCore core;
    const vk::DebugUtilsMessengerCallbackDataEXT data{};  // pMessageIdName and pMessage are null
    core.onMessage(Severity::eWarning, Type::eGeneral, data);
    CHECK(core.validationMessageCount() == 0);
    core.onMessage(Severity::eError, Type::eGeneral, data);
    core.onMessage(Severity::eWarning, Type::eValidation, data);
    CHECK(core.validationMessageCount() == 2);
}

TEST_CASE("a validation layer that fails to load is dropped unless validation is required") {
    CHECK(retryWithoutValidation(VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT, ValidationMode::On, false));
    CHECK(retryWithoutValidation(VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT, ValidationMode::Sync, false));
    CHECK(retryWithoutValidation(VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY, ValidationMode::On, false));
    CHECK_FALSE(retryWithoutValidation(VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY, ValidationMode::On, true));
    CHECK_FALSE(retryWithoutValidation(VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT, ValidationMode::Sync, true));
    CHECK_FALSE(retryWithoutValidation(VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT, ValidationMode::Off, false));
    CHECK_FALSE(retryWithoutValidation(VK_ERROR_INCOMPATIBLE_DRIVER, ValidationMode::On, false));
}
```

```cpp
// Compile-fail cases for dvv-gpu's managed types (spec §9.4). DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE selects one:
//   0  control: a registry created through its factory compiles
//   1  constructing a CreationKey<GpuRegistry> outside the registry
//   2  constructing a GpuRegistry without a key
#include <memory>

#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"

int main() {
    const auto registry = dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry::create();
#if DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 1
    dvv::CreationKey<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry> forged(
        dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry>());
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 2
    dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry direct(std::unique_ptr<dvv::gpu::detail::InstanceCore>{});
#endif
    return registry.has_value() ? 0 : 1;
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/package/main.cpp` (replace the whole file; without a Vulkan loader, for example on CI's Windows runners, `create` reports `Unsupported` and that counts as success; validation stays off because a system's older layer may be installed):

```cpp
#include <cstdio>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"

int main() {
    // Without a Vulkan loader (CI's Windows runners) the registry reports Unsupported; anything
    // else is a failure. Validation stays off: a system's older layer may be installed.
    const auto registry =
        dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry::create({.validation = dvv::gpu::ValidationMode::Off});
    if (!registry && registry.error().code != dvv::Errc::Unsupported) {
        std::printf("%s\n", registry.error().describe().c_str());
        return 1;
    }
    std::puts(registry ? "dvv_gpu package works" : "dvv_gpu package works (no Vulkan loader)");
    return 0;
}
```

```cmake
# Compile-fail tests (spec §9.4), run by dvv-base's shared runner. Case 0 is the control and must
# build; cases 1-2 must fail to compile with the expected diagnostic: a forged registry key and a
# registry constructed without a key.
set(compile_fail_source ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp)
add_executable(dvv_gpu_compile_ok ${compile_fail_source})
target_compile_definitions(dvv_gpu_compile_ok PRIVATE DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE=0)
target_link_libraries(dvv_gpu_compile_ok PRIVATE dvv::gpu)

else()
    set(compile_fail_patterns "private" "no matching constructor")
endif()
foreach(case IN ITEMS 1 2)
    set(target dvv_gpu_compile_fail_${case})
    add_executable(${target} EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL ${compile_fail_source})
    target_compile_definitions(${target} PRIVATE DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE=${case})
    target_link_libraries(${target} PRIVATE dvv::gpu)
    dvv_set_compiler_options(${target})
    math(EXPR pattern_index "${case} - 1")
    list(GET compile_fail_patterns ${pattern_index} pattern)
    # cmake --build needs the top-level tree; in the workspace build dvv-gpu is a subproject.
    add_test(NAME dvv_gpu.compile_fail.${case}
             COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
                     -DBUILD_DIR=${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}
                     -DCONFIG=$<CONFIG>
                     -DTARGET=${target}
                     "-DPATTERN=${pattern}"
                     -P ${DVV_CMAKE_DIR}/run-compile-fail.cmake)
    set_tests_properties(dvv_gpu.compile_fail.${case} PROPERTIES RESOURCE_LOCK dvv_build_tree)
endforeach()
```

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

namespace detail {
class InstanceCore;
}  // namespace detail

// Vulkan validation (VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation). Sync adds synchronization validation.
enum class ValidationMode : std::uint8_t { Off, On, Sync };

[[nodiscard]] std::string_view validationModeName(ValidationMode mode) noexcept;

// "major.minor.patch" of a Vulkan version number (VK_MAKE_API_VERSION encoding; the variant bits
// are ignored).
[[nodiscard]] std::string formatVulkanVersion(std::uint32_t version);

struct GpuRegistryOptions {
    std::string applicationName = "dvv";
    // Unset: the environment variable DVV_VK_VALIDATION (off, on or sync); without it On in Debug
    // builds and Off in Release builds. A requested layer that is not installed turns validation
    // off with a log line, unless validation is required.
    std::optional<ValidationMode> validation = std::nullopt;
    // Unset: DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED (1 or 0, default 0). When true, a requested validation
    // layer that is not installed is an error (Errc::Unsupported); used by CI.
    std::optional<bool> validationRequired = std::nullopt;
};

// The process's Vulkan instance (spec §5.2): loads the Vulkan loader at run time, creates a
// headless instance (Vulkan 1.4 API on a loader of at least 1.3) and routes debug messages to the
// dvv Logger. Its own creation authority; thread-safe.
class GpuRegistry final : public Managed<GpuRegistry>,
                          public std::enable_shared_from_this<GpuRegistry> {
public:
    // Errc::Unsupported "Vulkan loader not found" without a loader; Errc::Unsupported when the
    // loader is older than 1.3 or a required validation layer is missing; Errc::Format for a bad
    // DVV_VK_VALIDATION or DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED value.
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry>> create(
        const GpuRegistryOptions& options = {});

    GpuRegistry(CreationKey<GpuRegistry> key,
                std::unique_ptr<detail::InstanceCore> instance) noexcept;
    ~GpuRegistry();

    // The validation actually enabled (Off when the layer was requested but is not installed).
    [[nodiscard]] ValidationMode validation() const noexcept;
    // Debug messages that count as failures: errors of any type, and validation or performance
    // warnings. Every message is logged; tests require this count to stay zero.
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t validationMessageCount() const noexcept;
    // Version of the Vulkan loader (VK_MAKE_API_VERSION encoding).
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t loaderVersion() const noexcept;

private:
    std::unique_ptr<detail::InstanceCore> instance_;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/loader.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The Vulkan loader library, loaded at run time (nothing links it).

#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "vk.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

// Loads the loader library (the platform's default name when library_name is empty) and returns
// its vkGetInstanceProcAddr. Errc::Unsupported "Vulkan loader not found" when the library or the
// function is missing. A loaded library is never unloaded: vk-bootstrap keeps the first
// vkGetInstanceProcAddr it is given for the rest of the process (see vkBootstrapMutex() in
// instance.h for the rest of its process-wide state).
[[nodiscard]] Result<PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr> loadVulkanLoader(const std::string& library_name);

// The default loader: loaded on first success, then cached. A failure is retried on the next call.
[[nodiscard]] Result<PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr> vulkanLoader();

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/loader.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "loader.h"

#include <format>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

namespace {

// Process infrastructure: the loaded libraries, intentionally never destroyed (see loader.h).
struct LoadedLibraries {
    std::mutex mutex;
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<vk::detail::DynamicLoader>> libraries;
    PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr defaultLoader = nullptr;
};

LoadedLibraries& loadedLibraries() {
    static auto* const LIBRARIES = new LoadedLibraries();
    return *LIBRARIES;
}

std::string describe(const std::string& library_name) {
    return library_name.empty() ? std::string("the default library")
                                : std::format("'{}'", library_name);
}

}  // namespace

Result<PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr> loadVulkanLoader(const std::string& library_name) {
    auto library = std::make_unique<vk::detail::DynamicLoader>(library_name);
    if (!library->success()) {
        return makeError(Errc::Unsupported,
                         std::format("Vulkan loader not found ({})", describe(library_name)));
    }
    const auto get_instance_proc_addr =
        library->getProcAddress<PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr>("vkGetInstanceProcAddr");
    if (get_instance_proc_addr == nullptr) {
        return makeError(Errc::Unsupported,
                         std::format("Vulkan loader not found ({} has no vkGetInstanceProcAddr)",
                                     describe(library_name)));
    }
    auto& loaded = loadedLibraries();
    const std::scoped_lock lock(loaded.mutex);
    loaded.libraries.push_back(std::move(library));
    return get_instance_proc_addr;
}

Result<PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr> vulkanLoader() {
    auto& loaded = loadedLibraries();
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(loaded.mutex);
        if (loaded.defaultLoader != nullptr) {
            return loaded.defaultLoader;
        }
    }
    DVV_TRY(const auto get_instance_proc_addr, loadVulkanLoader({}));
    const std::scoped_lock lock(loaded.mutex);
    if (loaded.defaultLoader == nullptr) {
        loaded.defaultLoader = get_instance_proc_addr;
    }
    return loaded.defaultLoader;
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/instance.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The Vulkan instance behind a GpuRegistry: validation settings, the instance and its dispatcher,
// and the debug messenger that counts and logs messages.

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/log.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "vk.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

inline constexpr char VALIDATION_LAYER[] = "VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation";

// What GpuRegistry::create was asked for, before the layer's presence is known.
struct ValidationRequest {
    ValidationMode mode = ValidationMode::Off;
    bool required = false;
    bool explicitlyRequested = false;  // by the options or DVV_VK_VALIDATION, not by default
};

// "off", "on" or "sync"; Errc::Format otherwise.
[[nodiscard]] Result<ValidationMode> parseValidationMode(std::string_view text);

// The options win; unset options fall back to the environment values (nullopt or empty = unset),
// then to the default (On in Debug builds, Off in Release builds; not required).
[[nodiscard]] Result<ValidationRequest> resolveValidation(
    const GpuRegistryOptions& options, const std::optional<std::string>& environment_mode,
    const std::optional<std::string>& environment_required, bool debug_build);

// The mode to enable: the requested one when the layer is installed (in version 1.4 or newer);
// otherwise Off, or Errc::Unsupported when validation is required.
[[nodiscard]] Result<ValidationMode> effectiveValidation(const ValidationRequest& request,
                                                         bool layer_installed);

// Messenger policy: errors of any type count, and so do validation or performance warnings.
// General warnings come from the loader and depend on the machine, so they do not count.
[[nodiscard]] bool countsAsFailure(vk::DebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT severity,
                                   vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT types) noexcept;

// Counted messages are logged at their severity (Error, Warn); the others one level lower
// (warning -> Info, info -> Debug, verbose -> Trace).
[[nodiscard]] LogLevel debugMessageLevel(vk::DebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT severity,
                                         bool counted) noexcept;

// "Vulkan validation error [VUID-...]: <message>"; types are joined with '|'.
[[nodiscard]] std::string formatDebugMessage(vk::DebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT severity,
                                             vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT types,
                                             std::string_view id_name, std::string_view message);

// vk-bootstrap keeps process-wide state: the first vkGetInstanceProcAddr it is given (loader.h)
// and the instance-level function pointers of the first instance, which stay in use after that
// instance is gone. This is safe only because they are loader trampolines. Its tables are not
// synchronised, so every vk-bootstrap call (instance, physical device or device builders) must
// hold this mutex.
[[nodiscard]] std::mutex& vkBootstrapMutex();

// Whether a failed vkCreateInstance is retried without the validation layer: the layer was listed
// but could not be loaded, and validation is not required. Loaders report that as
// VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT (Linux loader 1.3.275) or VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY (Windows loader
// 1.4.341); a real out-of-memory failure then fails again in the retry.
[[nodiscard]] bool retryWithoutValidation(VkResult result, ValidationMode validation,
                                          bool required) noexcept;

// Implementation part of GpuRegistry (exempt from spec §9.4 registration: owned only by its
// registry, with the same lifetime). Created only by create(); the public default constructor
// serves create() and white-box tests.
class InstanceCore {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<std::unique_ptr<InstanceCore>> create(
        const GpuRegistryOptions& options);

    InstanceCore() = default;
    InstanceCore(const InstanceCore&) = delete;
    InstanceCore& operator=(const InstanceCore&) = delete;
    InstanceCore(InstanceCore&&) = delete;
    InstanceCore& operator=(InstanceCore&&) = delete;
    // Destroys the messenger, then the instance.
    ~InstanceCore();

    [[nodiscard]] vk::Instance instance() const noexcept { return instance_; }
    [[nodiscard]] const vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic& dispatch() const noexcept {
        return dispatch_;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] ValidationMode validation() const noexcept { return validation_; }
    // VK_EXT_debug_utils is enabled: messages are routed and objects can carry debug names.
    [[nodiscard]] bool debugUtils() const noexcept { return debug_utils_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t loaderVersion() const noexcept { return loader_version_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t validationMessageCount() const noexcept {
        return validation_messages_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }

    // The messenger callback: counts the message by the policy above and logs it. Any thread.
    void onMessage(vk::DebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT severity,
                   vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT types,
                   const vk::DebugUtilsMessengerCallbackDataEXT& data) noexcept;

private:
    vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic dispatch_;
    vk::Instance instance_;
    vk::DebugUtilsMessengerEXT messenger_;
    ValidationMode validation_ = ValidationMode::Off;
    bool debug_utils_ = false;
    std::uint32_t loader_version_ = 0;
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> validation_messages_{0};
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/instance.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "instance.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <format>
#include <mutex>
#include <source_location>
#include <string>
#include <utility>

#include <VkBootstrap.h>

#include "dvv/base/environment.h"
#include "loader.h"
#include "vk_result.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

namespace {

#if defined(NDEBUG)
constexpr bool DEBUG_BUILD = false;
#else
constexpr bool DEBUG_BUILD = true;
#endif

using Severity = vk::DebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT;
using Type = vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagBitsEXT;

VKAPI_ATTR VkBool32 VKAPI_CALL onDebugMessage(VkDebugUtilsMessageSeverityFlagBitsEXT severity,
                                              VkDebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT types,
                                              const VkDebugUtilsMessengerCallbackDataEXT* data,
                                              void* user_data) noexcept {
    if (user_data != nullptr && data != nullptr) {
        static_cast<InstanceCore*>(user_data)->onMessage(
            static_cast<Severity>(severity), vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT(types),
            *reinterpret_cast<const vk::DebugUtilsMessengerCallbackDataEXT*>(data));
    }
    return VK_FALSE;  // never abort the Vulkan call
}

// Errc from the Vulkan result when there is one, otherwise Unsupported (vk-bootstrap's own checks:
// versions, layers, extensions).
template <class T>
std::unexpected<Error> vkBootstrapError(
    const vkb::Result<T>& result, std::string_view what,
    std::source_location where = std::source_location::current()) {
    const VkResult vk_result = result.vk_result();
    const Errc code =
        vk_result == VK_SUCCESS ? Errc::Unsupported : errcOf(static_cast<vk::Result>(vk_result));
    std::string message = std::format("{} failed: {}", what, result.error().message());
    if (vk_result != VK_SUCCESS) {
        message += std::format(" ({})", vk::to_string(static_cast<vk::Result>(vk_result)));
    }
    return makeError(code, std::move(message), where);
}

// Builds the instance with vk-bootstrap; the caller holds vkBootstrapMutex().
vkb::Result<vkb::Instance> buildInstance(PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr get_instance_proc_addr,
                                         const GpuRegistryOptions& options, InstanceCore* core,
                                         bool debug_utils, ValidationMode validation) {
    static constexpr VkBool32 ENABLED = VK_TRUE;
    vkb::InstanceBuilder builder(get_instance_proc_addr);
    // The instance asks for Vulkan 1.4 (the devices' API) but accepts a 1.3 loader: Ubuntu 24.04
    // ships loader 1.3.275, and the 1.4 lavapipe device works through it.
    // vk-bootstrap 1.4.365 drops both names (its empty() check is inverted), so the driver sees
    // empty names; they are set for the fixed versions.
    builder.set_app_name(options.applicationName)
        .set_engine_name("dvv")
        .set_minimum_instance_version(1, 3, 0)
        .require_api_version(1, 4, 0)
        .set_headless(true);
    if (debug_utils) {
        builder.set_debug_callback(&onDebugMessage)
            .set_debug_callback_user_data_pointer(core)
            .set_debug_messenger_severity(VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_VERBOSE_BIT_EXT |
                                          VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_INFO_BIT_EXT |
                                          VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_WARNING_BIT_EXT |
                                          VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_SEVERITY_ERROR_BIT_EXT)
            .set_debug_messenger_type(VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_TYPE_GENERAL_BIT_EXT |
                                      VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_TYPE_VALIDATION_BIT_EXT |
                                      VK_DEBUG_UTILS_MESSAGE_TYPE_PERFORMANCE_BIT_EXT);
    }
    if (validation != ValidationMode::Off) {
        builder.enable_validation_layers(true);
    }
    if (validation == ValidationMode::Sync) {
        builder.add_layer_setting({.pLayerName = VALIDATION_LAYER,
                                   .pSettingName = "validate_sync",
                                   .type = VK_LAYER_SETTING_TYPE_BOOL32_EXT,
                                   .valueCount = 1,
                                   .pValues = &ENABLED});
    }
    return builder.build();
}

}  // namespace

std::mutex& vkBootstrapMutex() {
    static std::mutex mutex;
    return mutex;
}

bool retryWithoutValidation(VkResult result, ValidationMode validation, bool required) noexcept {
    return (result == VK_ERROR_LAYER_NOT_PRESENT || result == VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_HOST_MEMORY) &&
           validation != ValidationMode::Off && !required;
}

Result<ValidationMode> parseValidationMode(std::string_view text) {
    if (text == "off") {
        return ValidationMode::Off;
    }
    if (text == "on") {
        return ValidationMode::On;
    }
    if (text == "sync") {
        return ValidationMode::Sync;
    }
    return makeError(Errc::Format,
                     std::format("Vulkan validation mode must be off, on or sync, got '{}'", text));
}

Result<ValidationRequest> resolveValidation(const GpuRegistryOptions& options,
                                            const std::optional<std::string>& environment_mode,
                                            const std::optional<std::string>& environment_required,
                                            bool debug_build) {
    ValidationRequest request{.mode = debug_build ? ValidationMode::On : ValidationMode::Off};
    if (options.validation) {
        request.mode = *options.validation;
        request.explicitlyRequested = true;
    } else if (environment_mode && !environment_mode->empty()) {
        const auto mode = parseValidationMode(*environment_mode);
        if (!mode) {
            return makeError(Errc::Format,
                             std::format("DVV_VK_VALIDATION must be off, on or sync, got '{}'",
                                         *environment_mode));
        }
        request.mode = *mode;
        request.explicitlyRequested = true;
    }
    if (options.validationRequired) {
        request.required = *options.validationRequired;
    } else if (environment_required && !environment_required->empty()) {
        if (*environment_required != "0" && *environment_required != "1") {
            return makeError(Errc::Format,
                             std::format("DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED must be 0 or 1, got '{}'",
                                         *environment_required));
        }
        request.required = *environment_required == "1";
    }
    return request;
}

Result<ValidationMode> effectiveValidation(const ValidationRequest& request, bool layer_installed) {
    if (request.mode == ValidationMode::Off || layer_installed) {
        return request.mode;
    }
    if (request.required) {
        return makeError(
            Errc::Unsupported,
            std::format("Vulkan validation '{}' is required, but {} 1.4 or newer is not installed",
                        validationModeName(request.mode), VALIDATION_LAYER));
    }
    return ValidationMode::Off;
}

bool countsAsFailure(Severity severity, vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT types) noexcept {
    if (severity == Severity::eError) {
        return true;
    }
    const vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT checked = Type::eValidation | Type::ePerformance;
    return severity == Severity::eWarning &&
           (types & checked) != vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT{};
}

LogLevel debugMessageLevel(Severity severity, bool counted) noexcept {
    switch (severity) {
        case Severity::eError: return LogLevel::Error;
        case Severity::eWarning: return counted ? LogLevel::Warn : LogLevel::Info;
        case Severity::eInfo: return LogLevel::Debug;
        default: return LogLevel::Trace;
    }
}

std::string formatDebugMessage(Severity severity, vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT types,
                               std::string_view id_name, std::string_view message) {
    std::string type_names;
    const auto add = [&type_names](std::string_view name) {
        type_names += type_names.empty() ? "" : "|";
        type_names += name;
    };
    if (types & Type::eGeneral) {
        add("general");
    }
    if (types & Type::eValidation) {
        add("validation");
    }
    if (types & Type::ePerformance) {
        add("performance");
    }
    if (types & Type::eDeviceAddressBinding) {
        add("device-address-binding");
    }
    if (type_names.empty()) {
        add("other");
    }
    std::string_view severity_name = "verbose";
    if (severity == Severity::eError) {
        severity_name = "error";
    } else if (severity == Severity::eWarning) {
        severity_name = "warning";
    } else if (severity == Severity::eInfo) {
        severity_name = "info";
    }
    if (id_name.empty()) {
        return std::format("Vulkan {} {}: {}", type_names, severity_name, message);
    }
    return std::format("Vulkan {} {} [{}]: {}", type_names, severity_name, id_name, message);
}

Result<std::unique_ptr<InstanceCore>> InstanceCore::create(const GpuRegistryOptions& options) {
    DVV_TRY(const ValidationRequest request,
            resolveValidation(options, getEnvironment("DVV_VK_VALIDATION"),
                              getEnvironment("DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED"), DEBUG_BUILD));
    DVV_TRY(const PFN_vkGetInstanceProcAddr get_instance_proc_addr, vulkanLoader());

    auto core = std::make_unique<InstanceCore>();
    core->dispatch_.init(get_instance_proc_addr);
    const auto& dispatch = core->dispatch_;

    // vkEnumerateInstanceVersion exists from loader 1.1 on.
    if (dispatch.vkEnumerateInstanceVersion == nullptr) {
        return makeError(Errc::Unsupported, "the Vulkan loader is 1.0; 1.3 or newer is required");
    }
    const auto loader_version = vk::enumerateInstanceVersion(dispatch);
    DVV_TRY_VOID(vkCheck(loader_version.result, "vkEnumerateInstanceVersion"));
    core->loader_version_ = loader_version.value;
    if (loader_version.value < VK_API_VERSION_1_3) {
        return makeError(Errc::Unsupported,
                         std::format("the Vulkan loader is {}; 1.3 or newer is required",
                                     formatVulkanVersion(loader_version.value)));
    }

    const auto layers = vk::enumerateInstanceLayerProperties(dispatch);
    DVV_TRY_VOID(vkCheck(layers.result, "vkEnumerateInstanceLayerProperties"));
    const auto layer =
        std::ranges::find_if(layers.value, [](const vk::LayerProperties& properties) {
            return std::string_view(properties.layerName) == VALIDATION_LAYER;
        });
    // An older layer (e.g. a system's 1.3) does not know Vulkan 1.4 and reports false errors.
    const bool layer_installed =
        layer != layers.value.end() && layer->specVersion >= VK_API_VERSION_1_4;
    if (layer != layers.value.end() && !layer_installed) {
        DVV_LOG_INFO("{} {} is too old for Vulkan 1.4 and is not used", VALIDATION_LAYER,
                     formatVulkanVersion(layer->specVersion));
    }
    DVV_TRY(core->validation_, effectiveValidation(request, layer_installed));
    if (core->validation_ != request.mode) {
        DVV_LOG(request.explicitlyRequested ? LogLevel::Warn : LogLevel::Info,
                "Vulkan validation '{}' was requested, but {} 1.4 or newer is not installed; "
                "validation is off",
                validationModeName(request.mode), VALIDATION_LAYER);
    }

    const auto extensions = vk::enumerateInstanceExtensionProperties(nullptr, dispatch);
    DVV_TRY_VOID(vkCheck(extensions.result, "vkEnumerateInstanceExtensionProperties"));
    core->debug_utils_ =
        std::ranges::any_of(extensions.value, [](const vk::ExtensionProperties& extension) {
            return std::string_view(extension.extensionName) == vk::EXTDebugUtilsExtensionName;
        });

    const std::scoped_lock lock(vkBootstrapMutex());
    auto built = buildInstance(get_instance_proc_addr, options, core.get(), core->debug_utils_,
                               core->validation_);
    // A layer whose manifest is listed but whose library does not load fails vkCreateInstance;
    // unless validation is required, the instance is built once more without it.
    if (!built && retryWithoutValidation(built.vk_result(), core->validation_, request.required)) {
        DVV_LOG_WARN("{} could not be loaded; validation is off", VALIDATION_LAYER);
        core->validation_ = ValidationMode::Off;
        // The failed attempt's loader errors (the layer library did not load, reported at ERROR
        // through the instance-creation messenger) were logged and are not validation findings.
        core->validation_messages_.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        built = buildInstance(get_instance_proc_addr, options, core.get(), core->debug_utils_,
                              core->validation_);
    }
    if (!built) {
        // When vk-bootstrap fails after vkCreateInstance (only when creating the messenger fails)
        // it leaks the instance; that is practically unreachable here because debug_utils_ was
        // checked first.
        return vkBootstrapError(built, "creating the Vulkan instance");
    }
    core->instance_ = vk::Instance(built->instance);
    core->messenger_ = vk::DebugUtilsMessengerEXT(built->debug_messenger);
    core->dispatch_.init(core->instance_);
    DVV_LOG_INFO("Vulkan instance created: loader {}, API 1.4, validation {}",
                 formatVulkanVersion(core->loader_version_), validationModeName(core->validation_));
    return core;
}

InstanceCore::~InstanceCore() {
    if (messenger_) {
        instance_.destroyDebugUtilsMessengerEXT(messenger_, nullptr, dispatch_);
    }
    if (instance_) {
        instance_.destroy(nullptr, dispatch_);
    }
}

void InstanceCore::onMessage(Severity severity, vk::DebugUtilsMessageTypeFlagsEXT types,
                             const vk::DebugUtilsMessengerCallbackDataEXT& data) noexcept {
    const bool counted = countsAsFailure(severity, types);
    if (counted) {
        validation_messages_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    }
    const LogLevel level = debugMessageLevel(severity, counted);
    auto& logger = Logger::instance();
    if (!logger.enabled(level)) {
        return;
    }
    try {
        logger.write(level,
                     formatDebugMessage(severity, types,
                                        data.pMessageIdName != nullptr ? data.pMessageIdName : "",
                                        data.pMessage != nullptr ? data.pMessage : ""));
    } catch (...) {
        // The callback runs inside the Vulkan driver; nothing may propagate into it.
        std::fputs("dvv: a Vulkan debug message could not be logged\n", stderr);
    }
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/gpu_registry.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"

#include <format>
#include <utility>

#include "instance.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

static_assert(ManagedType<GpuRegistry>);

std::string_view validationModeName(ValidationMode mode) noexcept {
    switch (mode) {
        case ValidationMode::Off: return "off";
        case ValidationMode::On: return "on";
        case ValidationMode::Sync: return "sync";
    }
    return "?";
}

std::string formatVulkanVersion(std::uint32_t version) {
    return std::format("{}.{}.{}", VK_API_VERSION_MAJOR(version), VK_API_VERSION_MINOR(version),
                       VK_API_VERSION_PATCH(version));
}

Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry>> GpuRegistry::create(const GpuRegistryOptions& options) {
    DVV_TRY(auto instance, detail::InstanceCore::create(options));
    return std::make_shared<GpuRegistry>(
        CreationKey<GpuRegistry>(LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<GpuRegistry>()),
        std::move(instance));
}

GpuRegistry::GpuRegistry(CreationKey<GpuRegistry> key,
                         std::unique_ptr<detail::InstanceCore> instance) noexcept
    : Managed(std::move(key)), instance_(std::move(instance)) {}

GpuRegistry::~GpuRegistry() = default;

ValidationMode GpuRegistry::validation() const noexcept {
    return instance_->validation();
}

std::uint64_t GpuRegistry::validationMessageCount() const noexcept {
    return instance_->validationMessageCount();
}

std::uint32_t GpuRegistry::loaderVersion() const noexcept {
    return instance_->loaderVersion();
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Commit and privacy lint**

```powershell
git -C dvv-gpu add -A
@'
feat: add GpuRegistry with the Vulkan instance and validation settings

GpuRegistry::create(GpuRegistryOptions) loads the Vulkan loader at run time (kept loaded for the
process, because vk-bootstrap keeps the first vkGetInstanceProcAddr) and builds a headless instance
with vk-bootstrap: Vulkan 1.4 API on a loader of at least 1.3. A missing loader is
Errc::Unsupported "Vulkan loader not found".

Validation comes from the options, else DVV_VK_VALIDATION (off, on, sync), else on in Debug and off
in Release; sync adds synchronization validation through VK_EXT_layer_settings. A missing layer, or
one older than 1.4, turns validation off with a log line unless DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED=1 (or
the option) makes it an error. The debug messenger logs every message to the dvv Logger and counts
errors and validation or performance warnings; validationMessageCount() reports the count.

The instance has its own dispatcher; destruction does not go through vk-bootstrap. Unit tests cover
the option resolution, the counting and logging policy and the missing loader; compile-fail tests
cover a forged key and construction without one.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C dvv-gpu commit -F -
```

### Task 14: Enumerate and rank Vulkan devices and add dvv-gpu-info

**Files:**
- dvv-gpu: Create `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/gpu_info.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/devices.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/devices.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/src/gpu_info.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tools/dvv_gpu_info.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/test_gpu_info.cpp`; Modify `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt`, `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/gpu_registry.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/src/instance.h`, `dvv-gpu/tests/CMakeLists.txt`
- rawreel-workspace: Modify `rawreel-workspace/.github/workflows/dvv-ci.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 12 (`src/vk.h`, `vkCheck` from `src/vk_result.h`, `DeviceId` with its `std::formatter`), Task 13 (`GpuRegistry`, `GpuRegistryOptions`, `formatVulkanVersion`, `validationModeName`, `detail::InstanceCore::create`, `instance()`, `dispatch()`). dvv-base: `dvv::json::Value` (= `nlohmann::json`, `dvv/base/json.h`), `dvv::json::dump(const Value&, int indent = -1)`, `dvv::errcName(Errc)`, `Error::describe()`, `DVV_TRY`, `DVV_TRY_VOID`, `DVV_LOG_INFO`, `dvv::Logger::instance().addSink(std::shared_ptr<ILogSink>)`, `Logger::setLevel(LogLevel)`, `dvv::StderrSink`, `dvv::finishWithLeakCheck(int)`.
- Produces (C++, public, `dvv/gpu/gpu_info.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu`): `enum class DeviceType : std::uint8_t { Other, IntegratedGpu, DiscreteGpu, VirtualGpu, Cpu }`; `std::string_view deviceTypeName(DeviceType) noexcept` (`"discrete GPU"`, `"integrated GPU"`, `"virtual GPU"`, `"CPU"`, `"other"`); `struct OptionalFeatures { bool shaderInt16, shaderFloat16, hostImageCopy, memoryBudget; }` (all `false` by default); `struct GpuInfo { DeviceId id; std::string name; DeviceType type; std::uint32_t vendorId, productId, apiVersion, driverVersion; std::string driverName, driverInfo; std::array<std::uint8_t, 16> deviceUuid; std::uint64_t deviceLocalMemoryBytes; std::vector<std::string> missingRequirements; OptionalFeatures optional; bool usable() const noexcept; }` (`productId` is Vulkan's `deviceID`; `usable()` means `missingRequirements` is empty); `std::string formatUuid(const std::array<std::uint8_t, 16>&)` (lower-case 8-4-4-4-12); `json::Value toJson(const GpuInfo&)`; `std::vector<DeviceId> rankDevices(std::span<const GpuInfo>)` (usable first, then discrete > integrated > virtual > CPU > other, then more device-local memory, stable).
- Produces (C++, public, `GpuRegistry`): the constructor becomes `GpuRegistry(CreationKey<GpuRegistry>, std::unique_ptr<detail::InstanceCore>, std::vector<GpuInfo> devices, std::vector<DeviceId> ranked) noexcept`; new `std::span<const GpuInfo> devices() const noexcept` (enumeration order, `devices()[id.index()]`), `std::span<const DeviceId> rankedDevices() const noexcept` (best first), `std::optional<DeviceId> bestDevice() const noexcept` (the first ranked device when it is usable, else `std::nullopt`). `create()` logs one Info line per device.
- Produces (C++, private, `src/devices.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu::detail`): `using FeatureChain = vk::StructureChain<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2, vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features, vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features, vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan13Features, vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>`; `template <class Chain, class Visit> void forEachRequiredFeature(Chain& chain, Visit&& visit)` (calls `visit(name, flag)` for the 15 required features, `flag` being the `vk::Bool32` member); `struct DeviceSupport { std::uint32_t apiVersion; std::optional<std::uint32_t> universalQueueFamily; FeatureChain features; OptionalFeatures optional; }`; `std::vector<std::string> missingRequirements(const DeviceSupport&)`; `FeatureChain enabledFeatures(const OptionalFeatures&)` (Task 16 creates the device with it); `struct PhysicalDevice { vk::PhysicalDevice handle; std::optional<std::uint32_t> universalQueueFamily; }`; `struct Enumeration { std::vector<GpuInfo> infos; std::vector<PhysicalDevice> devices; }`; `Result<Enumeration> enumerateDevices(vk::Instance, const vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic&)`. `InstanceCore` gains `const std::vector<PhysicalDevice>& physicalDevices() const noexcept` (indexed by `DeviceId`) and `void setPhysicalDevices(std::vector<PhysicalDevice>) noexcept`.
- Produces (CMake): executable target `dvv_gpu_info` (file name `dvv-gpu-info`, installed to `bin`): `dvv-gpu-info [--json]`, `--help`/`-h`; exit 0 when a usable device exists, 4 without a loader or a usable device (also on an unexpected exception), 1 for a bad argument (spec §10.3). The logger writes to stderr at Warn.
- Produces (workspace): the `Tidy` step of `dvv-ci.yml` also checks `tools/*.cpp`.

Note: vk-bootstrap is used only for the instance (Task 13). Physical-device enumeration, the feature check and (in Task 16) device creation are dvv-gpu's own Vulkan-Hpp code, so the list of missing features is exact and vk-bootstrap's process-wide tables are not involved. A feature structure above a device's Vulkan version is not chained into the query (that is invalid usage); its features stay `false`. The same holds for the property structures: `PhysicalDeviceDriverProperties` (core 1.2) is unlinked below Vulkan 1.2 and `PhysicalDeviceIDProperties` (core 1.1) below Vulkan 1.1, so such a device reports an empty driver name and an all-zero UUID (the `IDProperties` unlink is a review fix). No test in this task creates a real registry with devices; Step 5 runs the tool by hand, and Task 15 adds the GPU test harness that runs it automatically. The CI change is committed to rawreel-workspace's `m1a` branch; sibling repositories call `dvv-ci.yml@main`, so it takes effect there only after the publishing task merges the workspace. Windows workflows can take several minutes: use a 10-minute timeout or run them in the background.

`dvv-gpu/tests/test_gpu_info.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

#include "devices.h"
#include "dvv/base/json.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_info.h"

using dvv::gpu::DeviceId;
using dvv::gpu::DeviceType;
using dvv::gpu::GpuInfo;
using dvv::gpu::detail::DeviceSupport;
using dvv::gpu::detail::enabledFeatures;
using dvv::gpu::detail::forEachRequiredFeature;
using dvv::gpu::detail::missingRequirements;

namespace {

GpuInfo device(std::uint32_t index, DeviceType type, std::uint64_t memory, bool usable = true) {
    GpuInfo info{.id = DeviceId(index), .type = type, .deviceLocalMemoryBytes = memory};
    if (!usable) {
        info.missingRequirements = {"maintenance5"};
    }
    return info;
}

// A Vulkan 1.4 device with a universal queue and every required feature.
DeviceSupport complete() {
    DeviceSupport support{.apiVersion = VK_API_VERSION_1_4, .universalQueueFamily = 0};
    forEachRequiredFeature(support.features,
                           [](std::string_view /*name*/, vk::Bool32& flag) { flag = VK_TRUE; });
    return support;
}

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE("devices rank usable first, then by type, then by device-local memory") {
    const std::vector<GpuInfo> devices{
        device(0, DeviceType::Cpu, 64),          device(1, DeviceType::DiscreteGpu, 8, false),
        device(2, DeviceType::IntegratedGpu, 2), device(3, DeviceType::DiscreteGpu, 4),
        device(4, DeviceType::DiscreteGpu, 12),  device(5, DeviceType::VirtualGpu, 1),
        device(6, DeviceType::Other, 1),         device(7, DeviceType::DiscreteGpu, 12),
    };
    const std::vector<DeviceId> expected{DeviceId(4), DeviceId(7), DeviceId(3), DeviceId(2),
                                         DeviceId(5), DeviceId(0), DeviceId(6), DeviceId(1)};
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::rankDevices(devices) == expected);
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::rankDevices({}).empty());
}

TEST_CASE("a Vulkan 1.4 device with every required feature misses nothing") {
    CHECK(missingRequirements(complete()).empty());
}

TEST_CASE("missing requirements are named in order") {
    DeviceSupport support = complete();
    support.apiVersion = VK_MAKE_API_VERSION(0, 1, 3, 280);
    support.universalQueueFamily.reset();
    support.features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features>().bufferDeviceAddress = VK_FALSE;
    support.features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>().maintenance6 = VK_FALSE;
    const std::vector<std::string> expected{"Vulkan 1.4 (the device has 1.3.280)",
                                            "a queue family with graphics and compute",
                                            "bufferDeviceAddress", "maintenance6"};
    CHECK(missingRequirements(support) == expected);
}

TEST_CASE("a device is created with every required feature and the optional ones it has") {
    const auto features = enabledFeatures({.shaderInt16 = true, .hostImageCopy = true});
    std::vector<std::string> disabled;
    forEachRequiredFeature(features, [&disabled](std::string_view name, vk::Bool32 flag) {
        if (flag != VK_TRUE) {
            disabled.emplace_back(name);
        }
    });
    CHECK(disabled.empty());
    CHECK(features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2>().features.shaderInt16 == VK_TRUE);
    CHECK(features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features>().shaderFloat16 == VK_FALSE);
    CHECK(features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>().hostImageCopy == VK_TRUE);
    // The structures are chained for vkCreateDevice.
    CHECK(features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2>().pNext ==
          &features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features>());
}

TEST_CASE("device information formats as text and JSON") {
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::deviceTypeName(DeviceType::DiscreteGpu) == "discrete GPU");
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::deviceTypeName(DeviceType::Cpu) == "CPU");
    CHECK(dvv::gpu::formatUuid({0x01, 0x23, 0x45, 0x67, 0x89, 0xab, 0xcd, 0xef, 0x00, 0x11, 0x22,
                                0x33, 0x44, 0x55, 0x66, 0xff}) ==
          "01234567-89ab-cdef-0011-2233445566ff");

    GpuInfo info = device(2, DeviceType::Cpu, 1024, false);
    info.name = "llvmpipe";
    info.apiVersion = VK_MAKE_API_VERSION(0, 1, 4, 318);
    info.optional.memoryBudget = true;
    const dvv::json::Value json = dvv::gpu::toJson(info);
    CHECK(json.at("id") == 2);
    CHECK(json.at("name") == "llvmpipe");
    CHECK(json.at("type") == "CPU");
    CHECK(json.at("apiVersion") == "1.4.318");
    CHECK(json.at("deviceUuid") == "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000");
    CHECK(json.at("deviceLocalMemoryBytes") == 1024);
    CHECK(json.at("usable") == false);
    CHECK(json.at("missingRequirements") == dvv::json::Value::array({"maintenance5"}));
    CHECK(json.at("optionalFeatures").at("memoryBudget") == true);
    CHECK(json.at("optionalFeatures").at("shaderInt16") == false);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the device information and the enumeration**

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/gpu_info.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/json.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/device_id.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

enum class DeviceType : std::uint8_t { Other, IntegratedGpu, DiscreteGpu, VirtualGpu, Cpu };

// "discrete GPU", "integrated GPU", "virtual GPU", "CPU" or "other".
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view deviceTypeName(DeviceType type) noexcept;

// Used when present (spec §5.1); a device without them is still usable.
struct OptionalFeatures {
    bool shaderInt16 = false;
    bool shaderFloat16 = false;
    bool hostImageCopy = false;
    bool memoryBudget = false;  // VK_EXT_memory_budget
};

// One physical device as the GpuRegistry found it.
struct GpuInfo {
    DeviceId id{0};
    std::string name{};
    DeviceType type = DeviceType::Other;
    std::uint32_t vendorId = 0;
    std::uint32_t productId = 0;      // VkPhysicalDeviceProperties::deviceID
    std::uint32_t apiVersion = 0;     // VK_MAKE_API_VERSION encoding
    std::uint32_t driverVersion = 0;  // vendor-specific encoding
    std::string driverName{};         // VkPhysicalDeviceDriverProperties
    std::string driverInfo{};         // usually the driver's own version text
    std::array<std::uint8_t, 16> deviceUuid{};
    std::uint64_t deviceLocalMemoryBytes = 0;  // sum of the device-local heaps
    // Requirements of spec §5.1 the device does not meet ("Vulkan 1.4", feature names, ...); empty
    // when the device is usable.
    std::vector<std::string> missingRequirements{};
    OptionalFeatures optional{};

    [[nodiscard]] bool usable() const noexcept { return missingRequirements.empty(); }
};

// Lower case hexadecimal in the 8-4-4-4-12 grouping.
[[nodiscard]] std::string formatUuid(const std::array<std::uint8_t, 16>& uuid);

// Every field; versions as "major.minor.patch" text, the UUID as formatUuid() text.
[[nodiscard]] json::Value toJson(const GpuInfo& info);

// Best first: devices that meet the requirements, then discrete > integrated > virtual > CPU >
// other, then more device-local memory; ties keep the enumeration order.
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<DeviceId> rankDevices(std::span<const GpuInfo> devices);

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/gpu_info.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_info.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <format>

#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

namespace {

// Smaller is better.
int typeRank(DeviceType type) noexcept {
    switch (type) {
        case DeviceType::DiscreteGpu: return 0;
        case DeviceType::IntegratedGpu: return 1;
        case DeviceType::VirtualGpu: return 2;
        case DeviceType::Cpu: return 3;
        case DeviceType::Other: break;
    }
    return 4;
}

}  // namespace

std::string_view deviceTypeName(DeviceType type) noexcept {
    switch (type) {
        case DeviceType::IntegratedGpu: return "integrated GPU";
        case DeviceType::DiscreteGpu: return "discrete GPU";
        case DeviceType::VirtualGpu: return "virtual GPU";
        case DeviceType::Cpu: return "CPU";
        case DeviceType::Other: break;
    }
    return "other";
}

std::string formatUuid(const std::array<std::uint8_t, 16>& uuid) {
    std::string text;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < uuid.size(); ++i) {
        if (i == 4 || i == 6 || i == 8 || i == 10) {
            text += '-';
        }
        text += std::format("{:02x}", uuid[i]);
    }
    return text;
}

json::Value toJson(const GpuInfo& info) {
    return json::Value{
        {"id", info.id.index()},
        {"name", info.name},
        {"type", deviceTypeName(info.type)},
        {"vendorId", info.vendorId},
        {"productId", info.productId},
        {"apiVersion", formatVulkanVersion(info.apiVersion)},
        {"driverVersion", info.driverVersion},
        {"driverName", info.driverName},
        {"driverInfo", info.driverInfo},
        {"deviceUuid", formatUuid(info.deviceUuid)},
        {"deviceLocalMemoryBytes", info.deviceLocalMemoryBytes},
        {"usable", info.usable()},
        {"missingRequirements", info.missingRequirements},
        {"optionalFeatures",
         {{"shaderInt16", info.optional.shaderInt16},
          {"shaderFloat16", info.optional.shaderFloat16},
          {"hostImageCopy", info.optional.hostImageCopy},
          {"memoryBudget", info.optional.memoryBudget}}},
    };
}

std::vector<DeviceId> rankDevices(std::span<const GpuInfo> devices) {
    std::vector<const GpuInfo*> order;
    order.reserve(devices.size());
    for (const GpuInfo& device : devices) {
        order.push_back(&device);
    }
    std::ranges::stable_sort(order, [](const GpuInfo* left, const GpuInfo* right) {
        if (left->usable() != right->usable()) {
            return left->usable();
        }
        if (typeRank(left->type) != typeRank(right->type)) {
            return typeRank(left->type) < typeRank(right->type);
        }
        return left->deviceLocalMemoryBytes > right->deviceLocalMemoryBytes;
    });
    std::vector<DeviceId> ranked;
    ranked.reserve(order.size());
    for (const GpuInfo* device : order) {
        ranked.push_back(device->id);
    }
    return ranked;
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/devices.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Physical devices: what they support, which of the required features (spec §5.1) they lack, and
// the feature set a device is created with.

#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_info.h"
#include "vk.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

using FeatureChain =
    vk::StructureChain<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2, vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features,
                       vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features, vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan13Features,
                       vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>;

// The required features, in one list for checking and enabling: calls visit(name, flag) for each,
// where flag is the vk::Bool32 member of the chain.
template <class Chain, class Visit>
void forEachRequiredFeature(Chain& chain, Visit&& visit) {
    auto& core = chain.template get<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2>().features;
    auto& v11 = chain.template get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features>();
    auto& v12 = chain.template get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features>();
    auto& v13 = chain.template get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan13Features>();
    auto& v14 = chain.template get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>();
    visit("shaderInt64", core.shaderInt64);  // buffer device addresses in shaders
    visit("storageBuffer16BitAccess", v11.storageBuffer16BitAccess);
    visit("uniformAndStorageBuffer16BitAccess", v11.uniformAndStorageBuffer16BitAccess);
    visit("timelineSemaphore", v12.timelineSemaphore);
    visit("bufferDeviceAddress", v12.bufferDeviceAddress);
    visit("descriptorIndexing", v12.descriptorIndexing);
    visit("runtimeDescriptorArray", v12.runtimeDescriptorArray);
    visit("descriptorBindingPartiallyBound", v12.descriptorBindingPartiallyBound);
    visit("descriptorBindingVariableDescriptorCount", v12.descriptorBindingVariableDescriptorCount);
    visit("shaderSampledImageArrayNonUniformIndexing",
          v12.shaderSampledImageArrayNonUniformIndexing);
    visit("descriptorBindingSampledImageUpdateAfterBind",
          v12.descriptorBindingSampledImageUpdateAfterBind);
    visit("synchronization2", v13.synchronization2);
    visit("dynamicRendering", v13.dynamicRendering);
    visit("maintenance5", v14.maintenance5);
    visit("maintenance6", v14.maintenance6);
}

// What a physical device offers, as far as the GpuRegistry needs it.
struct DeviceSupport {
    std::uint32_t apiVersion = 0;
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> universalQueueFamily = std::nullopt;  // graphics + compute
    FeatureChain features{};  // structures above the device's API version stay all false
    OptionalFeatures optional{};
};

// The requirements of spec §5.1 the device misses: "Vulkan 1.4 (the device has 1.3.280)", "a queue
// family with graphics and compute", then the missing feature names in list order.
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> missingRequirements(const DeviceSupport& support);

// The features a context's device is created with: every required one, plus the optional ones the
// device supports.
[[nodiscard]] FeatureChain enabledFeatures(const OptionalFeatures& optional);

// A physical device of the instance, indexed by DeviceId.
struct PhysicalDevice {
    vk::PhysicalDevice handle{};
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> universalQueueFamily = std::nullopt;
};

struct Enumeration {
    std::vector<GpuInfo> infos{};
    std::vector<PhysicalDevice> devices{};  // same order as infos
};

[[nodiscard]] Result<Enumeration> enumerateDevices(
    vk::Instance instance, const vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic& dispatch);

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/devices.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "devices.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <format>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>

#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "vk_result.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

namespace {

using Dispatch = vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic;

DeviceType toDeviceType(vk::PhysicalDeviceType type) noexcept {
    switch (type) {
        case vk::PhysicalDeviceType::eIntegratedGpu: return DeviceType::IntegratedGpu;
        case vk::PhysicalDeviceType::eDiscreteGpu: return DeviceType::DiscreteGpu;
        case vk::PhysicalDeviceType::eVirtualGpu: return DeviceType::VirtualGpu;
        case vk::PhysicalDeviceType::eCpu: return DeviceType::Cpu;
        default: return DeviceType::Other;
    }
}

Result<DeviceSupport> querySupport(vk::PhysicalDevice device, std::uint32_t api_version,
                                   const Dispatch& dispatch) {
    DeviceSupport support{.apiVersion = api_version};

    const auto families = device.getQueueFamilyProperties(dispatch);
    constexpr auto UNIVERSAL = vk::QueueFlagBits::eGraphics | vk::QueueFlagBits::eCompute;
    for (std::uint32_t index = 0; index < families.size(); ++index) {
        if ((families[index].queueFlags & UNIVERSAL) == UNIVERSAL) {
            support.universalQueueFamily = index;
            break;
        }
    }

    // Chaining a structure of a newer Vulkan version than the device's is invalid usage, so those
    // stay unlinked and all false.
    auto& features = support.features;
    if (api_version < VK_API_VERSION_1_4) {
        features.unlink<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>();
    }
    if (api_version < VK_API_VERSION_1_3) {
        features.unlink<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan13Features>();
    }
    if (api_version < VK_API_VERSION_1_2) {
        features.unlink<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features>();
        features.unlink<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features>();
    }
    device.getFeatures2(&features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2>(), dispatch);
    support.optional.shaderInt16 =
        features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2>().features.shaderInt16 == VK_TRUE;
    support.optional.shaderFloat16 =
        features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features>().shaderFloat16 == VK_TRUE;
    support.optional.hostImageCopy =
        features.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>().hostImageCopy == VK_TRUE;

    const auto extensions = device.enumerateDeviceExtensionProperties(nullptr, dispatch);
    DVV_TRY_VOID(vkCheck(extensions.result, "vkEnumerateDeviceExtensionProperties"));
    support.optional.memoryBudget =
        std::ranges::any_of(extensions.value, [](const vk::ExtensionProperties& extension) {
            return std::string_view(extension.extensionName) == vk::EXTMemoryBudgetExtensionName;
        });
    return support;
}

}  // namespace

std::vector<std::string> missingRequirements(const DeviceSupport& support) {
    std::vector<std::string> missing;
    if (support.apiVersion < VK_API_VERSION_1_4) {
        missing.push_back(
            std::format("Vulkan 1.4 (the device has {})", formatVulkanVersion(support.apiVersion)));
    }
    if (!support.universalQueueFamily) {
        missing.emplace_back("a queue family with graphics and compute");
    }
    forEachRequiredFeature(support.features, [&missing](std::string_view name, vk::Bool32 flag) {
        if (flag != VK_TRUE) {
            missing.emplace_back(name);
        }
    });
    return missing;
}

FeatureChain enabledFeatures(const OptionalFeatures& optional) {
    FeatureChain chain;
    forEachRequiredFeature(chain,
                           [](std::string_view /*name*/, vk::Bool32& flag) { flag = VK_TRUE; });
    const auto flag = [](bool enabled) { return enabled ? VK_TRUE : VK_FALSE; };
    chain.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceFeatures2>().features.shaderInt16 = flag(optional.shaderInt16);
    chain.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan12Features>().shaderFloat16 = flag(optional.shaderFloat16);
    chain.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceVulkan14Features>().hostImageCopy = flag(optional.hostImageCopy);
    return chain;
}

Result<Enumeration> enumerateDevices(vk::Instance instance, const Dispatch& dispatch) {
    const auto handles = instance.enumeratePhysicalDevices(dispatch);
    DVV_TRY_VOID(vkCheck(handles.result, "vkEnumeratePhysicalDevices"));

    Enumeration enumeration;
    for (std::uint32_t index = 0; index < handles.value.size(); ++index) {
        const vk::PhysicalDevice device = handles.value[index];
        const std::uint32_t api_version = device.getProperties(dispatch).apiVersion;

        vk::StructureChain<vk::PhysicalDeviceProperties2, vk::PhysicalDeviceIDProperties,
                           vk::PhysicalDeviceDriverProperties>
            properties;
        if (api_version < VK_API_VERSION_1_2) {
            properties.unlink<vk::PhysicalDeviceDriverProperties>();
        }
        if (api_version < VK_API_VERSION_1_1) {
            properties.unlink<vk::PhysicalDeviceIDProperties>();  // the UUID then stays zero
        }
        device.getProperties2(&properties.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceProperties2>(), dispatch);
        const auto& basic = properties.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceProperties2>().properties;
        const auto& ids = properties.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceIDProperties>();
        const auto& driver = properties.get<vk::PhysicalDeviceDriverProperties>();

        GpuInfo info{.id = DeviceId(index),
                     .name = std::string(basic.deviceName.data()),
                     .type = toDeviceType(basic.deviceType),
                     .vendorId = basic.vendorID,
                     .productId = basic.deviceID,
                     .apiVersion = api_version,
                     .driverVersion = basic.driverVersion,
                     .driverName = std::string(driver.driverName.data()),
                     .driverInfo = std::string(driver.driverInfo.data())};
        std::ranges::copy(ids.deviceUUID, info.deviceUuid.begin());
        const auto memory = device.getMemoryProperties(dispatch);
        for (std::uint32_t heap = 0; heap < memory.memoryHeapCount; ++heap) {
            if (memory.memoryHeaps[heap].flags & vk::MemoryHeapFlagBits::eDeviceLocal) {
                info.deviceLocalMemoryBytes += memory.memoryHeaps[heap].size;
            }
        }

        DVV_TRY(const DeviceSupport support, querySupport(device, api_version, dispatch));
        info.missingRequirements = missingRequirements(support);
        info.optional = support.optional;
        enumeration.infos.push_back(std::move(info));
        enumeration.devices.push_back(
            {.handle = device, .universalQueueFamily = support.universalQueueFamily});
    }
    return enumeration;
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```
