### Task 17: GpuBuffer and GpuImage with deferred destruction

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/resources.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/resources.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_test_resources.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/tsan.supp`
- Modify: `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt` (one source), `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/execution_context.h` (`createBuffer`, `createImage`), `dvv-gpu/src/execution_context.cpp` (the factories), `dvv-gpu/src/access.h` (`Access::setLayout`), `dvv-gpu/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (ThreadSanitizer suppressions, GPU test source, compile-fail cases 4-5), `dvv-gpu/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 16): `ExecutionContext` (private `std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core_`), `ContextOptions`, `RetireStats`, `ExecutionContext::retireStats()`; `detail::DeviceCore`: `collect()`, `retire(std::variant<vk::Buffer, vk::Image>, VmaAllocation) noexcept`, `allocator()`, `device()`, `dispatch()`, `physicalDevice()`, `id()`, `generation()`, `setDebugName(Handle, const std::string&)`, `lockQueue()`, `reserveSubmitValue()`, `setSubmitted(std::uint64_t, const std::unique_lock<std::mutex>&)`, `lastReservedValue()`, `lastSubmittedValue()`, `timeline()`; `GpuRegistry::create()` and `createContext()` (a test makes a registry of its own); `detail::Access::core(const ExecutionContext&)`; the fixture's `GpuTest::context()`; compile-fail cases 1-3 and the GPU test block of `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (VMA linked into `dvv_gpu_device_tests`).
- Consumes (Task 12): `detail::vkCheck(...)`; `src/vk.h` (`vk::to_string`, `vk::BufferCreateInfo`, `vk::ImageCreateInfo`).
- Consumes (dvv-base): as in Task 16, plus `[[nodiscard]] std::size_t LifetimeRegistry::liveCount() const noexcept`.
- Produces (C++, public, `dvv/gpu/resources.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu`; Vulkan C types from `<vulkan/vulkan_core.h>`, `struct VmaAllocation_T;` forward-declared at global scope): `enum class MemoryUsage : std::uint8_t { DeviceOnly, HostUpload, HostReadback };`; `struct BufferDesc { std::uint64_t size = 0; VkBufferUsageFlags usage = 0; MemoryUsage memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly; std::string name{}; };`; `struct ImageDesc { std::uint32_t width = 0; std::uint32_t height = 0; VkFormat format = VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED; VkImageUsageFlags usage = 0; std::string name{}; };`; `class GpuBuffer final : public Managed<GpuBuffer>` with constructor `GpuBuffer(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core, BufferDesc desc, VkBuffer buffer, VmaAllocation_T* allocation, VkDeviceAddress address, std::span<std::byte> mapped) noexcept`, `desc() -> const BufferDesc&`, `size() -> std::uint64_t`, `handle() -> VkBuffer`, `deviceAddress() -> VkDeviceAddress`, `mapped() -> std::span<std::byte>` (empty for `DeviceOnly`), `deviceId() -> DeviceId`, `generation() -> DeviceGeneration` (all `const noexcept`), `[[nodiscard]] Result<void> flush(std::uint64_t offset = 0, std::uint64_t size = VK_WHOLE_SIZE)`, `[[nodiscard]] Result<void> invalidate(std::uint64_t offset = 0, std::uint64_t size = VK_WHOLE_SIZE)`; `class GpuImage final : public Managed<GpuImage>, public std::enable_shared_from_this<GpuImage>` (the command recorder of Task 18 keeps the images whose layout a submission changes) with constructor `GpuImage(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core, ImageDesc desc, VkImage image, VmaAllocation_T* allocation) noexcept`, `desc() -> const ImageDesc&`, `width()`, `height() -> std::uint32_t`, `format() -> VkFormat`, `handle() -> VkImage`, `layout() -> VkImageLayout` (atomic, `VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED` at first), `deviceId()`, `generation()` (all `const noexcept`). In `dvv/gpu/execution_context.h` (which now includes `resources.h`): `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> ExecutionContext::createBuffer(BufferDesc desc)` and `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>> ExecutionContext::createImage(ImageDesc desc)`; each resource's owner in the lifetime registry is the context.
- Produces (C++, private): `static void detail::Access::setLayout(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout) noexcept` (for the command recorder of Task 18).
- Produces (tests): `tests/tsan.supp` (ThreadSanitizer suppressions; `TSAN_OPTIONS=suppressions=<it>` is added to `gpu_test_environment`, the environment of `dvv_gpu.gpu` and `dvv_gpu.gpu_info`, when `DVV_SANITIZE` is `thread`); compile-fail cases `dvv_gpu.compile_fail.4` and `.5` (`std::make_shared` of a `GpuBuffer` or `GpuImage` without a key: MSVC `C2665: 'dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer::GpuBuffer'` / `'dvv::gpu::GpuImage::GpuImage'`, Clang `no matching constructor for initialization of 'dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer'` / `'dvv::gpu::GpuImage'`).
- Behaviour: `createBuffer`: size 0 -> `Errc::Internal` `buffer '<name>': the size is zero`; `VK_BUFFER_USAGE_SHADER_DEVICE_ADDRESS_BIT` is always added; VMA usage `AUTO_PREFER_DEVICE` (`DeviceOnly`), `AUTO` with `HOST_ACCESS_SEQUENTIAL_WRITE | MAPPED` (`HostUpload`), `AUTO` with `HOST_ACCESS_RANDOM | MAPPED` (`HostReadback`); a non-empty name names the buffer and its VMA allocation. `createImage`: zero width or height, `VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED` or no usage -> `Errc::Internal`; `vkGetPhysicalDeviceImageFormatProperties2` (2D, optimal tiling) decides `Errc::Unsupported` for an unsupported format or usage and for an extent above `maxExtent`; device-local memory, 1 mip, 1 layer, initial layout `UNDEFINED`. Both factories call `collect()` first. A destructor calls `retire(handle, allocation)` (tagged with the last reserved timeline value) and then `collect()`, so a resource no submission can have used is freed at once. Resources may be destroyed on any thread; the retire queue is guarded by its own mutex. When `std::make_shared` throws, the factory destroys the VMA object and rethrows.

Note: public headers use the Vulkan C types (`VkBuffer`, `VkFormat`, ...), so consumers need no Vulkan-Hpp configuration; `src/vk.h` stays private. `GpuBuffer::flush()` and `invalidate()` are explicit for now (no-ops on host-coherent memory); whether submission flushes host-visible buffers automatically is decided with Task 18.

Note (review fixes): `GpuImage` derives from `std::enable_shared_from_this<GpuImage>` now, so Task 18's recorder can keep an image alive without a later change to this class. The test helper `pretendSubmission` uses Task 16's `reserveSubmitValue()` and `setSubmitted()`. "a resource keeps the device after its context is gone" uses a registry of its own and a `std::weak_ptr` on it: the buffer keeps the device core, which keeps the registry, so the registry outlives the context and is released exactly when the buffer goes (checked without relying on validation, which is off in Release without `DVV_VK_VALIDATION`). "buffers are created and retired from several threads" creates and destroys 200 buffers on 4 threads while the main thread runs `collect()` and `drain()`, so the TSan preset exercises the retire queue's locking.

Note (ThreadSanitizer suppression): under `ninja-clang-tsan` the multi-threaded retire test failed in about 4 of 10 prototype runs on lavapipe. Every report came from inside the driver (races between the driver's own `malloc` and `free`, no dvv-gpu frame involved): Mesa lavapipe is not instrumented and synchronizes its objects with futex-based locks that ThreadSanitizer cannot see. `tests/tsan.supp` therefore suppresses races in `libvulkan_lvp.so` only, and the GPU tests get `TSAN_OPTIONS=suppressions=...` only in thread-sanitizer builds; races in dvv-gpu's own code are still reported.

Every Windows command below that runs the GPU tests sets `DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync` itself, in the same command, as in Task 15, and removes it afterwards.

`dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_test_resources.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

#include "access.h"
#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "gpu_fixture.h"

using dvv::gpu::BufferDesc;
using dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer;
using dvv::gpu::GpuImage;
using dvv::gpu::ImageDesc;
using dvv::gpu::MemoryUsage;
using dvv::gpu::detail::Access;
using dvv::gpu::test::GpuTest;

namespace {

// As if a submission signalling the next timeline value were in flight; returns that value.
std::uint64_t pretendSubmission(dvv::gpu::detail::DeviceCore& core) {
    const std::uint64_t value = core.reserveSubmitValue();
    const auto lock = core.lockQueue();
    core.setSubmitted(value, lock);
    return value;
}

void signalTimeline(dvv::gpu::detail::DeviceCore& core, std::uint64_t value) {
    const vk::SemaphoreSignalInfo signal{.semaphore = core.timeline(), .value = value};
    REQUIRE(core.device().signalSemaphore(signal, core.dispatch()) == vk::Result::eSuccess);
}

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a host-visible buffer is mapped and has a device address") {
    const auto buffer = context()
                            .createBuffer({.size = 1000 * sizeof(std::uint32_t),
                                           .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT,
                                           .memory = MemoryUsage::HostReadback,
                                           .name = "test values"})
                            .value();
    CHECK(buffer->size() == 4000);
    CHECK(buffer->desc().name == "test values");
    CHECK(buffer->handle() != VK_NULL_HANDLE);
    CHECK(buffer->deviceAddress() != 0);
    CHECK(buffer->deviceId() == device());
    CHECK(buffer->generation() == dvv::gpu::FIRST_GENERATION);
    CHECK(buffer->ownerId() == context().objectId());
    CHECK(buffer->typeName() == "dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer");

    const std::span<std::byte> mapped = buffer->mapped();
    REQUIRE(mapped.size() == 4000);
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> values(1000);
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < values.size(); ++i) {
        values[i] = i * 3;
    }
    std::memcpy(mapped.data(), values.data(), mapped.size());
    REQUIRE(buffer->flush().has_value());
    REQUIRE(buffer->invalidate().has_value());
    CHECK(std::memcmp(mapped.data(), values.data(), mapped.size()) == 0);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "an upload buffer is mapped, a device-only buffer is not") {
    const auto upload = context()
                            .createBuffer({.size = 64,
                                           .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
                                           .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload})
                            .value();
    CHECK(upload->mapped().size() == 64);

    const auto device_only = context()
                                 .createBuffer({.size = 64,
                                                .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT,
                                                .memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly})
                                 .value();
    CHECK(device_only->mapped().empty());
    CHECK(device_only->deviceAddress() != 0);
    CHECK(device_only->deviceAddress() != upload->deviceAddress());
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a buffer without a size is a programming error") {
    const auto buffer = context().createBuffer({.size = 0, .name = "empty"});
    REQUIRE_FALSE(buffer.has_value());
    CHECK(buffer.error().code == dvv::Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(buffer.error().message == "buffer 'empty': the size is zero");
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "an image is created undefined and tracks its layout") {
    const auto image =
        context()
            .createImage({.width = 33,
                          .height = 17,
                          .format = VK_FORMAT_R32_UINT,
                          .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT |
                                   VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT,
                          .name = "test image"})
            .value();
    CHECK(image->width() == 33);
    CHECK(image->height() == 17);
    CHECK(image->format() == VK_FORMAT_R32_UINT);
    CHECK(image->handle() != VK_NULL_HANDLE);
    CHECK(image->deviceId() == device());
    CHECK(image->generation() == dvv::gpu::FIRST_GENERATION);
    CHECK(image->ownerId() == context().objectId());
    CHECK(image->layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED);

    Access::setLayout(*image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL);
    CHECK(image->layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "images the device cannot create are reported") {
    const auto unset = context().createImage({.width = 4, .height = 4, .name = "unset"});
    REQUIRE_FALSE(unset.has_value());
    CHECK(unset.error().code == dvv::Errc::Internal);

    // Block-compressed formats are never storage images.
    const auto compressed = context().createImage({.width = 4,
                                                   .height = 4,
                                                   .format = VK_FORMAT_BC1_RGB_UNORM_BLOCK,
                                                   .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT});
    REQUIRE_FALSE(compressed.has_value());
    CHECK(compressed.error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);

    const auto huge = context().createImage({.width = 1U << 20U,
                                             .height = 1,
                                             .format = VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
                                             .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT});
    REQUIRE_FALSE(huge.has_value());
    CHECK(huge.error().code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported);
}

    auto buffer =
        context().createBuffer({.size = 256, .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT}).value();
    auto image = context()
                     .createImage({.width = 8,
                                   .height = 8,
                                   .format = VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
                                   .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT})
                     .value();
    const auto live_before = dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount();
    buffer.reset();
    image.reset();
    CHECK(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == live_before - 2);
    const auto stats = context().retireStats();
    CHECK(stats.retiring == 0);
    CHECK(stats.retired == 2);
    CHECK(stats.freed == 2);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a retired resource waits for the submission that may use it") {
    auto& core = Access::core(context());
    auto buffer =
        context().createBuffer({.size = 256, .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT}).value();
    auto image = context()
                     .createImage({.width = 8,
                                   .height = 8,
                                   .format = VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
                                   .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT})
                     .value();
    const std::uint64_t value = pretendSubmission(core);

    buffer.reset();
    image.reset();
    CHECK(context().retireStats().retiring == 2);
    CHECK(core.collect() == 0);

    signalTimeline(core, value);
    CHECK(core.collect() == 2);
    CHECK(context().retireStats().retiring == 0);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a resource keeps the device after its context is gone") {
    auto own = dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry::create().value();
    const std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry> watch = own;
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> ctx = own->createContext(device()).value();
    auto buffer = ctx->createBuffer({.size = 1024,
                                     .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT,
                                     .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload})
                      .value();
    own.reset();
    ctx.reset();
    // Still usable: the buffer keeps the device, and the device keeps the registry.
    CHECK_FALSE(watch.expired());
    std::ranges::fill(buffer->mapped(), std::byte{0x5a});
    CHECK(buffer->flush().has_value());
    CHECK(watch.lock()->validationMessageCount() == 0);
    buffer.reset();  // frees the buffer, then the device, then the registry
    CHECK(watch.expired());
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "buffers are created and retired from several threads") {
    constexpr int THREADS = 4;
    constexpr int BUFFERS_PER_THREAD = 50;
    constexpr std::uint64_t BUFFERS = std::uint64_t{THREADS} * BUFFERS_PER_THREAD;
    dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext& ctx = context();
    auto& core = Access::core(ctx);
    const auto before = ctx.retireStats();
    std::atomic<int> running{THREADS};
    std::atomic<int> failures{0};
    {
        std::vector<std::jthread> threads;
        threads.reserve(THREADS);
        for (int thread = 0; thread < THREADS; ++thread) {
            threads.emplace_back([&] {
                for (int i = 0; i < BUFFERS_PER_THREAD; ++i) {
                    const auto buffer = ctx.createBuffer(
                        {.size = 256, .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT});
                    if (!buffer) {
                        failures.fetch_add(1);
                    }
                }
                running.fetch_sub(1);
            });
        }
        while (running.load() > 0) {
            static_cast<void>(core.collect());
            if (!ctx.drain()) {
                failures.fetch_add(1);
            }
        }
    }  // joins the threads
    CHECK(failures.load() == 0);
    const auto after = ctx.retireStats();
    CHECK(after.retired - before.retired == BUFFERS);
    CHECK(after.freed - before.freed == BUFFERS);
    CHECK(after.retiring == 0);
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp` (replace the whole file; cases 4 and 5 make a buffer and an image without their context):

```cpp
// Compile-fail cases for dvv-gpu's managed types (spec §9.4). DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE selects one:
//   0  control: a registry created through its factory compiles
//   1  constructing a CreationKey<GpuRegistry> outside the registry
//   2  constructing a GpuRegistry without a key
//   3  constructing an ExecutionContext without a key
//   4  creating a GpuBuffer without its context
//   5  creating a GpuImage without its context
#include <memory>

#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"

int main() {
    const auto registry = dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry::create();
#if DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 1
    dvv::CreationKey<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry> forged(
        dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry>());
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 2
    dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry direct(std::unique_ptr<dvv::gpu::detail::InstanceCore>{});
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 3
    dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext direct(*registry, std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::detail::DeviceCore>{});
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 4
    auto buffer = std::make_shared<dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer>(dvv::gpu::BufferDesc{.size = 4});
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 5
    auto image = std::make_shared<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>(dvv::gpu::ImageDesc{.width = 4});
#endif
    return registry.has_value() ? 0 : 1;
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/tsan.supp` (see the Note above; used by the thread-sanitizer preset only):

```text
# ThreadSanitizer suppressions for the GPU tests (used by the thread-sanitizer preset only).
# Vulkan drivers (here Mesa lavapipe) are not instrumented: they synchronize their own objects with
# primitives ThreadSanitizer cannot see (futex-based locks), so concurrent, valid calls such as
# creating and destroying buffers from several threads are reported as races between the driver's
# malloc and free. Races in dvv-gpu's own code are not affected.
race:libvulkan_lvp.so
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/resources.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// GPU buffers and images (spec §5.3): RAII over a VMA allocation, the Vulkan handle and a debug
// name, created by an ExecutionContext. The destructor hands the handle and its memory to the
// context's retire queue, which frees them once the GPU has finished every submission made so far.

#include <atomic>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <span>
#include <string>

#include <vulkan/vulkan_core.h>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/device_id.h"

struct VmaAllocation_T;

namespace dvv::gpu {

class ExecutionContext;

namespace detail {
class DeviceCore;
struct Access;
}  // namespace detail

// Where a buffer's memory lives and how the host reaches it.
enum class MemoryUsage : std::uint8_t {
    DeviceOnly,    // device-local; no host access
    HostUpload,    // host-visible and mapped; the host writes it sequentially
    HostReadback,  // host-visible and mapped; the host reads (and writes) it in any order
};

struct BufferDesc {
    std::uint64_t size = 0;
    // Vulkan usage flags; VK_BUFFER_USAGE_SHADER_DEVICE_ADDRESS_BIT is always added, because
    // shaders receive buffers by device address (spec §5.4).
    VkBufferUsageFlags usage = 0;
    MemoryUsage memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly;
    std::string name{};  // debug name for tools and validation messages
};

// A 2D image with one mip level and one layer in device-local memory, optimal tiling.
struct ImageDesc {
    std::uint32_t width = 0;
    std::uint32_t height = 0;
    VkFormat format = VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED;
    VkImageUsageFlags usage = 0;
    std::string name{};
};

class GpuBuffer final : public Managed<GpuBuffer> {
public:
    GpuBuffer(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
              BufferDesc desc, VkBuffer buffer, VmaAllocation_T* allocation,
              VkDeviceAddress address, std::span<std::byte> mapped) noexcept;
    // Retires the buffer and its memory (see above).
    ~GpuBuffer();

    [[nodiscard]] const BufferDesc& desc() const noexcept { return desc_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t size() const noexcept { return desc_.size; }
    [[nodiscard]] VkBuffer handle() const noexcept { return buffer_; }
    [[nodiscard]] VkDeviceAddress deviceAddress() const noexcept { return address_; }
    // The mapped memory of a host-visible buffer (HostUpload, HostReadback); empty otherwise.
    [[nodiscard]] std::span<std::byte> mapped() const noexcept { return mapped_; }
    // Makes host writes visible to the device; call after writing mapped() and before the GPU
    // reads. Does nothing on host-coherent memory.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<void> flush(std::uint64_t offset = 0, std::uint64_t size = VK_WHOLE_SIZE);
    // Makes device writes visible to the host; call after the GPU work completed and before
    // reading mapped(). Does nothing on host-coherent memory.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<void> invalidate(std::uint64_t offset = 0,
                                          std::uint64_t size = VK_WHOLE_SIZE);
    [[nodiscard]] DeviceId deviceId() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] DeviceGeneration generation() const noexcept { return generation_; }

private:
    std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core_;
    BufferDesc desc_;
    VkBuffer buffer_;
    VmaAllocation_T* allocation_;
    VkDeviceAddress address_;
    std::span<std::byte> mapped_;
    DeviceGeneration generation_;
};

// Shared from this: the command recorder keeps the images whose layout a submission changes.
class GpuImage final : public Managed<GpuImage>, public std::enable_shared_from_this<GpuImage> {
public:
    GpuImage(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
             ImageDesc desc, VkImage image, VmaAllocation_T* allocation) noexcept;
    // Retires the image and its memory (see above).
    ~GpuImage();

    [[nodiscard]] const ImageDesc& desc() const noexcept { return desc_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t width() const noexcept { return desc_.width; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t height() const noexcept { return desc_.height; }
    [[nodiscard]] VkFormat format() const noexcept { return desc_.format; }
    [[nodiscard]] VkImage handle() const noexcept { return image_; }
    // The layout the recorded commands leave the image in; VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED at first.
    [[nodiscard]] VkImageLayout layout() const noexcept {
        return layout_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    }
    [[nodiscard]] DeviceId deviceId() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] DeviceGeneration generation() const noexcept { return generation_; }

private:
    friend struct detail::Access;  // the command recorder updates the layout

    std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core_;
    ImageDesc desc_;
    VkImage image_;
    VmaAllocation_T* allocation_;
    std::atomic<VkImageLayout> layout_{VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED};
    DeviceGeneration generation_;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/resources.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"

#include <utility>

#include "device_core.h"
#include "vk_result.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

static_assert(ManagedType<GpuBuffer>);
static_assert(ManagedType<GpuImage>);

GpuBuffer::GpuBuffer(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
                     BufferDesc desc, VkBuffer buffer, VmaAllocation_T* allocation,
                     VkDeviceAddress address, std::span<std::byte> mapped) noexcept
    : Managed(std::move(key)),
      core_(std::move(core)),
      desc_(std::move(desc)),
      buffer_(buffer),
      allocation_(allocation),
      address_(address),
      mapped_(mapped),
      generation_(core_->generation()) {}

GpuBuffer::~GpuBuffer() {
    core_->retire(vk::Buffer(buffer_), allocation_);
    core_->collect();
}

Result<void> GpuBuffer::flush(std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t size) {
    return detail::vkCheck(vmaFlushAllocation(core_->allocator(), allocation_, offset, size),
                           "vmaFlushAllocation");
}

Result<void> GpuBuffer::invalidate(std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t size) {
    return detail::vkCheck(vmaInvalidateAllocation(core_->allocator(), allocation_, offset, size),
                           "vmaInvalidateAllocation");
}

DeviceId GpuBuffer::deviceId() const noexcept {
    return core_->id();
}

GpuImage::GpuImage(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
                   ImageDesc desc, VkImage image, VmaAllocation_T* allocation) noexcept
    : Managed(std::move(key)),
      core_(std::move(core)),
      desc_(std::move(desc)),
      image_(image),
      allocation_(allocation),
      generation_(core_->generation()) {}

GpuImage::~GpuImage() {
    core_->retire(vk::Image(image_), allocation_);
    core_->collect();
}

DeviceId GpuImage::deviceId() const noexcept {
    return core_->id();
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/execution_context.cpp` (replace the whole file; the factories follow `retireStats()`):

```cpp
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"

#include <cstddef>
#include <format>
#include <span>
#include <utility>

#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/base/log.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "vk_result.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

static_assert(ManagedType<ExecutionContext>);

ExecutionContext::ExecutionContext(CreationKey<GpuRegistry> key,
                                   std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry,
                                   std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core) noexcept
    : Managed(std::move(key)), registry_(std::move(registry)), core_(std::move(core)) {}

ExecutionContext::~ExecutionContext() {
    if (const auto drained = core_->drain(); !drained) {
        DVV_LOG_ERROR("closing the context of device {} failed: {}", core_->id(),
                      drained.error().describe());
    }
}

DeviceId ExecutionContext::deviceId() const noexcept {
    return core_->id();
}

DeviceGeneration ExecutionContext::generation() const noexcept {
    return core_->generation();
}

const GpuInfo& ExecutionContext::deviceInfo() const noexcept {
    return core_->info();
}

Result<void> ExecutionContext::drain() {
    return core_->drain();
}

RetireStats ExecutionContext::retireStats() const noexcept {
    return core_->retireStats();
}

Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> ExecutionContext::createBuffer(BufferDesc desc) {
    if (desc.size == 0) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("buffer '{}': the size is zero", desc.name));
    }
    core_->collect();  // memory the GPU has finished with is reused first

    const vk::BufferCreateInfo buffer_info{
        .size = desc.size,
        .usage = vk::BufferUsageFlags(desc.usage) | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eShaderDeviceAddress,
        .sharingMode = vk::SharingMode::eExclusive};
    VmaAllocationCreateInfo allocation_info{};
    switch (desc.memory) {
        case MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly:
            allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO_PREFER_DEVICE;
            break;
        case MemoryUsage::HostUpload:
            allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO;
            allocation_info.flags = VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_HOST_ACCESS_SEQUENTIAL_WRITE_BIT |
                                    VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_MAPPED_BIT;
            break;
        case MemoryUsage::HostReadback:
            allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO;
            allocation_info.flags =
                VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_HOST_ACCESS_RANDOM_BIT | VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_MAPPED_BIT;
            break;
    }
    VkBuffer buffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VmaAllocation allocation = nullptr;
    VmaAllocationInfo allocated{};
    DVV_TRY_VOID(detail::vkCheck(
        vmaCreateBuffer(core_->allocator(), &static_cast<const VkBufferCreateInfo&>(buffer_info),
                        &allocation_info, &buffer, &allocation, &allocated),
        "vmaCreateBuffer"));
    const VkDeviceAddress address =
        core_->device().getBufferAddress({.buffer = vk::Buffer(buffer)}, core_->dispatch());
    std::span<std::byte> mapped;
    if (desc.memory != MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly) {
        mapped = {static_cast<std::byte*>(allocated.pMappedData),
                  static_cast<std::size_t>(desc.size)};
    }
    if (!desc.name.empty()) {
        core_->setDebugName(vk::Buffer(buffer), desc.name);
        vmaSetAllocationName(core_->allocator(), allocation, desc.name.c_str());
    }
    try {
        return std::make_shared<GpuBuffer>(
            CreationKey<ExecutionContext>(
                LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<GpuBuffer>(objectId())),
            core_, std::move(desc), buffer, allocation, address, mapped);
    } catch (...) {
        vmaDestroyBuffer(core_->allocator(), buffer, allocation);
        throw;
    }
}

Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>> ExecutionContext::createImage(ImageDesc desc) {
    if (desc.width == 0 || desc.height == 0 || desc.format == VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED ||
        desc.usage == 0) {
        return makeError(
            Errc::Internal,
            std::format("image '{}': width, height, format and usage must be set", desc.name));
    }
    const auto format = static_cast<vk::Format>(desc.format);
    const auto usage = vk::ImageUsageFlags(desc.usage);
    const auto supported =
        core_->physicalDevice().getImageFormatProperties2({.format = format,
                                                           .type = vk::ImageType::e2D,
                                                           .tiling = vk::ImageTiling::eOptimal,
                                                           .usage = usage},
                                                          core_->dispatch());
    if (supported.result == vk::Result::eErrorFormatNotSupported) {
        return makeError(
            Errc::Unsupported,
            std::format("image '{}': device {} does not support {} images with usage {}", desc.name,
                        core_->id(), vk::to_string(format), vk::to_string(usage)));
    }
    DVV_TRY_VOID(detail::vkCheck(supported.result, "vkGetPhysicalDeviceImageFormatProperties2"));
    const vk::Extent3D& max = supported.value.imageFormatProperties.maxExtent;
    if (desc.width > max.width || desc.height > max.height) {
        return makeError(Errc::Unsupported,
                         std::format("image '{}': {}x{} exceeds the largest {} image of device {} "
                                     "({}x{})",
                                     desc.name, desc.width, desc.height, vk::to_string(format),
                                     core_->id(), max.width, max.height));
    }
    core_->collect();

    const vk::ImageCreateInfo image_info{
        .imageType = vk::ImageType::e2D,
        .format = format,
        .extent = {.width = desc.width, .height = desc.height, .depth = 1},
        .mipLevels = 1,
        .arrayLayers = 1,
        .samples = vk::SampleCountFlagBits::e1,
        .tiling = vk::ImageTiling::eOptimal,
        .usage = usage,
        .sharingMode = vk::SharingMode::eExclusive,
        .initialLayout = vk::ImageLayout::eUndefined};
    VmaAllocationCreateInfo allocation_info{};
    allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO_PREFER_DEVICE;
    VkImage image = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VmaAllocation allocation = nullptr;
    DVV_TRY_VOID(detail::vkCheck(
        vmaCreateImage(core_->allocator(), &static_cast<const VkImageCreateInfo&>(image_info),
                       &allocation_info, &image, &allocation, nullptr),
        "vmaCreateImage"));
    if (!desc.name.empty()) {
        core_->setDebugName(vk::Image(image), desc.name);
        vmaSetAllocationName(core_->allocator(), allocation, desc.name.c_str());
    }
    try {
        return std::make_shared<GpuImage>(
            CreationKey<ExecutionContext>(LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<GpuImage>(objectId())),
            core_, std::move(desc), image, allocation);
    } catch (...) {
        vmaDestroyImage(core_->allocator(), image, allocation);
        throw;
    }
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/access.h` (replace the whole file):

```cpp
#pragma once

// Access to the private parts of dvv-gpu's public classes, for its own sources and white-box tests.

#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "instance.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

struct Access {
    [[nodiscard]] static InstanceCore& instance(const GpuRegistry& registry) noexcept {
        return *registry.instance_;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] static DeviceCore& core(const ExecutionContext& context) noexcept {
        return *context.core_;
    }
    // The command recorder tracks the layout its commands leave an image in.
    static void setLayout(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout) noexcept {
        image.layout_.store(layout, std::memory_order_release);
    }
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

```powershell
git -C dvv-gpu add -A
@'
feat: add GpuBuffer and GpuImage with deferred destruction

ctx->createBuffer(BufferDesc) and ctx->createImage(ImageDesc) return managed, shared resources
(authority ExecutionContext, owner recorded in the lifetime registry) that keep the device core
alive.

- GpuBuffer: size, Vulkan usage (shader device address always added), memory usage (DeviceOnly,
  HostUpload, HostReadback), debug name; handle(), deviceAddress(), mapped() span for host-visible
  memory, flush()/invalidate() for non-coherent memory, DeviceId and generation.
- GpuImage: 2D, one mip, one layer, optimal tiling, device-local; format and usage checked against
  the device first (Errc::Unsupported for an unsupported format, usage or size); the tracked
  layout starts as UNDEFINED and is updated by the command recorder through detail::Access.
- Destruction moves the handle and its VMA allocation into the retire queue, tagged with the last
  reserved timeline value, and frees what the timeline has already reached; a resource the GPU
  never used is freed at once. Creating a resource collects finished ones first.

GPU tests cover mapping and device addresses, size and format errors, layout tracking, immediate
and deferred freeing against a host-signalled timeline, a buffer outliving its context, and
resources destroyed on several threads at once;
compile-fail cases cover buffers and images made without their context.

The thread-sanitizer preset suppresses races reported inside the uninstrumented lavapipe driver
(tests/tsan.supp).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C dvv-gpu commit -F -
```

### Task 18: Command recorder and the submit and waiter threads

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h`, `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/submission.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/command_recorder.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/src/submitter.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/submitter.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_test_submission.cpp`
- Modify: `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt` (two sources), `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/execution_context.h` (`submit`, the submitter), `dvv-gpu/src/execution_context.cpp` (constructor, destructor, `submit`, `SubmitOperation`), `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/resources.h` (`GpuBuffer` befriends `detail::Access`), `dvv-gpu/src/access.h` (`Access::core` of a buffer and an image, `Access::submitter`, `Access::hostSignal`), `dvv-gpu/src/device_core.h` (the contract comments of `reserveSubmitValue` and `setSubmitted` name the host signal), `dvv-gpu/src/gpu_registry.cpp` (`createContext` creates the submitter), `dvv-gpu/src/vk.h` (format traits), `dvv-gpu/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (GPU test source), `dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_fixture.h`, `dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_fixture.cpp` (executor and `submitAndWait`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 16): `ExecutionContext` (`Managed<ExecutionContext>`, `std::enable_shared_from_this<ExecutionContext>`, private `std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry_` and `std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core_`, `friend struct detail::Access`, the destructor that drains), `GpuRegistry::createContext(DeviceId, const ContextOptions&)`; `detail::DeviceCore`: `[[nodiscard]] std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lockQueue()`, `[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t reserveSubmitValue() noexcept`, `void setSubmitted(std::uint64_t value, const std::unique_lock<std::mutex>& queue_lock) noexcept`, `lastReservedValue()`, `lastSubmittedValue()`, `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> completedValue() const`, `std::size_t collect() noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] Result<void> drain()`, `retireStats()`, `queue() -> vk::Queue`, `queueFamily() -> std::uint32_t`, `timeline() -> vk::Semaphore`, `device() -> vk::Device`, `dispatch() -> const vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic&`, `id()`, `generation()`, and the reserved-value contract of Task 16 (implemented here; its comment in `src/device_core.h` gains the host signal in Step 3); `detail::Access::core(const ExecutionContext&)`; the fixture `GpuTest` (`registry()`, `device()`, `context()`).
- Consumes (Task 17): `GpuBuffer` (`desc()`, `size()`, `handle()`, `mapped()`, `flush()`, `invalidate()`, `generation()`, private `std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core_`), `GpuImage` (`desc()`, `width()`, `height()`, `format()`, `handle()`, `layout()`, `generation()`, private `core_`, `std::enable_shared_from_this<GpuImage>`, `friend struct detail::Access`), `BufferDesc`, `ImageDesc`, `MemoryUsage`, `ExecutionContext::createBuffer()`, `createImage()`, `retireStats()`; `static void detail::Access::setLayout(GpuImage&, VkImageLayout) noexcept`; the GPU test source list of `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (ending with `gpu_test_resources.cpp)`).
- Consumes (Task 12): `[[nodiscard]] Result<void> detail::vkCheck(vk::Result|VkResult, std::string_view what, std::source_location = current())`, `[[nodiscard]] std::unexpected<Error> detail::vkError(vk::Result|VkResult, std::string_view what, std::source_location = current())` (`src/vk_result.h`); `src/vk.h`.
- Consumes (dvv-base): `IExecutor` (`virtual void post(std::move_only_function<void()> work)`: never runs the work inline; when it throws, the work was not queued), `Task<T>`, `syncWait(Task<T>)`, `ThreadPool`, `[[nodiscard]] static Result<std::shared_ptr<ThreadPool>> ExecutorFactory::makeThreadPool(std::size_t thread_count = 0, std::source_location = current())`, `Error` (default constructible; `code`, `message`), `makeError`, `DVV_TRY`, `DVV_TRY_VOID`, `DVV_LOG_ERROR`.
- Produces (C++, public, `dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu`; Vulkan C types only): `struct RawCommands { VkCommandBuffer commandBuffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE; VkDevice device = VK_NULL_HANDLE; PFN_vkGetDeviceProcAddr getDeviceProcAddr = nullptr; };` and `class CommandRecorder` (not copyable or movable; valid only during the record function) with `void fillBuffer(const GpuBuffer& buffer, std::uint32_t value, std::uint64_t offset = 0, std::uint64_t size = VK_WHOLE_SIZE)`, `void copyBuffer(const GpuBuffer& source, const GpuBuffer& destination)` (the whole source), `void copyBuffer(const GpuBuffer& source, std::uint64_t source_offset, const GpuBuffer& destination, std::uint64_t destination_offset, std::uint64_t size)`, `void copyBufferToImage(const GpuBuffer& source, GpuImage& destination, std::uint64_t source_offset = 0)`, `void copyImageToBuffer(GpuImage& source, const GpuBuffer& destination, std::uint64_t destination_offset = 0)` (whole 2D image, tightly packed), `void transition(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout)` (to `GENERAL`, `TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL` or `TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL` with that transfer usage, or `SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL` with `SAMPLED` usage; any other layout fails the recorder), `[[nodiscard]] RawCommands raw()` (the automatic barriers cover compute and transfer stages only), `[[nodiscard]] bool failed() const noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] const std::optional<Error>& error() const noexcept`. Private, for the commands Task 21 adds (`dispatch`): constructor `CommandRecorder(detail::DeviceCore& core, VkCommandBuffer command_buffer) noexcept` (`friend class detail::Submitter`), `void finish()`, `void applyLayouts() noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] bool usable() const noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] bool check(bool condition, const char* command, const std::string& problem)`, `[[nodiscard]] bool checkBuffer(const char* command, const GpuBuffer& buffer, VkBufferUsageFlags usage, std::uint64_t offset, std::uint64_t size)`, `[[nodiscard]] bool checkImage(const char* command, const GpuImage& image, VkImageUsageFlags usage)`, `[[nodiscard]] bool checkFormat(const char* command, const GpuImage& image)` (image copies and transitions handle single-plane colour formats with 1x1 texel blocks only: no depth, stencil, multi-planar or block-compressed formats), `void fail(std::string message)`, `[[nodiscard]] VkImageLayout layoutOf(const GpuImage& image) const noexcept`, `void barrier(GpuImage* image = nullptr, VkImageLayout layout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED)` (the automatic global barrier, combined with a layout change), `VkImageLayout prepareTransfer(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout)`, members `detail::DeviceCore* core_`, `VkCommandBuffer command_buffer_`, `std::vector<std::pair<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>, VkImageLayout>> layouts_`, `std::optional<Error> error_`. A new command follows the pattern `if (!usable() || !check...(...)) return; barrier(); <record with core_->dispatch()>`.
- Produces (C++, public, `dvv/gpu/submission.h`): `using RecordFunction = std::move_only_function<void(CommandRecorder&)>;` and `class [[nodiscard]] SubmitOperation` (not copyable or movable; lazy) with `bool await_ready() const noexcept` (false), `void await_suspend(std::coroutine_handle<> continuation)` (queues the submission; throws `std::bad_alloc` when it cannot), `Result<std::uint64_t> await_resume() noexcept`; private constructor `SubmitOperation(std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context, IExecutor& resume_on, RecordFunction record) noexcept` (`friend class ExecutionContext`); it holds the context while awaited; there is no cancellation: the awaiting coroutine must not be destroyed while it is suspended on the operation. In `dvv/gpu/execution_context.h` (which now includes `dvv/base/executor.h` and `submission.h`): `[[nodiscard]] SubmitOperation ExecutionContext::submit(IExecutor& resume_on, RecordFunction record)`, used as `Result<std::uint64_t> value = co_await ctx->submit(executor, [&](CommandRecorder& recorder) { ... });`; the record function runs on the submit thread and must not block or wait for another submission (the submit thread records one submission at a time; spec §6.5); an empty record function submits only the final host-visibility barrier; the constructor becomes `ExecutionContext(CreationKey<GpuRegistry> key, std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core, std::unique_ptr<detail::Submitter> submitter) noexcept`; new private member `std::unique_ptr<detail::Submitter> submitter_` (declared after `core_`), `friend class SubmitOperation`.
- Produces (C++, private, `src/submitter.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu::detail`): `struct Submission` (`RecordFunction record`, `IExecutor* resumeOn`, `std::coroutine_handle<> continuation`, `Result<std::uint64_t>* result`, `std::uint64_t value`, `bool signalled`, `std::optional<Error> error`, `std::vector<std::pair<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>, VkImageLayout>> images` (the images the recorder kept, moved out of it after the batch), `std::unique_ptr<Submission> next`), `class SubmissionQueue` (intrusive FIFO, `push`/`pop`/`empty`, never allocates), `class Submitter` (`friend struct Access` for the white-box test of the host signal) with `[[nodiscard]] static Result<std::unique_ptr<Submitter>> create(const std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore>& core)` (command pool with `RESET_COMMAND_BUFFER`, starts both threads), `void enqueue(std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission) noexcept`, `void stop() noexcept` (finishes every queued and in-flight submission, joins both threads; idempotent; never called from either thread), destructor (`stop()`, then destroys the command pool). In `src/access.h`: `[[nodiscard]] static DeviceCore& Access::core(const GpuBuffer&) noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] static DeviceCore& Access::core(const GpuImage&) noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] static Submitter& Access::submitter(const ExecutionContext&) noexcept`, `static void Access::hostSignal(Submitter&, Submission&) noexcept` (the last-resort signal; only tests call it, while the submit thread is idle). `src/vk.h` also includes `<vulkan/vulkan_format_traits.hpp>` (`vk::blockSize()`).
- Produces (tests, `tests/gpu_fixture.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu::test`): `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> submitAndWait(ExecutionContext& context, IExecutor& executor, RecordFunction record)` (awaits `submit` in a `Task` and blocks in `syncWait`), `[[nodiscard]] ThreadPool& GpuTest::executor()` (one thread, created on first use, destroyed after the context and before the validation check), `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> GpuTest::submitAndWait(RecordFunction record)`.
- Behaviour (Submitter contract, also stated in `src/submitter.h`): the submit thread takes queued submissions in order, reserves each value with `reserveSubmitValue()` before recording, records (a command buffer whose earlier submission has finished is reused) and submits with `vkQueueSubmit2` and a timeline signal of that value. Every reserved value is signalled: by its batch; else (recording failed, the record function threw, a command buffer could not be begun or allocated, or the queue refused the batch) by a batch with the signal alone; else, as the last resort, by the host (`vkSignalSemaphore`) after waiting until the latest submitted signal is reached, because a host signal must not overtake a pending one (this wait is the one documented blocking call on the submit thread, on a path only queue failures reach). Each signal is reported with `setSubmitted()` under the queue lock. Only a lost device leaves a value unsignalled; its waits then fail instead of hanging. No batch waits on a semaphore, so `vkDeviceWaitIdle` in `drain()` always returns with every submitted value reached. Nothing is released while the queue lock is held, and neither thread releases the last reference to the device core or, apart from the out-of-memory path (where the recorder and the images it kept end on the submit thread, outside the queue lock), to a resource: the waiter posts the completion to `resume_on` (never inline), where the submission (the record function, everything it captured and the images whose layout it changed) is released before the coroutine resumes; the submitter, which keeps the core, is destroyed by the context after both threads are joined. When the executor's `post()` throws (anything, not only `std::exception`), the waiter retries it every millisecond (logged once) instead of resuming the coroutine on its own thread. The waiter thread waits for each value in order and calls `collect()` before it posts. Image layouts recorded by a submission are stored in the images once its batch is on the queue. Errors: a command that cannot be recorded fails the recorder (`Errc::Internal`, message `"<command>: <problem>"`, e.g. `fillBuffer: buffer 'small' (64 bytes) has no 64 bytes at offset 32`, `copyBufferToImage: image 'depth' (4x4 D32Sfloat) has a format the recorder does not handle`, `transition: image 'color' (4x4 R32Uint) cannot move to ColorAttachmentOptimal`); later commands are ignored and `raw()` returns null handles; a throwing record function gives `Errc::Internal` `recording submission <value> failed: <what>`; out of memory outside the record function gives `Errc::OutOfMemory` without a message; in every failure case no command of that submission runs. `~ExecutionContext` calls `submitter_->stop()`, then drains.

Note: synchronisation follows the design ("BDA synchronisation"): before every command (and before `raw()`) a global memory barrier makes earlier compute and transfer writes visible to compute and transfer reads and writes (combined with the image's layout change for a transition or a transfer that needs one), and `finish()` ends every successful recording with a barrier that makes all writes visible to the host. Image commands use `TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL` / `TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL` unless the image already is in that layout or in `GENERAL`. `vk.h` stays private: `raw()` hands out Vulkan C handles and `vkGetDeviceProcAddr`, so public headers still need no Vulkan-Hpp configuration. `GpuBuffer::flush()` and `invalidate()` stay explicit (Task 17 left the decision to this task): submissions do not flush or invalidate host-visible memory; the tests flush an upload before submitting and invalidate a readback after the await.

Note: the two forward risks found in Task 16's review are closed by the contract above: `~DeviceCore` takes the queue lock, and neither thread ever holds the last core reference (the submitter keeps the core and is destroyed only after both threads are joined; record functions and the resources they capture are released on the executor), so no core destructor runs under the queue lock; and `drain()` cannot hang on a semaphore wait because no batch waits on one. The case "a resource dropped while its submission is recorded waits for it" proves the reserved-value tagging of Task 16 end to end: a buffer released while the record function runs stays in the retire queue (`collect()` frees nothing) until the waiter thread has seen the submission finish.

Note: review fixes folded into this task (all in the prototype commit the files below come from): `src/device_core.h`'s contract comments for `reserveSubmitValue()` and `setSubmitted()` now name the last-resort host signal, so both headers state the same contract (Step 3 edits them; do not leave them as Task 16 wrote them); the recorder rejects image formats and target layouts it cannot record correctly instead of recording invalid commands; the images a submission's recorder kept are released with the submission on its executor; the waiter's retry around `post()` catches every exception; the public comments state that the record function must not block, that `raw()`'s automatic barriers cover compute and transfer stages only, and that an awaited operation cannot be cancelled. Two cases replace or add coverage: "a context released during a submission closes on the executor" makes the awaiting operation hold the last context reference, so `~ExecutionContext` (`stop()`, both joins, `drain()`) runs on the executor thread under TSan; "the host signals a reserved value once the earlier signals are reached" runs the last-resort host signal white-box through `Access::hostSignal` (the queue failures that reach it cannot be provoked otherwise).

Every Windows command below that runs the GPU tests sets `DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync` itself, in the same command, as in Task 15, and removes it afterwards.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_test_submission.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <latch>
#include <memory>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

#include "access.h"
#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executors.h"
#include "dvv/base/task.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "gpu_fixture.h"
#include "submitter.h"

using dvv::gpu::BufferDesc;
using dvv::gpu::CommandRecorder;
using dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer;
using dvv::gpu::MemoryUsage;
using dvv::gpu::detail::Access;
using dvv::gpu::test::GpuTest;

namespace {

constexpr VkBufferUsageFlags TRANSFER =
    VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT | VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT;

std::vector<std::uint32_t> read(GpuBuffer& buffer) {
    REQUIRE(buffer.invalidate().has_value());
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> values(buffer.size() / sizeof(std::uint32_t));
    std::memcpy(values.data(), buffer.mapped().data(), values.size() * sizeof(std::uint32_t));
    return values;
}

void write(GpuBuffer& buffer, const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& values) {
    REQUIRE(buffer.size() >= values.size() * sizeof(std::uint32_t));
    std::memcpy(buffer.mapped().data(), values.data(), values.size() * sizeof(std::uint32_t));
    REQUIRE(buffer.flush().has_value());
}

// Suspends on the submission, then reports the thread the coroutine resumed on.
dvv::Task<std::thread::id> resumedThread(dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext& context,
                                         dvv::IExecutor& executor,
                                         dvv::Result<std::uint64_t>& result) {
    result = co_await context.submit(executor, [](CommandRecorder&) {});
    co_return std::this_thread::get_id();
}

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a buffer is filled and copied on the GPU") {
    auto device_only = context()
                           .createBuffer({.size = 4096,
                                          .usage = TRANSFER,
                                          .memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly,
                                          .name = "filled"})
                           .value();
    auto readback = context()
                        .createBuffer({.size = 4096,
                                       .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                                       .memory = MemoryUsage::HostReadback,
                                       .name = "readback"})
                        .value();
    const auto value = submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
        recorder.fillBuffer(*device_only, 0xa5a5a5a5U);
        recorder.fillBuffer(*device_only, 7, 1024, 1024);  // overwrites part of the first fill
        recorder.copyBuffer(*device_only, *readback);
    });
    REQUIRE(value.has_value());
    CHECK(*value > 0);

    const std::vector<std::uint32_t> values = read(*readback);
    REQUIRE(values.size() == 1024);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < values.size(); ++i) {
        const std::uint32_t expected = i >= 256 && i < 512 ? 7U : 0xa5a5a5a5U;
        if (values[i] != expected) {
            FAIL("value " << i << " is " << values[i] << ", expected " << expected);
        }
    }
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a buffer goes through an R32_UINT image and back") {
    constexpr std::uint32_t WIDTH = 33;
    constexpr std::uint32_t HEIGHT = 17;
    constexpr std::uint64_t BYTES = std::uint64_t{WIDTH} * HEIGHT * sizeof(std::uint32_t);
    auto upload = context()
                      .createBuffer({.size = BYTES,
                                     .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
                                     .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload,
                                     .name = "texels"})
                      .value();
    auto readback = context()
                        .createBuffer({.size = BYTES,
                                       .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                                       .memory = MemoryUsage::HostReadback,
                                       .name = "texels back"})
                        .value();
    auto image = context()
                     .createImage({.width = WIDTH,
                                   .height = HEIGHT,
                                   .format = VK_FORMAT_R32_UINT,
                                   .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT |
                                            VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                                   .name = "texel image"})
                     .value();
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> texels(std::size_t{WIDTH} * HEIGHT);
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < texels.size(); ++i) {
        texels[i] = i * 7 + 1;
    }
    write(*upload, texels);

    REQUIRE(submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
                recorder.copyBufferToImage(*upload, *image);    // moves it to TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL
                recorder.copyImageToBuffer(*image, *readback);  // and on to TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL
            }).has_value());
    CHECK(read(*readback) == texels);
    CHECK(image->layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL);

    // In GENERAL, both copies use the image as it is.
    REQUIRE(submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
                recorder.transition(*image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL);
                recorder.fillBuffer(*readback, 0);
                recorder.copyImageToBuffer(*image, *readback);
            }).has_value());
    CHECK(image->layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL);
    CHECK(read(*readback) == texels);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "the awaiting coroutine resumes on the given executor") {
    std::thread::id pool_thread;
    std::latch probed(1);
    executor().post([&] {
        pool_thread = std::this_thread::get_id();
        probed.count_down();
    });
    probed.wait();

    dvv::Result<std::uint64_t> first;
    dvv::Result<std::uint64_t> second;
    CHECK(dvv::syncWait(resumedThread(context(), executor(), first)) == pool_thread);
    CHECK(dvv::syncWait(resumedThread(context(), executor(), second)) == pool_thread);
    REQUIRE(first.has_value());
    REQUIRE(second.has_value());
    CHECK(*second == *first + 1);
    CHECK(Access::core(context()).completedValue().value() >= *second);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "resources captured by the record function are freed after the GPU") {
    auto buffer =
        context().createBuffer({.size = 256, .usage = TRANSFER, .name = "captured"}).value();
    const std::weak_ptr<GpuBuffer> watch = buffer;
    const auto before = context().retireStats();
    REQUIRE(submitAndWait([buffer = std::move(buffer)](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
                recorder.fillBuffer(*buffer, 3);
            }).has_value());
    // Released on the executor once the GPU had finished, so freed at once.
    CHECK(watch.expired());
    const auto after = context().retireStats();
    CHECK(after.retired == before.retired + 1);
    CHECK(after.freed == before.freed + 1);
    CHECK(after.retiring == 0);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a resource dropped while its submission is recorded waits for it") {
    auto& core = Access::core(context());
    auto buffer = context()
                      .createBuffer({.size = 256,
                                     .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                                     .name = "dropped while recording"})
                      .value();
    GpuBuffer* const borrowed = buffer.get();
    std::latch recording(1);
    std::latch dropped(1);
    dvv::Result<std::uint64_t> result;
    std::jthread submitting([&] {
        result = submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
            recorder.fillBuffer(*borrowed, 1);
            recording.count_down();
            dropped.wait();  // the test drops the buffer meanwhile
        });
    });
    recording.wait();
    // The recorded fill uses the buffer: it is retired for the submission being recorded, not
    // freed at once.
    buffer.reset();
    CHECK(context().retireStats().retiring == 1);
    CHECK(core.collect() == 0);
    dropped.count_down();
    submitting.join();
    REQUIRE(result.has_value());
    // The waiter freed it once the submission had finished.
    CHECK(context().retireStats().retiring == 0);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a record function that throws fails only its own submission") {
    auto& core = Access::core(context());
    auto readback = context()
                        .createBuffer({.size = 64,
                                       .usage = TRANSFER,
                                       .memory = MemoryUsage::HostReadback,
                                       .name = "untouched"})
                        .value();
    write(*readback, std::vector<std::uint32_t>(16, 5));
    const std::uint64_t reserved_before = core.lastReservedValue();

    const auto failed = submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
        recorder.fillBuffer(*readback, 9);
        throw std::runtime_error("broken recording");
    });
    REQUIRE_FALSE(failed.has_value());
    CHECK(failed.error().code == dvv::Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(failed.error().message.find("broken recording") != std::string::npos);
    // Its value was signalled without running the fill.
    CHECK(core.completedValue().value() == reserved_before + 1);
    CHECK(read(*readback) == std::vector<std::uint32_t>(16, 5));

    const auto next =
        submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) { recorder.fillBuffer(*readback, 9); });
    REQUIRE(next.has_value());
    CHECK(*next == reserved_before + 2);
    CHECK(read(*readback) == std::vector<std::uint32_t>(16, 9));
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a command that cannot be recorded fails the submission") {
    auto small = context()
                     .createBuffer({.size = 64,
                                    .usage = TRANSFER,
                                    .memory = MemoryUsage::HostReadback,
                                    .name = "small"})
                     .value();
    auto no_transfer =
        context()
            .createBuffer(
                {.size = 64, .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT, .name = "storage only"})
            .value();
    write(*small, std::vector<std::uint32_t>(16, 1));

    bool failed = false;
    bool raw_empty = false;
    const auto out_of_range = submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
        recorder.fillBuffer(*small, 2);
        recorder.fillBuffer(*small, 3, 32, 64);  // ends 32 bytes past the buffer
        failed = recorder.failed();
        recorder.fillBuffer(*small, 4);  // ignored: the recorder has failed
        raw_empty = recorder.raw().commandBuffer == VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    });
    CHECK(failed);
    CHECK(raw_empty);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(out_of_range.has_value());
    CHECK(out_of_range.error().code == dvv::Errc::Internal);
    CHECK(out_of_range.error().message ==
          "fillBuffer: buffer 'small' (64 bytes) has no 64 bytes at offset 32");
    CHECK(read(*small) == std::vector<std::uint32_t>(16, 1));  // nothing ran

    const auto usage = submitAndWait(
        [&](CommandRecorder& recorder) { recorder.copyBuffer(*small, *no_transfer); });
    REQUIRE_FALSE(usage.has_value());
    CHECK(usage.error().message ==
          "copyBuffer: buffer 'storage only' (64 bytes) lacks the usage { TransferDst }");

    const auto alignment =
        submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) { recorder.fillBuffer(*small, 0, 2, 8); });
    REQUIRE_FALSE(alignment.has_value());
    CHECK(alignment.error().message ==
          "fillBuffer: offset 2 and size 8 must be multiples of 4, the size not 0");

    // A buffer of another context (another device as far as the recorder is concerned).
    auto other = registry().createContext(device()).value();
    auto foreign = other->createBuffer({.size = 64, .usage = TRANSFER, .name = "foreign"}).value();
    const auto device_check =
        submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) { recorder.copyBuffer(*foreign, *small); });
    REQUIRE_FALSE(device_check.has_value());
    CHECK(device_check.error().message ==
          "copyBuffer: buffer 'foreign' (64 bytes) belongs to another device");
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "an empty record function only waits for the earlier work") {
    const auto first = submitAndWait([](CommandRecorder&) {});
    const auto second = submitAndWait(nullptr);
    REQUIRE(first.has_value());
    REQUIRE(second.has_value());
    CHECK(*second == *first + 1);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "raw commands get the command buffer after the automatic barrier") {
    auto buffer = context()
                      .createBuffer({.size = 64,
                                     .usage = TRANSFER,
                                     .memory = MemoryUsage::HostReadback,
                                     .name = "raw fill"})
                      .value();
    bool recorded = false;
    REQUIRE(submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
                recorder.fillBuffer(*buffer, 1);
                const dvv::gpu::RawCommands raw = recorder.raw();
                if (raw.commandBuffer == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
                    return;
                }
                const auto fill = reinterpret_cast<PFN_vkCmdFillBuffer>(
                    raw.getDeviceProcAddr(raw.device, "vkCmdFillBuffer"));
                if (fill != nullptr) {
                    fill(raw.commandBuffer, buffer->handle(), 0, VK_WHOLE_SIZE, 6);
                    recorded = true;
                }
            }).has_value());
    CHECK(recorded);
    CHECK(read(*buffer) == std::vector<std::uint32_t>(16, 6));
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a context released during a submission closes on the executor") {
    auto ctx = registry().createContext(device()).value();
    const std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> watch = ctx;
    auto buffer = ctx->createBuffer({.size = 64,
                                     .usage = TRANSFER,
                                     .memory = MemoryUsage::HostReadback,
                                     .name = "last work"})
                      .value();
    dvv::IExecutor& pool = executor();
    std::latch recording(1);
    std::latch released(1);
    dvv::Result<std::uint64_t> result;
    std::jthread submitting([&, &context = *ctx] {
        result = dvv::gpu::test::submitAndWait(context, pool, [&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
            recorder.fillBuffer(*buffer, 3);
            recording.count_down();
            released.wait();
        });
    });
    recording.wait();
    ctx.reset();  // the awaiting operation now holds the last reference
    CHECK_FALSE(watch.expired());
    released.count_down();
    submitting.join();  // the context was closed on the executor thread before the task ended
    REQUIRE(result.has_value());
    CHECK(watch.expired());
    CHECK(read(*buffer) == std::vector<std::uint32_t>(16, 3));
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "image commands the recorder cannot handle fail the submission") {
    auto texels = context()
                      .createBuffer({.size = 64,
                                     .usage = TRANSFER,
                                     .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload,
                                     .name = "texels"})
                      .value();
    auto depth = context()
                     .createImage({.width = 4,
                                   .height = 4,
                                   .format = VK_FORMAT_D32_SFLOAT,
                                   .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                                   .name = "depth"})
                     .value();
    const auto depth_copy = submitAndWait(
        [&](CommandRecorder& recorder) { recorder.copyBufferToImage(*texels, *depth); });
    REQUIRE_FALSE(depth_copy.has_value());
    CHECK(depth_copy.error().message ==
          "copyBufferToImage: image 'depth' (4x4 D32Sfloat) has a format the recorder does not "
          "handle");

    auto color = context()
                     .createImage({.width = 4,
                                   .height = 4,
                                   .format = VK_FORMAT_R32_UINT,
                                   .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                                   .name = "color"})
                     .value();
    const auto attachment = submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
        recorder.transition(*color, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL);
    });
    REQUIRE_FALSE(attachment.has_value());
    CHECK(attachment.error().message ==
          "transition: image 'color' (4x4 R32Uint) cannot move to ColorAttachmentOptimal");

    const auto missing_usage = submitAndWait([&](CommandRecorder& recorder) {
        recorder.transition(*color, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL);
    });
    REQUIRE_FALSE(missing_usage.has_value());
    CHECK(missing_usage.error().message ==
          "transition: image 'color' (4x4 R32Uint) lacks the usage { TransferSrc }");
    CHECK(color->layout() == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED);  // nothing ran
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest,
                  "the host signals a reserved value once the earlier signals are reached") {
    // White-box: the last resort of the submit thread when the queue refuses even the signal.
    auto& core = Access::core(context());
    const auto first = submitAndWait([](CommandRecorder&) {});
    REQUIRE(first.has_value());

    dvv::gpu::detail::Submission submission;
    submission.value = core.reserveSubmitValue();  // the submit thread is idle
    Access::hostSignal(Access::submitter(context()), submission);
    CHECK(submission.signalled);
    CHECK(core.completedValue().value() == submission.value);
    CHECK(core.lastSubmittedValue() == submission.value);

    const auto next = submitAndWait([](CommandRecorder&) {});
    REQUIRE(next.has_value());
    CHECK(*next == submission.value + 1);
}
```

```cpp
#pragma once

namespace dvv::gpu::test {

// co_await context.submit(executor, record) from a coroutine; blocks until it has resumed.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> submitAndWait(ExecutionContext& context, IExecutor& executor,
                                                  RecordFunction record);

// Base of every GPU test (TEST_CASE_FIXTURE): a registry configured from the environment
// (DVV_VK_VALIDATION, DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED) and, on first use, a context on the best device
// and a one-thread executor for the coroutines that await submissions. At the end of the test the
// context is destroyed first (destroying a device reports leaked Vulkan objects), then no
// validation message may have been counted.
class GpuTest {
public:
    GpuTest();
    GpuTest(const GpuTest&) = delete;
    GpuTest& operator=(const GpuTest&) = delete;
    GpuTest(GpuTest&&) = delete;
    GpuTest& operator=(GpuTest&&) = delete;
    ~GpuTest();

    [[nodiscard]] GpuRegistry& registry() const noexcept { return *registry_; }
    // The best usable device (main() checked that one exists).
    [[nodiscard]] DeviceId device() const;
    // A context on device(), created on first use.
    [[nodiscard]] ExecutionContext& context();
    // The executor submissions resume on, created on first use.
    [[nodiscard]] ThreadPool& executor();
    // submitAndWait(context(), executor(), record).
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> submitAndWait(RecordFunction record);

private:
    std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry_;
    std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context_;
    std::shared_ptr<ThreadPool> executor_;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::test
```

```cpp
#include "gpu_fixture.h"

#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <utility>

#include "dvv/base/task.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::test {

namespace {

Task<Result<std::uint64_t>> submitTask(ExecutionContext& context, IExecutor& executor,
                                       RecordFunction record) {
    co_return co_await context.submit(executor, std::move(record));
}

}  // namespace

Result<std::uint64_t> submitAndWait(ExecutionContext& context, IExecutor& executor,
                                    RecordFunction record) {
    return syncWait(submitTask(context, executor, std::move(record)));
}

GpuTest::GpuTest() {
    auto registry = GpuRegistry::create();
    if (!registry) {
        FAIL(registry.error().describe());
    }
    registry_ = std::move(*registry);
}

GpuTest::~GpuTest() {
    context_.reset();
    executor_.reset();
    CHECK(registry_->validationMessageCount() == 0);
}

DeviceId GpuTest::device() const {
    if (const auto best = registry_->bestDevice()) {
        return *best;
    }
    FAIL("no usable Vulkan device");
    return DeviceId(0);  // not reached: FAIL throws
}

ExecutionContext& GpuTest::context() {
    if (!context_) {
        auto context = registry_->createContext(device());
        if (!context) {
            FAIL(context.error().describe());
        }
        context_ = std::move(*context);
    }
    return *context_;
}

ThreadPool& GpuTest::executor() {
    if (!executor_) {
        executor_ = ExecutorFactory::makeThreadPool(1).value();
    }
    return *executor_;
}

Result<std::uint64_t> GpuTest::submitAndWait(RecordFunction record) {
    return test::submitAndWait(context(), executor(), std::move(record));
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::test
```
