- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Records the commands of one submission (spec §4, §5.3). The submit thread passes a recorder to
// the function given to ExecutionContext::submit(); it is valid only during that call.
//
// Synchronisation is automatic and conservative: before every command a global memory barrier
// makes all earlier writes of compute shaders and transfers (also of earlier submissions) visible
// to it, and the end of every submission makes all writes visible to the host. Shaders reach
// buffers through device addresses, which synchronisation validation cannot see, so commands never
// rely on the caller for barriers.
//
// A command that cannot be recorded (a resource of another device, a range outside a buffer, a
// missing usage flag, ...) is a programming error: the recorder records nothing more, and the
// submission signals its timeline value without running any command and completes with that error
// (Errc::Internal).

#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include <vulkan/vulkan_core.h>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

namespace detail {
class DeviceCore;
class Submitter;
}  // namespace detail

// The escape hatch for commands the recorder does not offer (CommandRecorder::raw()). Device
// functions are loaded with getDeviceProcAddr(device, "vkCmd...").
struct RawCommands {
    VkCommandBuffer commandBuffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDevice device = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    PFN_vkGetDeviceProcAddr getDeviceProcAddr = nullptr;
};

class CommandRecorder {
public:
    CommandRecorder(const CommandRecorder&) = delete;
    CommandRecorder& operator=(const CommandRecorder&) = delete;
    CommandRecorder(CommandRecorder&&) = delete;
    CommandRecorder& operator=(CommandRecorder&&) = delete;
    ~CommandRecorder() = default;

    // Fills `size` bytes from `offset` with a 32-bit value (VK_WHOLE_SIZE: to the end, rounded
    // down to a multiple of 4). The buffer needs VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT; offset and size
    // are multiples of 4.
    void fillBuffer(const GpuBuffer& buffer, std::uint32_t value, std::uint64_t offset = 0,
                    std::uint64_t size = VK_WHOLE_SIZE);
    // Copies the whole source buffer to the start of the destination.
    void copyBuffer(const GpuBuffer& source, const GpuBuffer& destination);
    // Copies `size` bytes. Source needs TRANSFER_SRC, destination TRANSFER_DST usage.
    void copyBuffer(const GpuBuffer& source, std::uint64_t source_offset,
                    const GpuBuffer& destination, std::uint64_t destination_offset,
                    std::uint64_t size);
    // Copies tightly packed texels from the buffer into the whole image. The image moves to
    // TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL first unless it is in that layout or GENERAL. Image copies and
    // transitions handle single-plane colour formats with 1x1 texel blocks only (no depth,
    // stencil, multi-planar or block-compressed formats).
    void copyBufferToImage(const GpuBuffer& source, GpuImage& destination,
                           std::uint64_t source_offset = 0);
    // Copies the whole image into the buffer, tightly packed. The image moves to
    // TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL first unless it is in that layout or GENERAL.
    void copyImageToBuffer(GpuImage& source, const GpuBuffer& destination,
                           std::uint64_t destination_offset = 0);
    // Moves the image to a layout (nothing when it is already there): GENERAL,
    // TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL or TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL (the image needs that transfer usage) or
    // SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL (it needs SAMPLED usage); other layouts fail the recorder.
    // GpuImage::layout() reports the new layout once the submission is on the queue.
    void transition(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout);

    // The command buffer for commands of one's own, after the automatic barrier. The caller
    // synchronises between its own raw commands, and must not change image layouts (the recorder
    // would not know). The automatic barriers cover compute and transfer stages only; raw commands
    // in other stages need their own barriers before and after them. Empty (null handles) once
    // the recorder has failed.
    [[nodiscard]] RawCommands raw();

    // Whether a command could not be recorded; the submission will complete with error().
    [[nodiscard]] bool failed() const noexcept { return error_.has_value(); }
    [[nodiscard]] const std::optional<Error>& error() const noexcept { return error_; }

private:
    friend class detail::Submitter;

    CommandRecorder(detail::DeviceCore& core, VkCommandBuffer command_buffer) noexcept;

    // Records the barrier that makes every write visible to the host (end of the submission).
    void finish();
    // Stores the layouts recorded for each image in the images; after a successful submission.
    void applyLayouts() noexcept;

    // False once a command has failed: nothing more is recorded.
    [[nodiscard]] bool usable() const noexcept;
    // Fails the recorder with "<command>: <problem>" unless the condition holds.
    [[nodiscard]] bool check(bool condition, const char* command, const std::string& problem);
    [[nodiscard]] bool checkBuffer(const char* command, const GpuBuffer& buffer,
                                   VkBufferUsageFlags usage, std::uint64_t offset,
                                   std::uint64_t size);
    [[nodiscard]] bool checkImage(const char* command, const GpuImage& image,
                                  VkImageUsageFlags usage);
    // Single-plane colour formats with 1x1 texel blocks: what the copies and transitions handle.
    [[nodiscard]] bool checkFormat(const char* command, const GpuImage& image);
    void fail(std::string message);

    // The layout the commands recorded so far leave the image in.
    [[nodiscard]] VkImageLayout layoutOf(const GpuImage& image) const noexcept;
    // Global memory barrier, combined with the image's layout change when `image` is set.
    void barrier(GpuImage* image = nullptr, VkImageLayout layout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED);
    // Moves the image to `layout` unless it is in `layout` or GENERAL already; returns the layout
    // the command uses.
    VkImageLayout prepareTransfer(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout);

    detail::DeviceCore* core_;
    VkCommandBuffer command_buffer_;
    // The images whose layout this submission changes, with their latest layout. Owning
    // references: an image dropped by the record function stays valid until applyLayouts().
    std::vector<std::pair<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>, VkImageLayout>> layouts_;
    std::optional<Error> error_;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/submission.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The awaitable of ExecutionContext::submit() (spec §4).

#include <coroutine>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executor.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

class ExecutionContext;

// The record function of a submission; runs on the context's submit thread.
using RecordFunction = std::move_only_function<void(CommandRecorder&)>;

// co_await ctx->submit(executor, record) hands the record function to the submit thread, which
// records and submits it; the waiter thread waits until the GPU has finished, releases the record
// function on the executor and resumes the awaiting coroutine there (never inline). The result is
// the submission's timeline value, or the error that stopped it (recording, submission or the
// device). Nothing happens until the operation is awaited; it keeps the context alive meanwhile.
// There is no cancellation: the awaiting coroutine must not be destroyed while it is suspended
// here (the waiter thread writes the result into its frame and resumes it).
class [[nodiscard]] SubmitOperation {
public:
    SubmitOperation(const SubmitOperation&) = delete;
    SubmitOperation& operator=(const SubmitOperation&) = delete;
    SubmitOperation(SubmitOperation&&) = delete;
    SubmitOperation& operator=(SubmitOperation&&) = delete;
    ~SubmitOperation() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] bool await_ready() const noexcept { return false; }
    // Queues the submission; throws std::bad_alloc when it cannot.
    void await_suspend(std::coroutine_handle<> continuation);
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> await_resume() noexcept { return std::move(result_); }

private:
    friend class ExecutionContext;

    SubmitOperation(std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context, IExecutor& resume_on,
                    RecordFunction record) noexcept;

    std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context_;
    IExecutor* resume_on_;
    RecordFunction record_;
    Result<std::uint64_t> result_{};
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/command_recorder.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <format>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>

#include "access.h"
#include "device_core.h"
#include "vk.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

namespace {

// Every command the recorder records runs in one of these stages.
constexpr vk::PipelineStageFlags2 WORK_STAGES =
    vk::PipelineStageFlagBits2::eComputeShader | vk::PipelineStageFlagBits2::eAllTransfer;
constexpr vk::AccessFlags2 WORK_ACCESS =
    vk::AccessFlagBits2::eMemoryRead | vk::AccessFlagBits2::eMemoryWrite;
constexpr vk::ImageSubresourceRange COLOR_RANGE{.aspectMask = vk::ImageAspectFlagBits::eColor,
                                                .baseMipLevel = 0,
                                                .levelCount = 1,
                                                .baseArrayLayer = 0,
                                                .layerCount = 1};
constexpr vk::ImageSubresourceLayers COLOR_LAYERS{.aspectMask = vk::ImageAspectFlagBits::eColor,
                                                  .mipLevel = 0,
                                                  .baseArrayLayer = 0,
                                                  .layerCount = 1};

std::string describe(const GpuBuffer& buffer) {
    return std::format("buffer '{}' ({} bytes)", buffer.desc().name, buffer.size());
}

std::string describe(const GpuImage& image) {
    return std::format("image '{}' ({}x{} {})", image.desc().name, image.width(), image.height(),
                       vk::to_string(static_cast<vk::Format>(image.format())));
}

std::string layoutName(VkImageLayout layout) {
    return vk::to_string(static_cast<vk::ImageLayout>(layout));
}

// Bytes of the whole image, tightly packed.
std::uint64_t imageBytes(const GpuImage& image) {
    return std::uint64_t{image.width()} * image.height() *
           vk::blockSize(static_cast<vk::Format>(image.format()));
}

}  // namespace

CommandRecorder::CommandRecorder(detail::DeviceCore& core, VkCommandBuffer command_buffer) noexcept
    : core_(&core), command_buffer_(command_buffer) {}

void CommandRecorder::fillBuffer(const GpuBuffer& buffer, std::uint32_t value, std::uint64_t offset,
                                 std::uint64_t size) {
    if (!usable()) {
        return;
    }
    // VK_WHOLE_SIZE: the rest of the buffer, rounded down to whole 32-bit values.
    const std::uint64_t length =
        size != VK_WHOLE_SIZE ? size
                              : (offset < buffer.size() ? (buffer.size() - offset) & ~3ULL : 0);
    if (!check(offset % 4 == 0 && length % 4 == 0 && length > 0, "fillBuffer",
               std::format("offset {} and size {} must be multiples of 4, the size not 0", offset,
                           length)) ||
        !checkBuffer("fillBuffer", buffer, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT, offset, length)) {
        return;
    }
    barrier();
    vk::CommandBuffer(command_buffer_)
        .fillBuffer(vk::Buffer(buffer.handle()), offset, length, value, core_->dispatch());
}

void CommandRecorder::copyBuffer(const GpuBuffer& source, const GpuBuffer& destination) {
    copyBuffer(source, 0, destination, 0, source.size());
}

void CommandRecorder::copyBuffer(const GpuBuffer& source, std::uint64_t source_offset,
                                 const GpuBuffer& destination, std::uint64_t destination_offset,
                                 std::uint64_t size) {
    if (!usable()) {
        return;
    }
    if (!check(size > 0, "copyBuffer", "the size is 0") ||
        !checkBuffer("copyBuffer", source, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT, source_offset, size) ||
        !checkBuffer("copyBuffer", destination, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                     destination_offset, size) ||
        !check(&source != &destination || source_offset + size <= destination_offset ||
                   destination_offset + size <= source_offset,
               "copyBuffer", std::format("the ranges overlap in {}", describe(source)))) {
        return;
    }
    barrier();
    const vk::BufferCopy region{
        .srcOffset = source_offset, .dstOffset = destination_offset, .size = size};
    vk::CommandBuffer(command_buffer_)
        .copyBuffer(vk::Buffer(source.handle()), vk::Buffer(destination.handle()), region,
                    core_->dispatch());
}

void CommandRecorder::copyBufferToImage(const GpuBuffer& source, GpuImage& destination,
                                        std::uint64_t source_offset) {
    if (!usable()) {
        return;
    }
    const std::uint64_t texel = vk::blockSize(static_cast<vk::Format>(destination.format()));
    if (!checkImage("copyBufferToImage", destination, VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT) ||
        !checkFormat("copyBufferToImage", destination) ||
        !check(source_offset % texel == 0, "copyBufferToImage",
               std::format("the offset {} is not a multiple of the texel size {}", source_offset,
                           texel)) ||
        !checkBuffer("copyBufferToImage", source, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT, source_offset,
                     imageBytes(destination))) {
        return;
    }
    const VkImageLayout layout = prepareTransfer(destination, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL);
    const vk::BufferImageCopy region{
        .bufferOffset = source_offset,
        .imageSubresource = COLOR_LAYERS,
        .imageExtent = {.width = destination.width(), .height = destination.height(), .depth = 1}};
    vk::CommandBuffer(command_buffer_)
        .copyBufferToImage(vk::Buffer(source.handle()), vk::Image(destination.handle()),
                           static_cast<vk::ImageLayout>(layout), region, core_->dispatch());
}

void CommandRecorder::copyImageToBuffer(GpuImage& source, const GpuBuffer& destination,
                                        std::uint64_t destination_offset) {
    if (!usable()) {
        return;
    }
    const std::uint64_t texel = vk::blockSize(static_cast<vk::Format>(source.format()));
    if (!checkImage("copyImageToBuffer", source, VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT) ||
        !checkFormat("copyImageToBuffer", source) ||
        !check(destination_offset % texel == 0, "copyImageToBuffer",
               std::format("the offset {} is not a multiple of the texel size {}",
                           destination_offset, texel)) ||
        !checkBuffer("copyImageToBuffer", destination, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,
                     destination_offset, imageBytes(source))) {
        return;
    }
    const VkImageLayout layout = prepareTransfer(source, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL);
    const vk::BufferImageCopy region{
        .bufferOffset = destination_offset,
        .imageSubresource = COLOR_LAYERS,
        .imageExtent = {.width = source.width(), .height = source.height(), .depth = 1}};
    vk::CommandBuffer(command_buffer_)
        .copyImageToBuffer(vk::Image(source.handle()), static_cast<vk::ImageLayout>(layout),
                           vk::Buffer(destination.handle()), region, core_->dispatch());
}

void CommandRecorder::transition(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout) {
    if (!usable()) {
        return;
    }
    // The layouts compute and transfer work uses, with the usage each one needs.
    bool supported = true;
    VkImageUsageFlags usage = 0;
    switch (layout) {
        case VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL: break;
        case VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL: usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT; break;
        case VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL: usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT; break;
        case VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL: usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT; break;
        default: supported = false; break;
    }
    if (!check(supported, "transition",
               std::format("{} cannot move to {}", describe(image), layoutName(layout))) ||
        !checkImage("transition", image, usage) || !checkFormat("transition", image)) {
        return;
    }
    if (layoutOf(image) != layout) {
        barrier(&image, layout);
    }
}

RawCommands CommandRecorder::raw() {
    if (!usable()) {
        return {};
    }
    barrier();
    return {.commandBuffer = command_buffer_,
            .device = static_cast<VkDevice>(core_->device()),
            .getDeviceProcAddr = core_->dispatch().vkGetDeviceProcAddr};
}

void CommandRecorder::finish() {
    const vk::MemoryBarrier2 to_host{.srcStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                                     .srcAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits2::eMemoryWrite,
                                     .dstStageMask = vk::PipelineStageFlagBits2::eHost,
                                     .dstAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits2::eHostRead};
    vk::CommandBuffer(command_buffer_)
        .pipelineBarrier2({.memoryBarrierCount = 1, .pMemoryBarriers = &to_host},
                          core_->dispatch());
}

void CommandRecorder::applyLayouts() noexcept {
    for (const auto& [image, layout] : layouts_) {
        detail::Access::setLayout(*image, layout);
    }
}

bool CommandRecorder::usable() const noexcept {
    return !error_.has_value();
}

bool CommandRecorder::check(bool condition, const char* command, const std::string& problem) {
    if (!condition) {
        fail(std::format("{}: {}", command, problem));
    }
    return condition;
}

bool CommandRecorder::checkBuffer(const char* command, const GpuBuffer& buffer,
                                  VkBufferUsageFlags usage, std::uint64_t offset,
                                  std::uint64_t size) {
    return check(
               &detail::Access::core(buffer) == core_ && buffer.generation() == core_->generation(),
               command, std::format("{} belongs to another device", describe(buffer))) &&
           check((buffer.desc().usage & usage) == usage, command,
                 std::format("{} lacks the usage {}", describe(buffer),
                             vk::to_string(vk::BufferUsageFlags(usage)))) &&
           check(offset <= buffer.size() && size <= buffer.size() - offset, command,
                 std::format("{} has no {} bytes at offset {}", describe(buffer), size, offset));
}

bool CommandRecorder::checkImage(const char* command, const GpuImage& image,
                                 VkImageUsageFlags usage) {
    return check(&detail::Access::core(image) == core_ && image.generation() == core_->generation(),
                 command, std::format("{} belongs to another device", describe(image))) &&
           check((image.desc().usage & usage) == usage, command,
                 std::format("{} lacks the usage {}", describe(image),
                             vk::to_string(vk::ImageUsageFlags(usage))));
}

bool CommandRecorder::checkFormat(const char* command, const GpuImage& image) {
    const auto format = static_cast<vk::Format>(image.format());
    const auto block = vk::blockExtent(format);
    const std::string_view component =
        vk::componentCount(format) > 0 ? vk::componentName(format, 0) : "";
    return check(vk::planeCount(format) == 1 && block[0] == 1 && block[1] == 1 && block[2] == 1 &&
                     component != "D" && component != "S",
                 command,
                 std::format("{} has a format the recorder does not handle", describe(image)));
}

void CommandRecorder::fail(std::string message) {
    if (!error_) {
        error_ = makeError(Errc::Internal, std::move(message)).error();
    }
}

VkImageLayout CommandRecorder::layoutOf(const GpuImage& image) const noexcept {
    const auto recorded = std::ranges::find_if(
        layouts_, [&image](const auto& entry) { return entry.first.get() == &image; });
    return recorded != layouts_.end() ? recorded->second : image.layout();
}

void CommandRecorder::barrier(GpuImage* image, VkImageLayout layout) {
    const vk::MemoryBarrier2 memory{.srcStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                                    .srcAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits2::eMemoryWrite,
                                    .dstStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                                    .dstAccessMask = WORK_ACCESS};
    vk::DependencyInfo dependency{.memoryBarrierCount = 1, .pMemoryBarriers = &memory};
    vk::ImageMemoryBarrier2 layout_change{};
    if (image != nullptr) {
        layout_change = {.srcStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                         .srcAccessMask = vk::AccessFlagBits2::eMemoryWrite,
                         .dstStageMask = WORK_STAGES,
                         .dstAccessMask = WORK_ACCESS,
                         .oldLayout = static_cast<vk::ImageLayout>(layoutOf(*image)),
                         .newLayout = static_cast<vk::ImageLayout>(layout),
                         .srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED,
                         .dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED,
                         .image = vk::Image(image->handle()),
                         .subresourceRange = COLOR_RANGE};
        dependency.imageMemoryBarrierCount = 1;
        dependency.pImageMemoryBarriers = &layout_change;
        // Remembered before recording: if this throws, nothing was recorded.
        const auto recorded = std::ranges::find_if(
            layouts_, [image](const auto& entry) { return entry.first.get() == image; });
        if (recorded != layouts_.end()) {
            recorded->second = layout;
        } else {
            layouts_.emplace_back(image->shared_from_this(), layout);
        }
    }
    vk::CommandBuffer(command_buffer_).pipelineBarrier2(dependency, core_->dispatch());
}

VkImageLayout CommandRecorder::prepareTransfer(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout) {
    const VkImageLayout current = layoutOf(image);
    if (current == layout || current == VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL) {
        barrier();
        return current;
    }
    barrier(&image, layout);
    return layout;
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/submitter.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The submit thread and the waiter thread of an ExecutionContext (spec §4). An internal part of
// the context (exempt from spec §9.4 registration: owned only by its context, created only by
// GpuRegistry::createContext()).
//
// Submit thread: owns the command pool; takes the queued submissions in order, reserves each one's
// timeline value, records it through a CommandRecorder and submits it (vkQueueSubmit2) with a
// signal of that value. A submission that fails to record or to submit still signals its value
// (see the contract below), so the timeline never stalls; it completes with the error. The record
// functions run here one at a time: they must not block (spec §6.5); the host signal's wait below
// is the one documented exception, on a path that only queue failures reach.
// Waiter thread: waits until the timeline reaches each submitted value in order, frees what the
// retire queue can free, and posts the completion to the submission's executor, which releases the
// record function and resumes the awaiting coroutine.
//
// Contract with DeviceCore:
// - Every reserved timeline value is signalled: by its batch, else by a batch with the signal
//   alone, else by the host once every earlier signal is reached (a host signal must not overtake
//   a pending one). Only a lost device leaves a value unsignalled; its waits then fail instead of
//   hanging.
// - No batch waits on a semaphore: nothing submitted can block the queue, so vkDeviceWaitIdle in
//   drain() always returns and every submitted value is reached by then.
// - Nothing is released while the queue lock is held, and neither thread releases the last
//   reference to the device core or, apart from the out-of-memory path, to a resource: the record
//   function, what it captured and the images whose layout the submission changed are released on
//   the submission's executor, and this object, which keeps the core, is destroyed by the context
//   after both threads have been joined (~DeviceCore takes the queue lock and joins nothing).

#include <condition_variable>
#include <coroutine>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <optional>
#include <thread>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executor.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/submission.h"
#include "vk.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

class DeviceCore;
struct Access;

// One ctx->submit() on its way through both threads. Allocated when it is queued, so neither
// thread allocates to hand it on.
struct Submission {
    RecordFunction record{};
    IExecutor* resumeOn = nullptr;
    std::coroutine_handle<> continuation{};
    Result<std::uint64_t>* result = nullptr;  // in the awaiting coroutine's frame

    // Set by the submit thread.
    std::uint64_t value = 0;     // the reserved timeline value
    bool signalled = false;      // value is signalled (its signal is on the queue, or the host's)
    std::optional<Error> error;  // why the submission failed
    // The images whose layout the submission changed (from its recorder), released with it.
    std::vector<std::pair<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>, VkImageLayout>> images;

    std::unique_ptr<Submission> next;  // intrusive queue link
};

// First-in, first-out list of submissions; pushing and popping never allocate.
class SubmissionQueue {
public:
    SubmissionQueue() = default;
    SubmissionQueue(const SubmissionQueue&) = delete;
    SubmissionQueue& operator=(const SubmissionQueue&) = delete;
    SubmissionQueue(SubmissionQueue&&) = delete;
    SubmissionQueue& operator=(SubmissionQueue&&) = delete;
    // One at a time, so a long list cannot exhaust the stack through the links.
    ~SubmissionQueue() {
        while (pop() != nullptr) {
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept { return head_ == nullptr; }
    void push(std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission) noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] std::unique_ptr<Submission> pop() noexcept;

private:
    std::unique_ptr<Submission> head_;
    Submission* tail_ = nullptr;
};

class Submitter {
public:
    // Creates the command pool and starts both threads.
    [[nodiscard]] static Result<std::unique_ptr<Submitter>> create(
        const std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore>& core);

    Submitter(const Submitter&) = delete;
    Submitter& operator=(const Submitter&) = delete;
    Submitter(Submitter&&) = delete;
    Submitter& operator=(Submitter&&) = delete;
    // stop(), then destroys the command pool.
    ~Submitter();

    // Queues a submission for the submit thread.
    void enqueue(std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission) noexcept;
    // Finishes every queued and in-flight submission, then joins both threads. Idempotent; must
    // not be called from either thread (the context is never released there: the record
    // functions and coroutines are released on their executors).
    void stop() noexcept;

    // Use create().
    Submitter(std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore> core, vk::CommandPool pool) noexcept;

private:
    friend struct Access;  // white-box tests reach hostSignal()

    void runSubmitThread();
    void runWaiterThread();
    // Reserves the timeline value, records and submits; the value is signalled whatever fails
    // (submit thread).
    void submitOne(Submission& submission) noexcept;
    void recordAndSubmit(Submission& submission);
    // Submits the recorded commands with the signal, or only the signal when there are no
    // commands or they cannot be submitted. True when the commands were submitted.
    bool submitBatch(Submission& submission, vk::CommandBuffer commands);
    // Submits only the signal, without allocating (the out-of-memory path).
    void signalOnly(Submission& submission) noexcept;
    // The last resort when the queue refuses even the signal alone (see the contract above).
    void hostSignal(Submission& submission) noexcept;
    void markSignalled(Submission& submission,
                       const std::unique_lock<std::mutex>& queue_lock) noexcept;
    // A command buffer whose earlier submission has finished, or a new one (submit thread).
    [[nodiscard]] Result<vk::CommandBuffer> acquireCommandBuffer();
    // Posts the completion to the submission's executor (waiter thread).
    void complete(std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission);

    std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore> core_;
    vk::CommandPool pool_;

    // Submit thread only: command buffers in use (with their timeline value) and free ones.
    struct BusyBuffer {
        std::uint64_t value = 0;
        vk::CommandBuffer buffer;
    };
    std::vector<BusyBuffer> busy_buffers_;
    std::vector<vk::CommandBuffer> free_buffers_;
    std::uint64_t last_signalled_ = 0;  // the latest value whose signal is submitted or done

    std::mutex submit_mutex_;
    std::condition_variable submit_ready_;
    SubmissionQueue queued_;
    bool stopping_ = false;

    std::mutex wait_mutex_;
    std::condition_variable wait_ready_;
    SubmissionQueue in_flight_;
    bool submit_thread_done_ = false;

    std::mutex stop_mutex_;
    std::jthread submit_thread_;
    std::jthread waiter_thread_;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/submitter.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "submitter.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <exception>
#include <format>
#include <string_view>
#include <system_error>
#include <utility>

#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/base/log.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h"
#include "vk_result.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

void SubmissionQueue::push(std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission) noexcept {
    Submission* const last = submission.get();
    if (tail_ == nullptr) {
        head_ = std::move(submission);
    } else {
        tail_->next = std::move(submission);
    }
    tail_ = last;
}

std::unique_ptr<Submission> SubmissionQueue::pop() noexcept {
    std::unique_ptr<Submission> first = std::move(head_);
    if (first) {
        head_ = std::move(first->next);
        if (!head_) {
            tail_ = nullptr;
        }
    }
    return first;
}

Result<std::unique_ptr<Submitter>> Submitter::create(const std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore>& core) {
    const vk::CommandPoolCreateInfo pool_info{
        .flags = vk::CommandPoolCreateFlagBits::eResetCommandBuffer,
        .queueFamilyIndex = core->queueFamily()};
    const auto pool = core->device().createCommandPool(pool_info, nullptr, core->dispatch());
    DVV_TRY_VOID(vkCheck(pool.result, "vkCreateCommandPool"));
    std::unique_ptr<Submitter> submitter;
    try {
        submitter = std::make_unique<Submitter>(core, pool.value);
    } catch (...) {
        core->device().destroyCommandPool(pool.value, nullptr, core->dispatch());
        throw;
    }
    try {
        submitter->submit_thread_ =
            std::jthread([raw = submitter.get()] { raw->runSubmitThread(); });
        submitter->waiter_thread_ =
            std::jthread([raw = submitter.get()] { raw->runWaiterThread(); });
    } catch (const std::system_error& error) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal,
                         std::format("starting the submit threads of device {} failed: {}",
                                     core->id(), error.what()));
    }
    return submitter;
}

Submitter::Submitter(std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore> core, vk::CommandPool pool) noexcept
    : core_(std::move(core)), pool_(pool) {}

Submitter::~Submitter() {
    stop();
    // Every submission has finished (stop() waited for it), so no command buffer is in use.
    core_->device().destroyCommandPool(pool_, nullptr, core_->dispatch());
}

void Submitter::enqueue(std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission) noexcept {
    // Notified under the lock: once it is released, the submission may complete and its
    // coroutine may release the context, and with it this object.
    const std::scoped_lock lock(submit_mutex_);
    queued_.push(std::move(submission));
    submit_ready_.notify_one();
}

void Submitter::stop() noexcept {
    const std::scoped_lock stop_lock(stop_mutex_);
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(submit_mutex_);
        stopping_ = true;
        submit_ready_.notify_all();
    }
    if (submit_thread_.joinable()) {
        submit_thread_.join();  // after it has submitted everything queued
    }
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(wait_mutex_);
        submit_thread_done_ = true;
        wait_ready_.notify_all();
    }
    if (waiter_thread_.joinable()) {
        waiter_thread_.join();  // after it has completed everything submitted
    }
}

void Submitter::runSubmitThread() {
    while (true) {
        std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission;
        {
            std::unique_lock lock(submit_mutex_);
            submit_ready_.wait(lock, [this] { return stopping_ || !queued_.empty(); });
            if (queued_.empty()) {
                return;  // stopping, and everything queued is submitted
            }
            submission = queued_.pop();
        }
        submitOne(*submission);
        const std::scoped_lock lock(wait_mutex_);
        in_flight_.push(std::move(submission));
        wait_ready_.notify_one();
    }
}

void Submitter::submitOne(Submission& submission) noexcept {
    submission.value = core_->reserveSubmitValue();
    try {
        recordAndSubmit(submission);
    } catch (...) {
        // Out of memory outside the record function (which has its own handler). The error has no
        // message, which would need memory too.
        if (!submission.error) {
            Error out_of_memory;
            out_of_memory.code = Errc::OutOfMemory;
            submission.error = std::move(out_of_memory);
        }
        if (!submission.signalled) {
            signalOnly(submission);
        }
    }
}

void Submitter::recordAndSubmit(Submission& submission) {
    auto acquired = acquireCommandBuffer();
    if (!acquired) {
        submission.error = std::move(acquired).error();
        submitBatch(submission, {});
        return;
    }
    const vk::CommandBuffer commands = *acquired;
    const vk::Result begun = commands.begin(
        {.flags = vk::CommandBufferUsageFlagBits::eOneTimeSubmit}, core_->dispatch());
    if (begun != vk::Result::eSuccess) {
        submission.error = vkError(begun, "vkBeginCommandBuffer").error();
        free_buffers_.push_back(commands);  // never reallocates (capacity reserved)
        submitBatch(submission, {});
        return;
    }

    CommandRecorder recorder(*core_, static_cast<VkCommandBuffer>(commands));
    if (submission.record) {
        try {
            submission.record(recorder);
        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
            submission.error =
                makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("recording submission {} "
                                                      "failed: {}",
                                                      submission.value, error.what()))
                    .error();
        } catch (...) {
            submission.error =
                makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("recording submission {} failed with an "
                                                      "unknown exception",
                                                      submission.value))
                    .error();
        }
    }
    if (const std::optional<Error>& failed = recorder.error(); failed && !submission.error) {
        submission.error = *failed;
    }
    if (!submission.error) {
        recorder.finish();
    }
    const vk::Result ended = commands.end(core_->dispatch());
    if (ended != vk::Result::eSuccess && !submission.error) {
        submission.error = vkError(ended, "vkEndCommandBuffer").error();
    }

    // A failed recording submits only its signal: none of its commands run.
    if (submitBatch(submission, submission.error ? vk::CommandBuffer{} : commands)) {
        recorder.applyLayouts();
        busy_buffers_.push_back({.value = submission.value, .buffer = commands});
    } else {
        free_buffers_.push_back(commands);
    }
    // The images the recorder kept are released with the submission, on its executor.
    submission.images = std::move(recorder.layouts_);
}

bool Submitter::submitBatch(Submission& submission, vk::CommandBuffer commands) {
    const vk::CommandBufferSubmitInfo command_info{.commandBuffer = commands};
    const vk::SemaphoreSubmitInfo signal{.semaphore = core_->timeline(),
                                         .value = submission.value,
                                         .stageMask = vk::PipelineStageFlagBits2::eAllCommands};
    vk::SubmitInfo2 batch{.commandBufferInfoCount = commands ? 1U : 0U,
                          .pCommandBufferInfos = &command_info,
                          .signalSemaphoreInfoCount = 1,
                          .pSignalSemaphoreInfos = &signal};
    vk::Result result = vk::Result::eSuccess;
    {
        const auto lock = core_->lockQueue();
        result = core_->queue().submit2(batch, nullptr, core_->dispatch());
        if (result == vk::Result::eSuccess) {
            markSignalled(submission, lock);
            return static_cast<bool>(commands);
        }
        if (commands) {
            // The signal alone keeps the timeline going for the submissions after this one.
            batch.commandBufferInfoCount = 0;
            const vk::Result signalled = core_->queue().submit2(batch, nullptr, core_->dispatch());
            if (signalled == vk::Result::eSuccess) {
                markSignalled(submission, lock);
            }
        }
    }
    if (!submission.signalled) {
        hostSignal(submission);
    }
    if (!submission.error) {
        submission.error = vkError(result, "vkQueueSubmit2").error();
    }
    if (!submission.signalled) {
        DVV_LOG_ERROR("device {}: timeline value {} cannot be signalled: {}", core_->id(),
                      submission.value, vk::to_string(result));
    }
    return false;
}

void Submitter::signalOnly(Submission& submission) noexcept {
    const vk::SemaphoreSubmitInfo signal{.semaphore = core_->timeline(),
                                         .value = submission.value,
                                         .stageMask = vk::PipelineStageFlagBits2::eAllCommands};
    const vk::SubmitInfo2 batch{.signalSemaphoreInfoCount = 1, .pSignalSemaphoreInfos = &signal};
    {
        const auto lock = core_->lockQueue();
        if (core_->queue().submit2(batch, nullptr, core_->dispatch()) == vk::Result::eSuccess) {
            markSignalled(submission, lock);
            return;
        }
    }
    hostSignal(submission);
}

void Submitter::hostSignal(Submission& submission) noexcept {
    // A host signal must not overtake a pending signal: first wait until the latest value whose
    // signal was submitted is reached. On a lost device the wait fails, and nothing can signal.
    const vk::Semaphore timeline = core_->timeline();
    const std::uint64_t pending = last_signalled_;
    const vk::SemaphoreWaitInfo wait{
        .semaphoreCount = 1, .pSemaphores = &timeline, .pValues = &pending};
    if (core_->device().waitSemaphores(wait, UINT64_MAX, core_->dispatch()) !=
        vk::Result::eSuccess) {
        return;
    }
    const vk::SemaphoreSignalInfo signal{.semaphore = timeline, .value = submission.value};
    if (core_->device().signalSemaphore(signal, core_->dispatch()) == vk::Result::eSuccess) {
        const auto lock = core_->lockQueue();
        markSignalled(submission, lock);
    }
}

void Submitter::markSignalled(Submission& submission,
                              const std::unique_lock<std::mutex>& queue_lock) noexcept {
    submission.signalled = true;
    last_signalled_ = submission.value;
    core_->setSubmitted(submission.value, queue_lock);
}

Result<vk::CommandBuffer> Submitter::acquireCommandBuffer() {
    if (free_buffers_.empty() && !busy_buffers_.empty()) {
        DVV_TRY(const std::uint64_t completed, core_->completedValue());
        // Busy buffers are in submission order.
        const auto running = std::ranges::find_if(
            busy_buffers_, [completed](const BusyBuffer& busy) { return busy.value > completed; });
        for (auto busy = busy_buffers_.begin(); busy != running; ++busy) {
            free_buffers_.push_back(busy->buffer);
        }
        busy_buffers_.erase(busy_buffers_.begin(), running);
    }
    if (!free_buffers_.empty()) {
        const vk::CommandBuffer buffer = free_buffers_.back();
        free_buffers_.pop_back();
        return buffer;
    }
    // Both lists can hold every buffer, so moving buffers between them never reallocates.
    const std::size_t total = busy_buffers_.size() + 1;
    busy_buffers_.reserve(total);
    free_buffers_.reserve(total);
    const vk::CommandBufferAllocateInfo allocate_info{
        .commandPool = pool_, .level = vk::CommandBufferLevel::ePrimary, .commandBufferCount = 1};
    vk::CommandBuffer buffer;
    DVV_TRY_VOID(
        vkCheck(core_->device().allocateCommandBuffers(&allocate_info, &buffer, core_->dispatch()),
                "vkAllocateCommandBuffers"));
    return buffer;
}

void Submitter::runWaiterThread() {
    while (true) {
        std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission;
        {
            std::unique_lock lock(wait_mutex_);
            wait_ready_.wait(lock, [this] { return submit_thread_done_ || !in_flight_.empty(); });
            if (in_flight_.empty()) {
                return;  // the submit thread has stopped, and everything submitted is complete
            }
            submission = in_flight_.pop();
        }
        if (submission->signalled) {
            const vk::Semaphore timeline = core_->timeline();
            const vk::SemaphoreWaitInfo wait{
                .semaphoreCount = 1, .pSemaphores = &timeline, .pValues = &submission->value};
            const vk::Result waited =
                core_->device().waitSemaphores(wait, UINT64_MAX, core_->dispatch());
            if (waited != vk::Result::eSuccess && !submission->error) {
                submission->error = vkError(waited, "vkWaitSemaphores").error();
            }
        }
        core_->collect();
        complete(std::move(submission));
    }
}

void Submitter::complete(std::unique_ptr<Submission> submission) {
    IExecutor& executor = *submission->resumeOn;
    // The posted work owns the submission; until a post succeeds, this function does.
    Submission* const posted = submission.release();
    // The executor could not queue the work (out of memory, or whatever its post() throws).
    // Resuming the coroutine here would run it on the waiter thread, so the post is retried until
    // it succeeds.
    bool reported = false;
    const auto retry_later = [this, posted, &reported](std::string_view reason) {
        if (!reported) {
            DVV_LOG_ERROR("device {}: completing submission {} failed, retrying: {}", core_->id(),
                          posted->value, reason);
            reported = true;
        }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
    };
    while (true) {
        try {
            executor.post([posted] {
                std::unique_ptr<Submission> owned(posted);
                const std::coroutine_handle<> continuation = owned->continuation;
                if (owned->error) {
                    *owned->result = std::unexpected(std::move(*owned->error));
                } else {
                    *owned->result = owned->value;
                }
                owned.reset();  // releases the record function and everything it captured
                continuation.resume();
            });
            return;
        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
            retry_later(error.what());
        } catch (...) {
            retry_later("an unknown exception");
        }
    }
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/execution_context.h` (replace the whole file):

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executor.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/device_id.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_info.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/submission.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

class GpuRegistry;

namespace detail {
class DeviceCore;
class Submitter;
struct Access;
}  // namespace detail

// Settings for GpuRegistry::createContext(). None yet; the pipeline cache adds its file.
struct ContextOptions {};

// Counters of the retire queue (spec §5.3, §9.4).
struct RetireStats {
    std::size_t retiring = 0;   // handles waiting until the GPU no longer uses them
    std::uint64_t retired = 0;  // handles ever queued
    std::uint64_t freed = 0;    // handles ever destroyed
};

// One Vulkan device and everything that works on it (spec §5.2): the device with the required
// features, one universal queue (graphics, compute and transfer; access serialised by a mutex),
// the VMA allocator, the timeline semaphore, the retire queue, and the submit and waiter threads.
// Components reach the GPU only through a context. Created by GpuRegistry::createContext(); keeps
// its registry alive.
class ExecutionContext final : public Managed<ExecutionContext>,
                               public std::enable_shared_from_this<ExecutionContext> {
public:
    ExecutionContext(CreationKey<GpuRegistry> key, std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry,
                     std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
                     std::unique_ptr<detail::Submitter> submitter) noexcept;
    // Finishes every submission (recording, submitting, resuming the awaiting coroutines), stops
    // the submit and waiter threads, waits until the device is idle and frees everything retired.
    // Resources that are still alive keep the device until they are destroyed.
    ~ExecutionContext();

    [[nodiscard]] const std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry>& registry() const noexcept {
        return registry_;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] DeviceId deviceId() const noexcept;
    // Device generation (spec §5.2); FIRST_GENERATION until device-lost recovery exists.
    [[nodiscard]] DeviceGeneration generation() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const GpuInfo& deviceInfo() const noexcept;

    // A buffer (spec §5.3). Errc::Internal for a zero size; Vulkan errors as converted by dvv-gpu
    // (e.g. OutOfMemory). Frees retired memory the GPU has finished with first.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> createBuffer(BufferDesc desc);
    // A 2D image (spec §5.3). Errc::Internal when width, height, format or usage is missing;
    // Errc::Unsupported when the device cannot create it (format, usage or size).
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>> createImage(ImageDesc desc);

    // One submission (spec §4): co_await ctx->submit(executor, record). The submit thread calls
    // record with a CommandRecorder, then submits the commands with a signal of the next timeline
    // value; the awaiting coroutine resumes on `resume_on` once the GPU has finished them, with
    // that value or the error that stopped the submission. A record function that throws fails the
    // submission (Errc::Internal); its timeline value is signalled all the same, so later
    // submissions are not held up. The record function, and everything it captures, is released on
    // `resume_on` before the coroutine resumes: capturing the resources a submission uses keeps
    // them alive until the GPU is done with them. An empty record function submits only the final
    // host-visibility barrier. The record function must not block or wait for another submission:
    // the submit thread records one submission at a time. `resume_on` must outlive the operation.
    [[nodiscard]] SubmitOperation submit(IExecutor& resume_on, RecordFunction record);

    // Waits until the device is idle, then frees everything retired.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<void> drain();
    [[nodiscard]] RetireStats retireStats() const noexcept;

private:
    friend struct detail::Access;
    friend class SubmitOperation;

    std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry_;
    std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core_;
    std::unique_ptr<detail::Submitter> submitter_;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

```cpp
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"

#include <cstddef>
#include <format>
#include <span>
#include <utility>

#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/base/log.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "submitter.h"
#include "vk_result.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

static_assert(ManagedType<ExecutionContext>);

ExecutionContext::ExecutionContext(CreationKey<GpuRegistry> key,
                                   std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry,
                                   std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core,
                                   std::unique_ptr<detail::Submitter> submitter) noexcept
    : Managed(std::move(key)),
      registry_(std::move(registry)),
      core_(std::move(core)),
      submitter_(std::move(submitter)) {}

ExecutionContext::~ExecutionContext() {
    submitter_->stop();
    if (const auto drained = core_->drain(); !drained) {
        DVV_LOG_ERROR("closing the context of device {} failed: {}", core_->id(),
                      drained.error().describe());
    }
}

DeviceId ExecutionContext::deviceId() const noexcept {
    return core_->id();
}

DeviceGeneration ExecutionContext::generation() const noexcept {
    return core_->generation();
}

const GpuInfo& ExecutionContext::deviceInfo() const noexcept {
    return core_->info();
}

Result<void> ExecutionContext::drain() {
    return core_->drain();
}

SubmitOperation ExecutionContext::submit(IExecutor& resume_on, RecordFunction record) {
    return {shared_from_this(), resume_on, std::move(record)};
}

SubmitOperation::SubmitOperation(std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context, IExecutor& resume_on,
                                 RecordFunction record) noexcept
    : context_(std::move(context)), resume_on_(&resume_on), record_(std::move(record)) {}

void SubmitOperation::await_suspend(std::coroutine_handle<> continuation) {
    auto submission = std::make_unique<detail::Submission>();
    submission->record = std::move(record_);
    submission->resumeOn = resume_on_;
    submission->continuation = continuation;
    submission->result = &result_;
    // The last use of this object: the coroutine may resume (on another thread) at once.
    context_->submitter_->enqueue(std::move(submission));
}

RetireStats ExecutionContext::retireStats() const noexcept {
    return core_->retireStats();
}

Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> ExecutionContext::createBuffer(BufferDesc desc) {
    if (desc.size == 0) {
        return makeError(Errc::Internal, std::format("buffer '{}': the size is zero", desc.name));
    }
    core_->collect();  // memory the GPU has finished with is reused first

    const vk::BufferCreateInfo buffer_info{
        .size = desc.size,
        .usage = vk::BufferUsageFlags(desc.usage) | vk::BufferUsageFlagBits::eShaderDeviceAddress,
        .sharingMode = vk::SharingMode::eExclusive};
    VmaAllocationCreateInfo allocation_info{};
    switch (desc.memory) {
        case MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly:
            allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO_PREFER_DEVICE;
            break;
        case MemoryUsage::HostUpload:
            allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO;
            allocation_info.flags = VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_HOST_ACCESS_SEQUENTIAL_WRITE_BIT |
                                    VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_MAPPED_BIT;
            break;
        case MemoryUsage::HostReadback:
            allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO;
            allocation_info.flags =
                VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_HOST_ACCESS_RANDOM_BIT | VMA_ALLOCATION_CREATE_MAPPED_BIT;
            break;
    }
    VkBuffer buffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VmaAllocation allocation = nullptr;
    VmaAllocationInfo allocated{};
    DVV_TRY_VOID(detail::vkCheck(
        vmaCreateBuffer(core_->allocator(), &static_cast<const VkBufferCreateInfo&>(buffer_info),
                        &allocation_info, &buffer, &allocation, &allocated),
        "vmaCreateBuffer"));
    const VkDeviceAddress address =
        core_->device().getBufferAddress({.buffer = vk::Buffer(buffer)}, core_->dispatch());
    std::span<std::byte> mapped;
    if (desc.memory != MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly) {
        mapped = {static_cast<std::byte*>(allocated.pMappedData),
                  static_cast<std::size_t>(desc.size)};
    }
    if (!desc.name.empty()) {
        core_->setDebugName(vk::Buffer(buffer), desc.name);
        vmaSetAllocationName(core_->allocator(), allocation, desc.name.c_str());
    }
    try {
        return std::make_shared<GpuBuffer>(
            CreationKey<ExecutionContext>(
                LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<GpuBuffer>(objectId())),
            core_, std::move(desc), buffer, allocation, address, mapped);
    } catch (...) {
        vmaDestroyBuffer(core_->allocator(), buffer, allocation);
        throw;
    }
}

Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>> ExecutionContext::createImage(ImageDesc desc) {
    if (desc.width == 0 || desc.height == 0 || desc.format == VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED ||
        desc.usage == 0) {
        return makeError(
            Errc::Internal,
            std::format("image '{}': width, height, format and usage must be set", desc.name));
    }
    const auto format = static_cast<vk::Format>(desc.format);
    const auto usage = vk::ImageUsageFlags(desc.usage);
    const auto supported =
        core_->physicalDevice().getImageFormatProperties2({.format = format,
                                                           .type = vk::ImageType::e2D,
                                                           .tiling = vk::ImageTiling::eOptimal,
                                                           .usage = usage},
                                                          core_->dispatch());
    if (supported.result == vk::Result::eErrorFormatNotSupported) {
        return makeError(
            Errc::Unsupported,
            std::format("image '{}': device {} does not support {} images with usage {}", desc.name,
                        core_->id(), vk::to_string(format), vk::to_string(usage)));
    }
    DVV_TRY_VOID(detail::vkCheck(supported.result, "vkGetPhysicalDeviceImageFormatProperties2"));
    const vk::Extent3D& max = supported.value.imageFormatProperties.maxExtent;
    if (desc.width > max.width || desc.height > max.height) {
        return makeError(Errc::Unsupported,
                         std::format("image '{}': {}x{} exceeds the largest {} image of device {} "
                                     "({}x{})",
                                     desc.name, desc.width, desc.height, vk::to_string(format),
                                     core_->id(), max.width, max.height));
    }
    core_->collect();

    const vk::ImageCreateInfo image_info{
        .imageType = vk::ImageType::e2D,
        .format = format,
        .extent = {.width = desc.width, .height = desc.height, .depth = 1},
        .mipLevels = 1,
        .arrayLayers = 1,
        .samples = vk::SampleCountFlagBits::e1,
        .tiling = vk::ImageTiling::eOptimal,
        .usage = usage,
        .sharingMode = vk::SharingMode::eExclusive,
        .initialLayout = vk::ImageLayout::eUndefined};
    VmaAllocationCreateInfo allocation_info{};
    allocation_info.usage = VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_AUTO_PREFER_DEVICE;
    VkImage image = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VmaAllocation allocation = nullptr;
    DVV_TRY_VOID(detail::vkCheck(
        vmaCreateImage(core_->allocator(), &static_cast<const VkImageCreateInfo&>(image_info),
                       &allocation_info, &image, &allocation, nullptr),
        "vmaCreateImage"));
    if (!desc.name.empty()) {
        core_->setDebugName(vk::Image(image), desc.name);
        vmaSetAllocationName(core_->allocator(), allocation, desc.name.c_str());
    }
    try {
        return std::make_shared<GpuImage>(
            CreationKey<ExecutionContext>(LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<GpuImage>(objectId())),
            core_, std::move(desc), image, allocation);
    } catch (...) {
        vmaDestroyImage(core_->allocator(), image, allocation);
        throw;
    }
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

```cpp
#pragma once

// Access to the private parts of dvv-gpu's public classes, for its own sources and white-box tests.

#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "instance.h"
#include "submitter.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

struct Access {
    [[nodiscard]] static InstanceCore& instance(const GpuRegistry& registry) noexcept {
        return *registry.instance_;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] static DeviceCore& core(const ExecutionContext& context) noexcept {
        return *context.core_;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] static DeviceCore& core(const GpuBuffer& buffer) noexcept {
        return *buffer.core_;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] static DeviceCore& core(const GpuImage& image) noexcept { return *image.core_; }
    [[nodiscard]] static Submitter& submitter(const ExecutionContext& context) noexcept {
        return *context.submitter_;
    }
    // The submitter's last-resort signal; tests call it while the submit thread is idle.
    static void hostSignal(Submitter& submitter, Submission& submission) noexcept {
        submitter.hostSignal(submission);
    }
    // The command recorder tracks the layout its commands leave an image in.
    static void setLayout(GpuImage& image, VkImageLayout layout) noexcept {
        image.layout_.store(layout, std::memory_order_release);
    }
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/vk.h` (replace the whole file; the format traits give the texel size of an image format):

```cpp
#pragma once

// The one place that configures Vulkan-Hpp and VMA. Every dvv-gpu source includes Vulkan through
// this header, so the configuration is identical in every translation unit:
// - VK_NO_PROTOTYPES (set by the build for every user of dvv_gpu): nothing links the Vulkan
//   loader; it is loaded at run time.
// - Dynamic dispatch with explicit dispatchers: every instance and device has its own
//   vk::detail::DispatchLoaderDynamic, passed to every call. No default dispatcher storage is
//   defined, so a call that forgets its dispatcher fails to link.
// - No exceptions: calls return results, which are converted to dvv::Error (vk_result.h).
//   VULKAN_HPP_ASSERT_ON_RESULT is a no-op; by default Debug builds would abort on recoverable
//   errors such as VK_ERROR_OUT_OF_DEVICE_MEMORY.
// - No struct constructors: structures are filled with designated initialisers.
// - VMA fetches every Vulkan function through vkGetInstanceProcAddr / vkGetDeviceProcAddr.
// - The format traits (texel block sizes) come with it; they need the same configuration.

#if defined(VULKAN_HPP)
#error "vulkan.hpp was included before vk.h; include Vulkan only through vk.h"
#endif
#if !defined(VK_NO_PROTOTYPES)
#error "dvv_gpu is built with VK_NO_PROTOTYPES"
#endif

#define VULKAN_HPP_DISPATCH_LOADER_DYNAMIC 1
#define VULKAN_HPP_NO_EXCEPTIONS
#define VULKAN_HPP_NO_STRUCT_CONSTRUCTORS
#define VULKAN_HPP_ASSERT_ON_RESULT(condition) static_cast<void>(0)
#define VMA_STATIC_VULKAN_FUNCTIONS 0
#define VMA_DYNAMIC_VULKAN_FUNCTIONS 1

#include <vulkan/vulkan.hpp>
#include <vulkan/vulkan_format_traits.hpp>  // vk::blockSize() and other format properties
```

```powershell
$env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION = 'sync'; Push-Location dvv-gpu; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan; Pop-Location; Remove-Item Env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION
wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && export DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1 DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED=1 && cd dvv-gpu && for p in ninja-clang ninja-clang-asan ninja-clang-tsan; do cmake --workflow --preset $p 2>&1 | grep -E "tests passed|tests failed|error"; done'
git -C dvv-gpu add -A
$format = git -C dvv-gpu ls-files '*.h' '*.cpp'; wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd dvv-gpu && clang-format --dry-run --Werror "$@" && echo FORMAT-OK' lint $format
$tidy = git -C dvv-gpu ls-files ':(glob)src/*.cpp' ':(glob)tests/*.cpp' ':(glob)tools/*.cpp'; wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd dvv-gpu && clang-tidy -p ../_build/dvv-gpu/ninja-clang --quiet "$@" && echo LINT-OK' lint $tidy
```

Git runs from Windows only: PowerShell lists the files (after `git add -A`, so the new files are included) and passes them to `clang-format` and `clang-tidy` in WSL as arguments (`lint` is the script name `$0`, the files are `"$@"`). The presets together can run longer than 10 minutes, and `clang-tidy` alone can take about 15 minutes: run the commands with the tool's background mode (`run_in_background`, then wait for the notification) or one preset per tool call. Expected: `vs2026-asan` `100% tests passed, 0 tests failed out of 8`; Linux lines read `100% tests passed` (`out of 9` for `ninja-clang` in Debug and Release, `out of 8` for ASan and TSan; the GPU tests run on lavapipe with sync validation required); `FORMAT-OK` and `LINT-OK` with no diagnostics. TSan must report nothing in dvv-gpu's code: the submit and waiter threads, the executor thread and the tests' own threads all run here, and `tests/tsan.supp` (Task 17) suppresses only reports inside the lavapipe driver. If clang-format reports a file, run `clang-format -i` on it and repeat from Step 4.

- [ ] **Step 7: Commit and privacy lint**

```powershell
git -C dvv-gpu add -A
@'
feat: add the command recorder and the submit and waiter threads

co_await ctx->submit(executor, record) records and submits one batch of GPU work and resumes the
awaiting coroutine on the executor once the GPU has finished it, with the submission's timeline
value or the error that stopped it.

- CommandRecorder: fillBuffer, copyBuffer, copyBufferToImage, copyImageToBuffer (whole 2D image,
  tightly packed), transition(image, layout) and raw() (command buffer, device and
  vkGetDeviceProcAddr; Vulkan C types, vk.h stays private). Barriers are automatic and
  conservative: a global memory barrier (compute and transfer writes -> reads and writes) before
  every command, combined with the layout change of a transition, and a barrier that makes every
  write visible to the host at the end of the submission. Image layouts are tracked per
  submission and stored in the images once the batch is on the queue. A command that cannot be
  recorded (another device, range, alignment, usage) fails the recorder with Errc::Internal.
- Submitter (internal part of the context): the submit thread owns the command pool, reserves the
  timeline value before recording, records, and submits with vkQueueSubmit2 and a timeline signal;
  a failed recording or submission still signals its value with a batch that has only the signal
  or, when the queue refuses that too, from the host once the latest submitted signal is reached,
  so the timeline never stalls (only a lost device leaves a value unsignalled, and its waits then
  fail). No batch waits on a semaphore, nothing is released under the queue lock, and neither
  thread can release the last reference to the device core. The waiter thread waits for each value
  in order, frees what the retire queue can free, and posts the completion to the executor, which
  releases the record function (and everything it captured) and resumes the coroutine there, never
  inline. Hand-over between the threads does not allocate; the context destructor finishes every
  submission, then joins both threads.
- SubmitOperation: the lazy awaitable; keeps the context alive while awaited.

GPU tests: fill and copy round trip, buffer -> R32_UINT image -> buffer, resumption on the
executor's thread, captured resources freed after completion, a resource dropped while its
submission is recorded, a throwing record function, recorder errors (including invalid formats and
layouts), an empty record function, raw commands, closing a context after work and during a
submission, and the host-signal fallback (through an internal test hook).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C dvv-gpu commit -F -
```

### Task 19: ResourcePool for reusing GPU buffers

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/resource_pool.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/free_list.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/resource_pool_state.h`, `dvv-gpu/src/resource_pool.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/test_free_list.cpp`, `dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_test_resource_pool.cpp`
- Modify: `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt` (one source), `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/execution_context.h` (`createResourcePool`), `dvv-gpu/src/execution_context.cpp` (the factory), `dvv-gpu/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (unit and GPU test sources, compile-fail case 6), `dvv-gpu/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 18): `ExecutionContext` with constructor `ExecutionContext(CreationKey<GpuRegistry> key, std::shared_ptr<GpuRegistry> registry, std::shared_ptr<detail::DeviceCore> core, std::unique_ptr<detail::Submitter> submitter) noexcept`, private members `core_` and `submitter_`, `[[nodiscard]] SubmitOperation submit(IExecutor& resume_on, RecordFunction record)` (`using RecordFunction = std::move_only_function<void(CommandRecorder&)>;` in `dvv/gpu/submission.h`, which `execution_context.h` includes after `dvv/gpu/resources.h`); in `src/execution_context.cpp` the `#include "submitter.h"` after `#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"` and the definitions of `submit()` and `SubmitOperation` right after `drain()`; in `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt` the sources `src/command_recorder.cpp` and `src/submitter.cpp`; in `tests/CMakeLists.txt` the GPU test source `gpu_test_submission.cpp` (last in the list of `dvv_gpu_device_tests`). The submit thread is the only user of `reserveSubmitValue()` in production code (the reserved-value contract of Task 16).
- Consumes (Task 17): `struct BufferDesc { std::uint64_t size = 0; VkBufferUsageFlags usage = 0; MemoryUsage memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly; std::string name{}; };`, `enum class MemoryUsage : std::uint8_t { DeviceOnly, HostUpload, HostReadback };`, `GpuBuffer` (`desc()`, `size()`, `handle()`), `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> ExecutionContext::createBuffer(BufferDesc desc)` (always adds `VK_BUFFER_USAGE_SHADER_DEVICE_ADDRESS_BIT`; its destructor retires the buffer); compile-fail cases 1-5 in `tests/CMakeLists.txt` and `tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp`.
- Consumes (Task 16): `ExecutionContext` derives from `std::enable_shared_from_this<ExecutionContext>`; `ExecutionContext::retireStats()`; `detail::DeviceCore` (`src/device_core.h`): `[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t lastReservedValue() const noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::uint64_t> completedValue() const`, `[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t reserveSubmitValue() noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lockQueue()`, `void setSubmitted(std::uint64_t value, const std::unique_lock<std::mutex>& queue_lock) noexcept`, `timeline()`, `device()`, `dispatch()`; `[[nodiscard]] static DeviceCore& detail::Access::core(const ExecutionContext& context) noexcept` (`src/access.h`); the fixture's `GpuTest::context()`.
- Consumes (dvv-base): as in Task 16, plus `[[nodiscard]] std::size_t LifetimeRegistry::liveCount() const noexcept`.
- Produces (C++, public, `dvv/gpu/resource_pool.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu`): `struct ResourcePoolStats { std::size_t free = 0; std::size_t leased = 0; std::uint64_t created = 0; std::uint64_t reused = 0; };`; `class ResourcePool final : public Managed<ResourcePool>` with constructor `ResourcePool(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context, std::shared_ptr<detail::ResourcePoolState> state) noexcept`, destructor (trims), `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> acquireBuffer(BufferDesc desc)`, `std::size_t trim()` (frees every returned buffer, returns how many; not `noexcept`: taking the free list may allocate), `[[nodiscard]] ResourcePoolStats stats() const`. In `dvv/gpu/execution_context.h` (which now includes `resource_pool.h`): `[[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<ResourcePool>> ExecutionContext::createResourcePool()`; the pool's owner in the lifetime registry is the context, and the pool keeps the context alive.
- Produces (C++, private, namespace `dvv::gpu::detail`): in `src/free_list.h`: `struct BufferKey { std::uint64_t size = 0; VkBufferUsageFlags usage = 0; MemoryUsage memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly; friend auto operator<=>(const BufferKey&, const BufferKey&) = default; };`, `[[nodiscard]] inline BufferKey bufferKey(const BufferDesc& desc) noexcept` (ignores the name, adds the device-address bit), `template <class T> class FreeList` with `void add(const BufferKey& key, T item, std::uint64_t reusable_at)`, `[[nodiscard]] std::optional<T> take(const BufferKey& key, std::uint64_t completed)` (the oldest item whose value `completed` has reached), `[[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const noexcept`, `[[nodiscard]] FreeList takeAll()` (moves every item into the returned list; constructing it may allocate, e.g. `std::map`'s head node on MSVC); in `src/resource_pool_state.h`: `struct ResourcePoolState { explicit ResourcePoolState(std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore> device) noexcept; void giveBack(std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>& buffer); std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore> core; mutable std::mutex mutex; FreeList<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> free; std::atomic<std::size_t> leased{0}; std::uint64_t created = 0; std::uint64_t reused = 0; };`.
- Produces (tests): compile-fail case `dvv_gpu.compile_fail.6` (`std::make_shared` of a `ResourcePool` with every constructor argument but the key, so the case still fails if the key parameter is removed: MSVC `C2661: 'dvv::gpu::ResourcePool::ResourcePool'`, Clang `no matching constructor for initialization of 'dvv::gpu::ResourcePool'`).
- Behaviour: `acquireBuffer` reads the timeline first, outside the lock (`completedValue()`, a Vulkan error is returned; the value only grows, so an entry it shows ready is ready), then looks up the key (size, usage with the device-address bit, memory) under the lock and hands out the oldest returned buffer whose tag the timeline has reached, otherwise it creates a new buffer through `createBuffer` (same errors). The returned `shared_ptr` is a lease: its deleter puts the buffer back on the free list, tagged with `lastReservedValue()` at that moment (every submission that can have used the buffer was reserved no later), or frees it when the pool is gone (the lease holds the pool state weakly) or when the free list cannot take it (allocation failure: `add()` destroys the buffer, under the pool lock; the lock order is pool, then retire queue, never the reverse). Each buffer is created and registered in the lifetime registry once; a reused buffer keeps the debug name it was created with. `trim()` and the destructor take the whole free list under the lock and free the buffers outside it (they go to the retire queue as usual). Thread-safe: one mutex for the free list and counters, `leased` atomic.

Note (design deviations): the pool is created by the context's factory `createResourcePool()` (authority `ExecutionContext`, as for buffers and images). "Released buffers return to the pool once retired" is implemented without the retire queue: a returned buffer goes on the free list at once with the last reserved timeline value as its tag and is handed out only when the timeline has reached that value, which gives the same guarantee as retiring it and creating a new one, without destroying the Vulkan buffer. `stats()` is added so the tests (and later diagnostics) can see free, leased, created and reused counts. The free list is a separate template so its ordering and readiness rules have unit tests without a GPU. Spec §5.3 also names transient images: pooled images are deferred to M2 (FrameStore, staging), because a leased `GpuImage` must not hand out `shared_from_this()` (the command recorder keeps images that way), so image leases need another design (a comment in `resource_pool.h` says so).

Every Windows command below that runs the GPU tests sets `DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync` itself, in the same command, as in Task 15, and removes it afterwards.
