`dvv-gpu/tests/test_free_list.cpp` (unit tests, no GPU):

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <optional>

#include "free_list.h"

using dvv::gpu::BufferDesc;
using dvv::gpu::MemoryUsage;
using dvv::gpu::detail::BufferKey;
using dvv::gpu::detail::bufferKey;
using dvv::gpu::detail::FreeList;

TEST_CASE("a buffer key ignores the name and always has the device-address usage") {
    const BufferKey key = bufferKey({.size = 64,
                                     .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT,
                                     .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload,
                                     .name = "a"});
    CHECK(key == bufferKey({.size = 64,
                            .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT |
                                     VK_BUFFER_USAGE_SHADER_DEVICE_ADDRESS_BIT,
                            .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload,
                            .name = "b"}));
    CHECK(key != bufferKey({.size = 128,
                            .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT,
                            .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload}));
    CHECK(key != bufferKey({.size = 64,
                            .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
                            .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload}));
    CHECK(key != bufferKey({.size = 64,
                            .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_STORAGE_BUFFER_BIT,
                            .memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly}));
}

TEST_CASE("the free list hands out matching items once the timeline has reached them") {
    const BufferKey small{.size = 64};
    const BufferKey large{.size = 128};
    FreeList<int> list;
    list.add(small, 1, 5);
    list.add(small, 2, 3);
    list.add(large, 3, 0);
    CHECK(list.size() == 3);
    CHECK(list.take(BufferKey{.size = 32}, 100) == std::nullopt);

    CHECK(list.take(small, 2) == std::nullopt);  // neither 5 nor 3 reached
    CHECK(list.take(small, 4) == 2);             // 3 reached, 5 not
    CHECK(list.take(small, 4) == std::nullopt);
    CHECK(list.take(small, 9) == 1);
    CHECK(list.take(small, 100) == std::nullopt);
    CHECK(list.take(large, 0) == 3);
    CHECK(list.size() == 0);
}

TEST_CASE("the free list hands out the oldest ready item first") {
    const BufferKey key{.size = 64};
    FreeList<int> list;
    list.add(key, 1, 1);
    list.add(key, 2, 2);
    CHECK(list.take(key, 10) == 1);
    CHECK(list.take(key, 10) == 2);
}

TEST_CASE("takeAll empties the free list") {
    FreeList<int> list;
    list.add(BufferKey{.size = 64}, 1, 0);
    list.add(BufferKey{.size = 128}, 2, 7);
    FreeList<int> all = list.takeAll();
    CHECK(all.size() == 2);
    CHECK(list.size() == 0);
    CHECK(list.take(BufferKey{.size = 64}, 100) == std::nullopt);
    CHECK(all.take(BufferKey{.size = 128}, 7) == 2);
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/gpu_test_resource_pool.cpp` (`core.reserveSubmitValue()` stands in for a submission that is still being recorded and `signalTimeline` for its completion, so the test fails if the pool tags with the last submitted value instead of the last reserved one; the last case acquires and returns buffers from 4 threads, 100 times each, which the TSan preset checks):

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

#include "access.h"
#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resource_pool.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "gpu_fixture.h"

using dvv::gpu::BufferDesc;
using dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer;
using dvv::gpu::MemoryUsage;
using dvv::gpu::ResourcePool;
using dvv::gpu::detail::Access;
using dvv::gpu::test::GpuTest;

namespace {

const BufferDesc STAGING{.size = 4096,
                         .usage = VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
                         .memory = MemoryUsage::HostUpload,
                         .name = "staging"};

void signalTimeline(dvv::gpu::detail::DeviceCore& core, std::uint64_t value) {
    const vk::SemaphoreSignalInfo signal{.semaphore = core.timeline(), .value = value};
    REQUIRE(core.device().signalSemaphore(signal, core.dispatch()) == vk::Result::eSuccess);
}

}  // namespace

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a pool is a managed object of its context") {
    const auto pool = context().createResourcePool().value();
    CHECK(pool->ownerId() == context().objectId());
    CHECK(pool->typeName() == "dvv::gpu::ResourcePool");
    const auto stats = pool->stats();
    CHECK(stats.free == 0);
    CHECK(stats.leased == 0);
    CHECK(stats.created == 0);
    CHECK(stats.reused == 0);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a returned buffer is handed out again for the same description") {
    const auto pool = context().createResourcePool().value();
    auto first = pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING).value();
    const VkBuffer handle = first->handle();
    CHECK(first->size() == 4096);
    CHECK(first->desc().name == "staging");
    CHECK(pool->stats().leased == 1);

    const auto live = dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount();
    first.reset();  // nothing was submitted: reusable at once
    CHECK(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == live);  // still alive in the pool
    CHECK(pool->stats().free == 1);
    CHECK(pool->stats().leased == 0);

    BufferDesc renamed = STAGING;
    renamed.name = "other name";
    const auto again = pool->acquireBuffer(renamed).value();
    CHECK(again->handle() == handle);
    CHECK(again->desc().name == "staging");  // a reused buffer keeps its name
    const auto stats = pool->stats();
    CHECK(stats.created == 1);
    CHECK(stats.reused == 1);
    CHECK(stats.free == 0);

    // Another size, usage or memory gets a new buffer.
    BufferDesc larger = STAGING;
    larger.size = 8192;
    CHECK(pool->acquireBuffer(larger).value()->handle() != handle);
    CHECK(pool->stats().created == 2);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a returned buffer waits for the submission being recorded") {
    auto& core = Access::core(context());
    const auto pool = context().createResourcePool().value();
    auto first = pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING).value();
    const VkBuffer first_handle = first->handle();
    // As if the submit thread were recording a submission: reserved, not yet submitted.
    const std::uint64_t recording = core.reserveSubmitValue();
    first.reset();  // the commands being recorded may use it

    auto second = pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING).value();
    CHECK(second->handle() != first_handle);
    CHECK(pool->stats().created == 2);

    signalTimeline(core, recording);
    const auto third = pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING).value();
    CHECK(third->handle() == first_handle);
    CHECK(pool->stats().reused == 1);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "trim frees the returned buffers") {
    const auto pool = context().createResourcePool().value();
    auto first = pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING).value();
    auto second = pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING).value();
    const auto live = dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount();
    first.reset();
    second.reset();
    CHECK(pool->stats().free == 2);
    CHECK(pool->trim() == 2);
    CHECK(pool->stats().free == 0);
    CHECK(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == live - 2);
    CHECK(context().retireStats().retiring == 0);
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "a buffer released after its pool is freed") {
    auto pool = context().createResourcePool().value();
    auto buffer = pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING).value();
    const auto live = dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount();
    pool.reset();
    CHECK(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == live - 1);  // the pool
    buffer.reset();
    CHECK(dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == live - 2);  // and the buffer
}

TEST_CASE_FIXTURE(GpuTest, "buffers are acquired and returned from several threads") {
    constexpr int THREADS = 4;
    constexpr int ROUNDS = 100;
    const auto pool = context().createResourcePool().value();
    std::atomic<int> failures{0};
    {
        std::vector<std::jthread> threads;
        threads.reserve(THREADS);
        for (int thread = 0; thread < THREADS; ++thread) {
            threads.emplace_back([&pool, &failures] {
                for (int round = 0; round < ROUNDS; ++round) {
                    if (!pool->acquireBuffer(STAGING)) {
                        failures.fetch_add(1);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
    }  // joins the threads
    CHECK(failures.load() == 0);
    const auto stats = pool->stats();
    CHECK(stats.leased == 0);
    CHECK(stats.created + stats.reused == std::uint64_t{THREADS} * ROUNDS);
    CHECK(stats.free == stats.created);
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/compile_fail/direct_construction.cpp` (replace the whole file; case 6 passes every argument of the pool's constructor but the key, so it fails because the key is missing, not because of the argument count):

```cpp
// Compile-fail cases for dvv-gpu's managed types (spec §9.4). DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE selects one:
//   0  control: a registry created through its factory compiles
//   1  constructing a CreationKey<GpuRegistry> outside the registry
//   2  constructing a GpuRegistry without a key
//   3  constructing an ExecutionContext without a key
//   4  creating a GpuBuffer without its context
//   5  creating a GpuImage without its context
//   6  creating a ResourcePool without its context
#include <memory>

#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resource_pool.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"

int main() {
    const auto registry = dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry::create();
#if DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 1
    dvv::CreationKey<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry> forged(
        dvv::LifetimeRegistry::instance().issue<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry>());
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 2
    dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry direct(std::unique_ptr<dvv::gpu::detail::InstanceCore>{});
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 3
    dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext direct(*registry, std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::detail::DeviceCore>{});
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 4
    auto buffer = std::make_shared<dvv::gpu::GpuBuffer>(dvv::gpu::BufferDesc{.size = 4});
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 5
    auto image = std::make_shared<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>(dvv::gpu::ImageDesc{.width = 4});
#elif DVV_COMPILE_FAIL_CASE == 6
    // Every argument but the key.
    auto pool = std::make_shared<dvv::gpu::ResourcePool>(
        std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext>{},
        std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::detail::ResourcePoolState>{});
#endif
    return registry.has_value() ? 0 : 1;
}
```

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/resource_pool.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// Reuse of transient GPU buffers (spec §5.3).

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/lifetime.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

class ExecutionContext;

namespace detail {
struct ResourcePoolState;
}  // namespace detail

struct ResourcePoolStats {
    std::size_t free = 0;       // returned buffers waiting in the pool
    std::size_t leased = 0;     // buffers handed out and not yet returned
    std::uint64_t created = 0;  // buffers the pool created
    std::uint64_t reused = 0;   // acquisitions served from the pool
};

// Hands out buffers and takes them back (spec §5.3). A buffer acquired from the pool returns to it
// when its last shared_ptr is released, and is handed out again, for the same size, usage and
// memory, once the GPU has finished every submission made or being recorded at its return. Each
// buffer is created (and registered in the lifetime registry) once. Created by
// ExecutionContext::createResourcePool(); keeps its context alive. Thread-safe.
// Pooled images are deferred to M2 (FrameStore, staging): a leased GpuImage must not hand out
// shared_from_this() (the command recorder keeps images that way), so image leases need another
// design.
class ResourcePool final : public Managed<ResourcePool> {
public:
    ResourcePool(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key, std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context,
                 std::shared_ptr<detail::ResourcePoolState> state) noexcept;
    // Frees the returned buffers. Buffers still handed out are freed when they are released.
    ~ResourcePool();

    // A returned buffer that matches size, usage and memory and that the GPU has finished with,
    // otherwise a new one from the context (errors as ExecutionContext::createBuffer()). A reused
    // buffer keeps the debug name it was created with.
    [[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> acquireBuffer(BufferDesc desc);
    // Frees every returned buffer; returns how many.
    std::size_t trim();
    [[nodiscard]] ResourcePoolStats stats() const;

private:
    std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context_;
    std::shared_ptr<detail::ResourcePoolState> state_;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/src/free_list.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The free list of a ResourcePool (spec §5.3): returned resources by key, each reusable once the
// timeline semaphore has reached the value it was returned at. Not synchronised; the pool locks.

#include <algorithm>
#include <compare>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <deque>
#include <map>
#include <optional>
#include <utility>

#include <vulkan/vulkan_core.h>

#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

// What makes two buffers interchangeable.
struct BufferKey {
    std::uint64_t size = 0;
    VkBufferUsageFlags usage = 0;  // as created: with VK_BUFFER_USAGE_SHADER_DEVICE_ADDRESS_BIT
    MemoryUsage memory = MemoryUsage::DeviceOnly;

    friend auto operator<=>(const BufferKey&, const BufferKey&) = default;
};

[[nodiscard]] inline BufferKey bufferKey(const BufferDesc& desc) noexcept {
    return {.size = desc.size,
            .usage = desc.usage | VK_BUFFER_USAGE_SHADER_DEVICE_ADDRESS_BIT,
            .memory = desc.memory};
}

template <class T>
class FreeList {
public:
    // Adds an item that may be reused once the timeline has reached `reusable_at`. If that cannot
    // allocate, the item is destroyed here and std::bad_alloc propagates.
    void add(const BufferKey& key, T item, std::uint64_t reusable_at) {
        entries_[key].push_back({.item = std::move(item), .reusableAt = reusable_at});
        ++size_;
    }

    // The oldest item for the key whose value the timeline has reached (`completed`).
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<T> take(const BufferKey& key, std::uint64_t completed) {
        const auto found = entries_.find(key);
        if (found == entries_.end()) {
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        auto& list = found->second;
        const auto ready = std::ranges::find_if(
            list, [completed](const Entry& entry) { return entry.reusableAt <= completed; });
        if (ready == list.end()) {
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        std::optional<T> item(std::move(ready->item));
        list.erase(ready);
        if (list.empty()) {
            entries_.erase(found);
        }
        --size_;
        return item;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const noexcept { return size_; }

    // Moves every item into the returned list, so the caller can destroy them outside its lock.
    // (Constructing the returned list may allocate, e.g. std::map's head node on MSVC.)
    [[nodiscard]] FreeList takeAll() {
        FreeList all;
        all.entries_.swap(entries_);
        all.size_ = std::exchange(size_, 0);
        return all;
    }

private:
    struct Entry {
        T item;
        std::uint64_t reusableAt = 0;
    };

    std::map<BufferKey, std::deque<Entry>> entries_;
    std::size_t size_ = 0;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/resource_pool_state.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// The shared part of a ResourcePool: its free list and counters. The leases handed out hold it
// weakly, so a buffer released after its pool is gone is simply freed.

#include <atomic>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>

#include "device_core.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "free_list.h"

namespace dvv::gpu::detail {

struct ResourcePoolState {
    explicit ResourcePoolState(std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore> device) noexcept
        : core(std::move(device)) {}

    // Takes a buffer back; reusable once the latest reserved submission has finished.
    void giveBack(std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>& buffer);

    std::shared_ptr<DeviceCore> core;
    mutable std::mutex mutex;
    FreeList<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> free;
    std::atomic<std::size_t> leased{0};
    std::uint64_t created = 0;
    std::uint64_t reused = 0;
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu::detail
```

`dvv-gpu/src/resource_pool.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dvv/gpu/resource_pool.h"

#include <optional>
#include <utility>

#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "resource_pool_state.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {

static_assert(ManagedType<ResourcePool>);

namespace {

// The deleter of a lease: returns the buffer to its pool, or frees it when the pool is gone.
class Return {
public:
    Return(std::weak_ptr<detail::ResourcePoolState> pool,
           std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer> buffer) noexcept
        : pool_(std::move(pool)), buffer_(std::move(buffer)) {}

    void operator()(GpuBuffer* /*lease*/) noexcept {
        const std::shared_ptr<detail::ResourcePoolState> pool = pool_.lock();
        if (!pool) {
            buffer_.reset();
            return;
        }
        pool->leased.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        try {
            pool->giveBack(buffer_);
        } catch (...) {
            buffer_.reset();  // out of memory: freed here, unless the free list already freed it
        }
    }

private:
    std::weak_ptr<detail::ResourcePoolState> pool_;
    std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer> buffer_;
};

}  // namespace

namespace detail {

void ResourcePoolState::giveBack(std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>& buffer) {
    // The key first: the order in which the arguments below are evaluated is unspecified.
    const BufferKey key = bufferKey(buffer->desc());
    const std::scoped_lock lock(mutex);
    // Any submission that used the buffer was reserved no later than now. A buffer the list cannot
    // take (out of memory) is freed by add(), under this lock; the lock order is pool, then retire
    // queue, never the reverse.
    free.add(key, std::move(buffer), core->lastReservedValue());
}

}  // namespace detail

ResourcePool::ResourcePool(CreationKey<ExecutionContext> key,
                           std::shared_ptr<ExecutionContext> context,
                           std::shared_ptr<detail::ResourcePoolState> state) noexcept
    : Managed(std::move(key)), context_(std::move(context)), state_(std::move(state)) {}

ResourcePool::~ResourcePool() {
    static_cast<void>(trim());
}

Result<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> ResourcePool::acquireBuffer(BufferDesc desc) {
    const detail::BufferKey key = detail::bufferKey(desc);
    // Read before taking the lock: the value only grows, so an entry it shows ready is ready.
    DVV_TRY(const std::uint64_t completed, state_->core->completedValue());
    std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer> buffer;
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
        if (std::optional<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> ready = state_->free.take(key, completed)) {
            buffer = std::move(*ready);
            ++state_->reused;
        }
    }
    if (!buffer) {
        DVV_TRY(buffer, context_->createBuffer(std::move(desc)));
        const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
        ++state_->created;
    }
    GpuBuffer* const lease = buffer.get();
    state_->leased.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    // Should the control block not be allocated, the deleter runs and the buffer returns.
    return std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>(lease, Return(state_, std::move(buffer)));
}

std::size_t ResourcePool::trim() {
    detail::FreeList<std::shared_ptr<GpuBuffer>> released;
    {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
        released = state_->free.takeAll();
    }
    return released.size();  // the buffers are freed (retired) here, outside the lock
}

ResourcePoolStats ResourcePool::stats() const {
    const std::scoped_lock lock(state_->mutex);
    return {.free = state_->free.size(),
            .leased = state_->leased.load(std::memory_order_relaxed),
            .created = state_->created,
            .reused = state_->reused};
}

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Commit and privacy lint**

```powershell
git -C dvv-gpu add -A
@'
feat: add ResourcePool for reusing GPU buffers

ctx->createResourcePool() returns a managed pool (authority ExecutionContext, owner the context,
which it keeps alive). acquireBuffer(BufferDesc) hands out a shared_ptr<GpuBuffer> whose deleter
returns the buffer to the pool; a returned buffer is reused for the same size, usage (with the
device-address bit) and memory once the timeline has reached the latest submission reserved at
its return, otherwise a new buffer is created through the context. Each buffer is created and
registered once; a reused buffer keeps its debug name. trim() frees the returned buffers, the
destructor too; a buffer released after its pool is gone is simply freed. stats() reports free,
leased, created and reused counts.

The free list is a small template (detail::FreeList, keyed by detail::BufferKey) with unit tests;
GPU tests cover reuse, waiting for a submission reserved before the return (also one still being
recorded), trim, a buffer outliving its pool, and four threads acquiring and returning buffers.
Compile-fail case 6: a ResourcePool constructed without its context.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C dvv-gpu commit -F -
```

### Task 20: dvv_add_shaders for build-time Slang compute shaders

**Files:**
- Create: `dvv-gpu/cmake/DvvShaders.cmake`, `dvv-gpu/cmake/DvvEmbedShader.cmake`, `dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/embedded_shader.h`, `dvv-gpu/tests/shaders/add_one.slang`, `dvv-gpu/tests/shaders/run-slangc-check.cmake`, `dvv-gpu/tests/shaders/run-shader-names-check.cmake`, `dvv-gpu/tests/test_shaders.cpp`
- Modify: `dvv-gpu/CMakeLists.txt` (include and install the modules), `dvv-gpu/cmake/dvv_gpuConfig.cmake.in` (include `DvvShaders.cmake`), `dvv-gpu/README.md` (Shaders section), `dvv-gpu/tests/CMakeLists.txt` (test shader library, unit test source, `dvv_gpu.slangc_check` and `dvv_gpu.shader_names`), `dvv-gpu/tests/package/CMakeLists.txt`, `dvv-gpu/tests/package/main.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 6, rawreel-workspace): Slang v2026.20 installed by the superbuild as `<prefix>/slang` (`_third_party/msvc/slang/bin/slangc.exe`, `_third_party/clang/slang/bin/slangc`); `slangc -version` prints `2026.20`. The presets pass the prefix in `CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH` (`${sourceDir}/../_third_party/msvc` or `.../clang`).
- Consumes (Task 12): the dvv-gpu project layout (`cmake/dvv_gpuConfig.cmake.in`, the `install(FILES ... DESTINATION ${config_dir})` of the package config), `DVV_CMAKE_DIR` and `dvv_set_compiler_options()`; the package test (`tests/package/run-package-test.cmake` configures the consumer in `tests/package/` twice, installed and in-tree, with `CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH` holding the third-party prefix).
- Consumes (Tasks 15-19): the unit test executable `dvv_gpu_tests` (`tests/CMakeLists.txt`, linked `PRIVATE dvv::gpu doctest::doctest`, `ENVIRONMENT DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail`) and the GPU test executable `dvv_gpu_device_tests` (linked `PRIVATE dvv::gpu doctest::doctest GPUOpen::VulkanMemoryAllocator`).
- Consumes (dvv-base): `[[nodiscard]] Result<Value> dvv::json::parse(std::string_view text)`, `template <class T> [[nodiscard]] Result<T> dvv::json::get(const Value& object, std::string_view key)`, `using dvv::json::Value = nlohmann::json;`.
- Produces (CMake, `cmake/DvvShaders.cmake`, included by dvv-gpu's `CMakeLists.txt` in-tree and by the installed `dvv_gpuConfig.cmake`; both modules installed to `${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/cmake/dvv_gpu`): `dvv_add_shaders(<target> [NAMESPACE <namespace>] SHADERS <file.slang>...)` (default namespace `<target>_shaders` made a C identifier; a namespace may be nested with `::`; adds the generated sources and stamps, `PUBLIC` include directory `$<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_shaders/<target>>` and `PUBLIC dvv::gpu`, so the target must use the keyword form of `target_link_libraries()`; records the function names and source files in the target properties `DVV_SHADER_FUNCTIONS` and `DVV_SHADER_SOURCES`, also across several calls); `dvv_find_slangc()` (sets the cache variable `DVV_SLANGC`, or fails configuring); the global property `DVV_SLANGC_VERSION` (`2026.20`). Script `cmake/DvvEmbedShader.cmake` (`-DNAME -DFUNCTION -DNAMESPACE -DHEADER_INCLUDE -DSPIRV -DREFLECTION -DHEADER -DSOURCE -DSTAMP`).
- Produces (generated, per shader `<name>.slang`, in the build tree only): header `<namespace as a path>/<name>.h` (`test_shaders/add_one.h`; `a::b` gives `a/b/add_one.h`) declaring `[[nodiscard]] const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& <namespace>::<name in camelCase>() noexcept;` (the parts between `-`, `_` and `.` joined, the first letter lower case, the first letter of each further part upper case, the rest kept: `add_one.slang` -> `addOne()`, `blurH.slang` -> `blurH()`, `YCbCr.slang` -> `yCbCr()`, `two-step.v2.slang` -> `twoStepV2()`), a source with the SPIR-V words and the reflection JSON (`SKIP_UNITY_BUILD_INCLUSION`, its arrays have the same names in every generated source), and a stamp `<name>.stamp`.
- Produces (C++, public, `dvv/gpu/embedded_shader.h`, namespace `dvv::gpu`): `struct EmbeddedShader { std::string_view name{}; std::span<const std::uint32_t> spirv{}; std::string_view reflectionJson{}; };`.
- Produces (tests): the static library `dvv_gpu_test_shaders` (`test_shaders::addOne()`, the `add_one` shader: `uint* data` and `uint count` as push constants, 16 bytes, `[numthreads(64, 1, 1)]`, adds 1 to every value below `count`), linked into `dvv_gpu_tests` and `dvv_gpu_device_tests` (the pipeline and demo tasks use it); the CTest script tests `dvv_gpu.slangc_check` and `dvv_gpu.shader_names` (no label, `TIMEOUT 120`, not behind `DVV_SANITIZE`).
- Behaviour: slangc is looked up only in `<prefix>/slang/bin` of `CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH` (`NO_DEFAULT_PATH`, never `PATH`: an installed Vulkan SDK may bring another version) and must print exactly the pinned version; otherwise configuring fails with `dvv_add_shaders: slangc 2026.20 was not found in the third-party prefix (...)`, `dvv_add_shaders: <path> -version failed (<status>): <output>` (a slangc that cannot run) or `dvv_add_shaders: <path> reports version '<v>', but dvv-gpu pins slangc 2026.20. Rebuild the third-party prefix, or update DVV_SLANGC_VERSION in dvv-gpu's cmake/DvvShaders.cmake together with rawreel-workspace's deps/versions.cmake.`. A namespace that is not a C++ namespace, a shader name that gives no C++ function name or gives a C++ keyword (`dvv_add_shaders: '<file>' does not make a C++ function name ('<function>')`), and two shaders of one target with the same function name (`dvv_add_shaders: '<file>' gives the same name as '<other>' (<function>)`) fail configuring. Flags: `-target spirv -profile spirv_1_6 -entry main -stage compute -O2 -warnings-as-errors all -reflection-json <file> -depfile <file>`. The embed step checks the SPIR-V size (a non-zero multiple of 4) and magic number, writes the JSON as a character array (MSVC limits string literals) and rewrites the generated header and source only when their content changes (they are byproducts); it always touches the stamp, which is the rule's output, so the rule is up to date afterwards on every generator (MSBuild included).

Note (design deviations): `NAMESPACE` is an addition (default `<target>_shaders`, and the header path follows the namespace, so two libraries that each embed a shader of the same name never share a header path or a symbol), and each shader is reached through a function returning a reference to one `static constexpr EmbeddedShader`, declared in a generated header `<namespace as a path>/<name>.h`; the design only named the struct. slangc also writes a depfile for imports, so a shader rebuilds when a file it imports changes; no test covers an import yet (`add_one.slang` imports nothing). The pinned version is repeated in `DvvShaders.cmake` (`DVV_SLANGC_VERSION`, used in-tree and by installed consumers, which do not see rawreel-workspace's `deps/versions.cmake`); a comment there says to keep both in step, and the version-mismatch message names both places. Turning the reflection JSON into a `ParameterSchema` stays deferred to the first component that needs it (dvv-gpu does not depend on dvv-registry).

Note (CI): `tests/test_shaders.cpp` includes the generated `test_shaders/add_one.h`, so clang-tidy needs a built tree; the `Tidy` step of rawreel-workspace's `dvv-ci.yml` gets the build before clang-tidy in Task 23 (it must reach rawreel-workspace `main` before dvv-gpu's CI runs).

Every Windows command below that runs the GPU tests sets `DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync` itself, in the same command, as in Task 15, and removes it afterwards.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`dvv-gpu/tests/shaders/add_one.slang` (the test shader, as in the spike):

```text
// Adds 1 to every uint32 of a buffer that the push constants pass by buffer device address.
struct PushConstants
{
    uint* data;  // buffer device address (a PhysicalStorageBuffer pointer)
    uint count;  // number of values
};

[[vk::push_constant]] ConstantBuffer<PushConstants> pc;

[shader("compute")]
[numthreads(64, 1, 1)]
void main(uint3 id: SV_DispatchThreadID)
{
    if (id.x < pc.count)
    {
        pc.data[id.x] = pc.data[id.x] + 1;
    }
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/test_shaders.cpp` (unit tests: the SPIR-V header words and the reflection JSON; the header is `test_shaders/add_one.h`, after the namespace):

```cpp
#include <doctest/doctest.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>

#include "dvv/base/json.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/embedded_shader.h"
#include "test_shaders/add_one.h"

TEST_CASE("an embedded shader holds SPIR-V 1.6 for its entry point") {
    const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& shader = test_shaders::addOne();
    CHECK(shader.name == "add_one");
    REQUIRE(shader.spirv.size() > 5);
    CHECK(shader.spirv[0] == 0x07230203U);  // the SPIR-V magic number
    CHECK(shader.spirv[1] == 0x00010600U);  // version 1.6
    CHECK(shader.spirv[3] > 1);             // the id bound
    CHECK(shader.spirv[4] == 0);            // the reserved schema word
}

TEST_CASE("an embedded shader's reflection describes its entry point and push constants") {
    const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& shader = test_shaders::addOne();
    const auto reflection = dvv::json::parse(shader.reflectionJson);
    REQUIRE(reflection.has_value());

    const auto& entry_points = reflection->at("entryPoints");
    REQUIRE(entry_points.size() == 1);
    CHECK(dvv::json::get<std::string>(entry_points[0], "name") == "main");
    CHECK(dvv::json::get<std::string>(entry_points[0], "stage") == "compute");
    CHECK(entry_points[0].at("threadGroupSize") == dvv::json::Value::parse("[64, 1, 1]"));

    const auto& parameters = reflection->at("parameters");
    REQUIRE(parameters.size() == 1);
    CHECK(dvv::json::get<std::string>(parameters[0], "name") == "pc");
    CHECK(dvv::json::get<std::string>(parameters[0].at("binding"), "kind") == "pushConstantBuffer");
    // The push constants are 16 bytes: an 8-byte address, a 4-byte count and padding.
    const auto& layout = parameters[0].at("type").at("elementVarLayout").at("binding");
    CHECK(dvv::json::get<std::uint32_t>(layout, "size") == 16);
}
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/shaders/run-slangc-check.cmake` (a script test: the pinned slangc, a wrong version, a slangc that cannot run, and a prefix without slangc while the real one is on `PATH`, so the case fails if `NO_DEFAULT_PATH` is dropped):

```cmake
# Checks that dvv_add_shaders refuses, at configure time and with a clear message, a slangc that
# is missing from the prefix (even when one is on PATH), one that cannot run and one of another
# version, and accepts the pinned one. Each case runs a child `cmake -P` that includes
# DvvShaders.cmake and calls dvv_find_slangc().
#   cmake -DSHADERS_MODULE=<DvvShaders.cmake> -DPREFIX_PATH=<prefix,...> -DWORK_DIR=<dir>
#         -P run-slangc-check.cmake
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

# The paths go into a generated script, so they must not hold backslashes.
file(TO_CMAKE_PATH "${SHADERS_MODULE}" SHADERS_MODULE)
file(TO_CMAKE_PATH "${WORK_DIR}" WORK_DIR)

file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${WORK_DIR}")
file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${WORK_DIR}/empty-prefix")
set(probe "${WORK_DIR}/probe-slangc.cmake")
file(WRITE "${probe}" "include(\"${SHADERS_MODULE}\")
string(REPLACE \",\" \";\" CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH \"\${PREFIX_PATH}\")
if(DEFINED REQUIRED_VERSION)
    set_property(GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_SLANGC_VERSION \"\${REQUIRED_VERSION}\")
endif()
dvv_find_slangc()
message(STATUS \"slangc: \${DVV_SLANGC}\")
")

# A prefix whose slangc is not a program.
set(broken "${WORK_DIR}/broken-prefix/slang/bin/slangc")
if(CMAKE_HOST_WIN32)
    string(APPEND broken ".exe")
endif()
file(WRITE "${broken}" "not a program\n")
file(CHMOD "${broken}" FILE_PERMISSIONS OWNER_READ OWNER_WRITE OWNER_EXECUTE)

# expect(<name> <0 = must succeed, 1 = must fail> <regular expression> [-D...])
function(expect name must_fail pattern)
    execute_process(COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" ${ARGN} -P "${probe}"
        RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE output ERROR_VARIABLE output)
    # CMake wraps long messages; compare with the white space folded.
    string(REGEX REPLACE "[ \t\r\n]+" " " folded "${output}")
    if(must_fail AND status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "${name}: configuring succeeded, a failure was expected:\n${output}")
    elseif(NOT must_fail AND NOT status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "${name}: configuring failed:\n${output}")
    endif()
    if(NOT folded MATCHES "${pattern}")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "${name}: the output does not match '${pattern}':\n${output}")
    endif()
    message(STATUS "${name}: ok")
endfunction()

expect("pinned slangc" 0 "slangc: .*slangc"
       "-DPREFIX_PATH=${PREFIX_PATH}")
string(CONCAT mismatch "reports version '[0-9.]+', but dvv-gpu pins slangc 1\\.2\\.3\\. "
       "Rebuild the third-party prefix, or update DVV_SLANGC_VERSION")
expect("wrong slangc version" 1 "${mismatch}"
       "-DPREFIX_PATH=${PREFIX_PATH}" -DREQUIRED_VERSION=1.2.3)
expect("slangc that cannot run" 1 "slangc[.a-z]* -version failed \\("
       "-DPREFIX_PATH=${WORK_DIR}/broken-prefix")

# The real slangc on PATH must not be found: only the prefix counts.
string(REPLACE "," ";" prefixes "${PREFIX_PATH}")
set(slang_dirs "")
foreach(prefix IN LISTS prefixes)
    list(APPEND slang_dirs "${prefix}/slang/bin")
endforeach()
cmake_path(CONVERT "${slang_dirs}" TO_NATIVE_PATH_LIST native_dirs)
if(CMAKE_HOST_WIN32)
    set(ENV{PATH} "${native_dirs};$ENV{PATH}")
else()
    set(ENV{PATH} "${native_dirs}:$ENV{PATH}")
endif()
expect("no slangc in the prefix, one on PATH" 1
       "slangc [0-9.]+ was not found in the third-party prefix"
       "-DPREFIX_PATH=${WORK_DIR}/empty-prefix")
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/shaders/run-shader-names-check.cmake` (a script test: camelCase names, a duplicate name and a C++ keyword; it configures a small `LANGUAGES NONE` project with the build's generator):

```cmake
# Checks the C++ names dvv_add_shaders gives shaders, and its configure errors for two shaders
# with the same name and for a name that is a C++ keyword. Each case configures a small project
# (no languages, nothing is built) that calls dvv_add_shaders on a static library.
#   cmake -DSHADERS_MODULE=<DvvShaders.cmake> -DPREFIX_PATH=<prefix,...> -DWORK_DIR=<dir>
#         -DGENERATOR=<generator> -P run-shader-names-check.cmake
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

file(TO_CMAKE_PATH "${SHADERS_MODULE}" SHADERS_MODULE)
file(TO_CMAKE_PATH "${WORK_DIR}" WORK_DIR)
file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${WORK_DIR}")
file(WRITE "${WORK_DIR}/source/CMakeLists.txt" "cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)
project(shader_names LANGUAGES NONE)
string(REPLACE \",\" \";\" CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH \"\${PREFIX_PATH}\")
include(\"${SHADERS_MODULE}\")
add_library(names STATIC)
string(REPLACE \",\" \";\" shaders \"\${SHADERS}\")
dvv_add_shaders(names SHADERS \${shaders})
get_property(functions TARGET names PROPERTY DVV_SHADER_FUNCTIONS)
# Stops before generating: the project has no language to build the generated sources with.
message(FATAL_ERROR \"functions: \${functions}\")
")

# expect(<name> <shaders, comma separated> <regular expression>); configuring always fails.
function(expect name shaders pattern)
    execute_process(
        COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" -S "${WORK_DIR}/source" -B "${WORK_DIR}/build" -G "${GENERATOR}"
                "-DPREFIX_PATH=${PREFIX_PATH}" "-DSHADERS=${shaders}"
        RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE output ERROR_VARIABLE output)
    file(REMOVE_RECURSE "${WORK_DIR}/build")
    string(REGEX REPLACE "[ \t\r\n]+" " " folded "${output}")
    if(status EQUAL 0 OR NOT folded MATCHES "${pattern}")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "${name}: the output does not match '${pattern}':\n${output}")
    endif()
    message(STATUS "${name}: ok")
endfunction()

expect(camel_case "add_one.slang,blurH.slang,YCbCr.slang,two-step.v2.slang"
       "functions: addOne;blurH;yCbCr;twoStepV2")
expect(same_name "a/add_one.slang,b/add-one.slang"
       "'[^']*/b/add-one.slang' gives the same name as '[^']*/a/add_one.slang' \\(addOne\\)")
expect(keyword "new.slang" "'new.slang' does not make a C\\+\\+ function name \\('new'\\)")
```

`dvv-gpu/tests/package/main.cpp` (replace the whole file; the consumer checks the embedded shader's magic number):

```cpp
#include <cstdio>

#include "consumer_shaders/add_one.h"
#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"

int main() {
    // A shader embedded by dvv_add_shaders().
    if (consumer_shaders::addOne().spirv.empty() ||
        consumer_shaders::addOne().spirv[0] != 0x07230203U) {
        std::puts("the embedded shader is not SPIR-V");
        return 1;
    }
    // Without a Vulkan loader (CI's Windows runners) the registry reports Unsupported; anything
    // else is a failure. Validation stays off: a system's older layer may be installed.
    const auto registry =
        dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry::create({.validation = dvv::gpu::ValidationMode::Off});
    if (!registry && registry.error().code != dvv::Errc::Unsupported) {
        std::printf("%s\n", registry.error().describe().c_str());
        return 1;
    }
    std::puts(registry ? "dvv_gpu package works" : "dvv_gpu package works (no Vulkan loader)");
    return 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`dvv-gpu/include/dvv/gpu/embedded_shader.h`:

```cpp
#pragma once

// A shader compiled at build time by dvv_add_shaders() (spec §5.4, §13.2).

#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <string_view>

namespace dvv::gpu {

struct EmbeddedShader {
    std::string_view name{};                 // the .slang file name without the extension
    std::span<const std::uint32_t> spirv{};  // SPIR-V 1.6, one compute entry point named main
    std::string_view reflectionJson{};       // slangc -reflection-json output
};

}  // namespace dvv::gpu
```

`dvv-gpu/cmake/DvvShaders.cmake`:

```cmake
# dvv_add_shaders(<target> [NAMESPACE <namespace>] SHADERS <file.slang>...)
#
# Compiles Slang compute shaders at build time with the pinned slangc of the third-party prefix
# (spec §5.4, §13.2) and embeds them in <target>. For each <name>.slang the generated header
# "<namespace as a path>/<name>.h" (e.g. "my_shaders/add_one.h") declares
#
#     const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& <namespace>::<name in camelCase>() noexcept;
#
# (add_one.slang -> addOne(), blurH.slang -> blurH()) with the SPIR-V words and slangc's
# reflection JSON. The default namespace is "<target>_shaders", so two libraries never share a
# header path or a symbol. Each shader has one compute entry point named main. Two shaders of a
# target that give the same function name, or a name that is a C++ keyword, fail configuring.
# The target gets the generated sources, their include directory (build tree only) and dvv::gpu,
# PUBLIC, so a library can pass the headers on; the target must use the keyword form of
# target_link_libraries().
#
# slangc is taken only from <prefix>/slang/bin of CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH, never from PATH (an installed
# Vulkan SDK may bring another version), and its version must be the pinned one (the global
# property DVV_SLANGC_VERSION); otherwise configuring fails with a message that says what is
# wrong. slangc also writes a depfile, so a shader is rebuilt when a file it imports changes.
include_guard(GLOBAL)

# The superbuild's Slang pin (rawreel-workspace deps/versions.cmake); keep both in step. A global
# property, so it is visible wherever dvv_add_shaders() is called (in-tree this file is included
# in dvv_gpu's directory).
set_property(GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_SLANGC_VERSION "2026.20")

# Sets DVV_SLANGC (cache) to the pinned slangc, or fails.
function(dvv_find_slangc)
    get_property(required GLOBAL PROPERTY DVV_SLANGC_VERSION)
    find_program(DVV_SLANGC NAMES slangc
        PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} PATH_SUFFIXES slang/bin NO_DEFAULT_PATH)
    if(NOT DVV_SLANGC)
        message(FATAL_ERROR
            "dvv_add_shaders: slangc ${required} was not found in the third-party "
            "prefix (looked in <prefix>/slang/bin for CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH '${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}'). "
            "Build the rawreel-workspace third-party superbuild and pass its prefix in "
            "CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH.")
    endif()
    execute_process(COMMAND "${DVV_SLANGC}" -version
        RESULT_VARIABLE status OUTPUT_VARIABLE output ERROR_VARIABLE output)
    string(STRIP "${output}" version)
    if(NOT status EQUAL 0)
        message(FATAL_ERROR
            "dvv_add_shaders: ${DVV_SLANGC} -version failed (${status}): ${version}")
    endif()
    if(NOT version STREQUAL required)
        message(FATAL_ERROR
            "dvv_add_shaders: ${DVV_SLANGC} reports version '${version}', but dvv-gpu pins "
            "slangc ${required}. Rebuild the third-party prefix, or update DVV_SLANGC_VERSION in "
            "dvv-gpu's cmake/DvvShaders.cmake together with rawreel-workspace's "
            "deps/versions.cmake.")
    endif()
endfunction()

# Sets <out> to the C++ function name of a shader file stem: the parts between '-', '_' and '.'
# in camelCase (the first letter lower case, the first letter of each further part upper case).
function(_dvv_shader_function stem out)
    string(REGEX REPLACE "[-_.]+" ";" parts "${stem}")
    set(function "")
    foreach(part IN LISTS parts)
        string(SUBSTRING "${part}" 0 1 first)
        string(SUBSTRING "${part}" 1 -1 rest)
        if(function STREQUAL "")
            string(TOLOWER "${first}" first)
        else()
            string(TOUPPER "${first}" first)
        endif()
        string(APPEND function "${first}${rest}")
    endforeach()
    set(${out} "${function}" PARENT_SCOPE)
endfunction()

function(dvv_add_shaders target)
    cmake_parse_arguments(PARSE_ARGV 1 arg "" "NAMESPACE" "SHADERS")
    if(arg_UNPARSED_ARGUMENTS OR NOT arg_SHADERS)
        message(FATAL_ERROR
            "usage: dvv_add_shaders(<target> [NAMESPACE <namespace>] SHADERS <file.slang>...)")
    endif()
    if(NOT TARGET ${target})
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_shaders: ${target} is not a target")
    endif()
    if(NOT arg_NAMESPACE)
        string(MAKE_C_IDENTIFIER "${target}_shaders" arg_NAMESPACE)
    endif()
    if(NOT arg_NAMESPACE MATCHES "^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*(::[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_shaders: '${arg_NAMESPACE}' is not a C++ namespace")
    endif()
    dvv_find_slangc()

    # C++ keywords (and alternative tokens) that a shader name must not become.
    set(keywords
        alignas alignof and and_eq asm auto bitand bitor bool break case catch char char8_t char16_t
        char32_t class compl concept const consteval constexpr constinit const_cast continue co_await
        co_return co_yield decltype default delete do double dynamic_cast else enum explicit export
        extern false float for friend goto if inline int long mutable namespace new noexcept not
        not_eq nullptr operator or or_eq private protected public register reinterpret_cast requires
        return short signed sizeof static static_assert static_cast struct switch template this
        thread_local throw true try typedef typeid typename union unsigned using virtual void
        volatile wchar_t while xor xor_eq)

    string(REPLACE "::" "/" namespace_dir "${arg_NAMESPACE}")
    set(generated "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/dvv_shaders/${target}")
    set(embed_script "${CMAKE_CURRENT_FUNCTION_LIST_DIR}/DvvEmbedShader.cmake")
    # Function names and their files, also across several calls for one target.
    get_property(functions TARGET ${target} PROPERTY DVV_SHADER_FUNCTIONS)
    get_property(sources TARGET ${target} PROPERTY DVV_SHADER_SOURCES)
    foreach(shader IN LISTS arg_SHADERS)
        cmake_path(ABSOLUTE_PATH shader BASE_DIRECTORY "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}"
                   NORMALIZE OUTPUT_VARIABLE source)
        cmake_path(GET source STEM LAST_ONLY name)
        _dvv_shader_function("${name}" function)
        if(NOT function MATCHES "^[a-z][A-Za-z0-9]*$" OR function IN_LIST keywords)
            message(FATAL_ERROR "dvv_add_shaders: '${shader}' does not make a C++ function name "
                                "('${function}')")
        endif()
        list(FIND functions "${function}" duplicate)
        if(NOT duplicate EQUAL -1)
            list(GET sources ${duplicate} other)
            message(FATAL_ERROR
                "dvv_add_shaders: '${source}' gives the same name as '${other}' (${function})")
        endif()
        list(APPEND functions "${function}")
        list(APPEND sources "${source}")

        set(spirv "${generated}/${name}.spv")
        set(reflection "${generated}/${name}.json")
        set(depfile "${generated}/${name}.d")
        set(header "${generated}/${namespace_dir}/${name}.h")
        set(cpp "${generated}/${name}.cpp")
        set(stamp "${generated}/${name}.stamp")
        add_custom_command(
            OUTPUT "${spirv}" "${reflection}"
            COMMAND "${DVV_SLANGC}" "${source}" -target spirv -profile spirv_1_6 -entry main
                    -stage compute -O2 -warnings-as-errors all
                    -reflection-json "${reflection}" -depfile "${depfile}" -o "${spirv}"
            DEPENDS "${source}"
            DEPFILE "${depfile}"
            COMMENT "slangc ${name}.slang"
            VERBATIM)
        # The script rewrites the source and the header only when they change, and always touches
        # the stamp, so the rule is up to date afterwards on every generator.
        add_custom_command(
            OUTPUT "${stamp}"
            BYPRODUCTS "${cpp}" "${header}"
            COMMAND "${CMAKE_COMMAND}" "-DNAME=${name}" "-DFUNCTION=${function}"
                    "-DNAMESPACE=${arg_NAMESPACE}" "-DHEADER_INCLUDE=${namespace_dir}/${name}.h"
                    "-DSPIRV=${spirv}" "-DREFLECTION=${reflection}" "-DHEADER=${header}"
                    "-DSOURCE=${cpp}" "-DSTAMP=${stamp}" -P "${embed_script}"
            DEPENDS "${spirv}" "${reflection}" "${embed_script}"
            COMMENT "embed ${name}.slang"
            VERBATIM)
        # The arrays in every generated source have the same names.
        set_source_files_properties("${cpp}" PROPERTIES SKIP_UNITY_BUILD_INCLUSION ON)
        target_sources(${target} PRIVATE "${cpp}" "${header}" "${stamp}")
    endforeach()
    set_property(TARGET ${target} PROPERTY DVV_SHADER_FUNCTIONS "${functions}")
    set_property(TARGET ${target} PROPERTY DVV_SHADER_SOURCES "${sources}")
    target_include_directories(${target} PUBLIC "$<BUILD_INTERFACE:${generated}>")
    target_link_libraries(${target} PUBLIC dvv::gpu)
endfunction()
```

`dvv-gpu/cmake/DvvEmbedShader.cmake`:

```cmake
# Script mode, run by dvv_add_shaders (DvvShaders.cmake): embeds one compiled shader in C++.
#   cmake -DNAME=<name> -DFUNCTION=<function> -DNAMESPACE=<namespace> -DHEADER_INCLUDE=<path.h>
#         -DSPIRV=<file.spv> -DREFLECTION=<file.json> -DHEADER=<file.h> -DSOURCE=<file.cpp>
#         -DSTAMP=<file.stamp> -P DvvEmbedShader.cmake
# The header declares `const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& <namespace>::<function>() noexcept;`; the
# source defines it with the SPIR-V words (little-endian in the file) and the reflection JSON as a
# character array (string literals have length limits on MSVC). Files are rewritten only when their
# content changes, so dependants do not rebuild needlessly; the stamp is always touched, so the
# rule that runs this script is up to date afterwards.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 4.2)

foreach(variable IN ITEMS NAME FUNCTION NAMESPACE HEADER_INCLUDE SPIRV REFLECTION HEADER SOURCE
                         STAMP)
    if(NOT DEFINED ${variable} OR "${${variable}}" STREQUAL "")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "DvvEmbedShader.cmake needs -D${variable}=<value>")
    endif()
endforeach()

file(SIZE "${SPIRV}" spirv_size)
math(EXPR remainder "${spirv_size} % 4")
if(spirv_size EQUAL 0 OR NOT remainder EQUAL 0)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "${SPIRV} has ${spirv_size} bytes, which is not a SPIR-V binary")
endif()
file(READ "${SPIRV}" spirv_hex HEX)
if(NOT spirv_hex MATCHES "^03022307")  # the magic number 0x07230203, little-endian
    message(FATAL_ERROR "${SPIRV} does not start with the SPIR-V magic number")
endif()
# Each group of 4 bytes becomes one 0xAABBCCDD word, 8 words per line (CMake regular expressions
# have no {n} repetition, hence string(REPEAT)).
string(REGEX REPLACE "(..)(..)(..)(..)" "0x\\4\\3\\2\\1U, " words "${spirv_hex}")
string(REPEAT "0x[0-9a-f]+U, " 8 eight_words)
string(REGEX REPLACE "(${eight_words})" "\\1\n    " words "${words}")
string(REPLACE " \n" "\n" words "${words}")
string(STRIP "${words}" words)
math(EXPR word_count "${spirv_size} / 4")

# Each byte of the JSON text becomes one '\xNN' character, 16 per line.
file(SIZE "${REFLECTION}" json_size)
if(json_size EQUAL 0)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "${REFLECTION} is empty")
endif()
file(READ "${REFLECTION}" json_hex HEX)
string(REGEX REPLACE "(..)" "'\\\\x\\1', " characters "${json_hex}")
string(REPEAT "'.x[0-9a-f]+', " 16 sixteen_characters)
string(REGEX REPLACE "(${sixteen_characters})" "\\1\n    " characters "${characters}")
string(REPLACE " \n" "\n" characters "${characters}")
string(STRIP "${characters}" characters)

set(header_content "// Generated by dvv_add_shaders from ${NAME}.slang. Do not edit.
#pragma once

#include \"dvv/gpu/embedded_shader.h\"

namespace ${NAMESPACE} {

[[nodiscard]] const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& ${FUNCTION}() noexcept;

}  // namespace ${NAMESPACE}
")

set(source_content "// Generated by dvv_add_shaders from ${NAME}.slang. Do not edit.
#include \"${HEADER_INCLUDE}\"

#include <cstdint>

namespace ${NAMESPACE} {

namespace {

constexpr std::uint32_t SPIRV[${word_count}] = {
    ${words}
};

constexpr char REFLECTION_JSON[${json_size}] = {
    ${characters}
};

}  // namespace

const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& ${FUNCTION}() noexcept {
    static constexpr dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader SHADER{
        .name = \"${NAME}\",
        .spirv = SPIRV,
        .reflectionJson = {REFLECTION_JSON, ${json_size}},
    };
    return SHADER;
}

}  // namespace ${NAMESPACE}
")

function(write_if_changed path content)
    if(EXISTS "${path}")
        file(READ "${path}" old)
        if(old STREQUAL content)
            return()
        endif()
    endif()
    file(WRITE "${path}" "${content}")
endfunction()

write_if_changed("${HEADER}" "${header_content}")
write_if_changed("${SOURCE}" "${source_content}")
file(TOUCH "${STAMP}")
```

In `dvv-gpu/README.md` replace

```markdown
Licence: MIT.
```

with

````markdown
## Shaders

Compute shaders are Slang files compiled at build time by the pinned `slangc` of the third-party
prefix (never one from `PATH`) and embedded in the program:

```cmake
find_package(dvv_gpu 0.1 REQUIRED)   # or dvv_gpu in-tree
dvv_add_shaders(my_target NAMESPACE my_shaders SHADERS shaders/add_one.slang)
```

`#include "my_shaders/add_one.h"` then gives `const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader& my_shaders::addOne()`:
the SPIR-V words and slangc's reflection JSON. The header path follows the namespace, which
defaults to `<target>_shaders`. Each shader has one compute entry point `main`. Configuring fails
with a clear message when `slangc` is missing from `CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH`, cannot run or is not the
pinned version, and when two shaders of a target give the same function name.

Limits: the target must use the keyword form of `target_link_libraries()` (dvv_add_shaders links
`dvv::gpu` PUBLIC), and the generated headers exist in the build tree only; they are not
installed with the target.

Licence: MIT.
````

```powershell
git -C dvv-gpu add -A
@'
feat: add dvv_add_shaders for build-time Slang compute shaders

dvv_add_shaders(<target> [NAMESPACE <ns>, default <target>_shaders] SHADERS <file.slang>...)
(cmake/DvvShaders.cmake, included in-tree and installed with the package) compiles each shader with
the pinned slangc of the third-party prefix: found only in <prefix>/slang/bin of CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH
(never on PATH) and checked against DVV_SLANGC_VERSION (2026.20); a missing or wrong slangc fails
configuring with a message that says what to do. Flags: -target spirv -profile spirv_1_6 -entry main
-stage compute -O2 -warnings-as-errors all, plus -reflection-json and a depfile for imports.

DvvEmbedShader.cmake turns the SPIR-V (checked for size and magic number) and the reflection JSON
into a generated header <ns as path>/<name>.h and source: const dvv::gpu::EmbeddedShader&
<ns>::<camelCaseName>() with {name, spirv words, reflectionJson} (the JSON as a character array,
free of MSVC's string literal limits). Files are rewritten only when their content changes; a stamp
keeps the rule up to date on every generator. Function names are checked: a C++ keyword or two
shaders with the same name fail configuring; generated sources are kept out of unity builds. The
target gets the sources, the include directory and dvv::gpu (PUBLIC).

Tests: the add_one test shader (buffer device address push constants) in a small library shared by
the unit and GPU tests; unit tests check the SPIR-V header and parse the reflection JSON with
dvv::json; script tests check the configure errors for a missing slangc (also with one on PATH), a
wrong slangc, and invalid or duplicate shader names; the package consumers (installed and in-tree)
call dvv_add_shaders.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
'@ | git -C dvv-gpu commit -F -
```
